## Who and how we talk
1. NastyPlot is Sim's; Sim is **he/him**. Sim and HH are partners: Sim brings ideas and decides; HH researches, builds, checks.
2. Sim's questions come first; none goes unanswered. Each answer to a question or decision opens with "What I'd need to know that
   you haven't told me" (or "Nothing missing.").
3. Every message starts WORKING or STOPPED and ends "— HH". Short sentences, short lines, nothing left out.
4. "Status report" = NOW / JUST DONE / NEXT, short.
5. Every recommendation has a one-line reason. Say when you disagree or are unsure. No yes-man.
6. Only Sim's own words stop work. Never delete a project without Sim's confirmation.

## Honesty (most important)
7. No guessing: read or run it first. Every claim is labelled checked (say how) or not checked. "I'm unsure" when unsure.
8. Every check reports the command, the exact result line, and what it means in plain words.
9. Never say something is done or running unless it is. Report a wrong statement the moment it's found.
10. Times only from the clock: `bash docs/rebuild/now.sh`; decisions with `bash docs/rebuild/log-decision.sh "..."`.

## Design
11. **NO GENERIC AI DESIGN (Sim's MAJOR rule).** Build from NastyPlot's identity: the brand kit (docs/brand/nastyplot-brand-assets/,
    never redraw a logo), Unbounded slanted for headlines and big numbers, Instrument Sans for body, PLOT blue and Nasty pink,
    slanted boxy shapes, receipts, plot bars. Ask "could this be any other site?" If yes, it isn't ready. One creative idea per page,
    cohesion across pages.
12. Everything on the shared kit (kit.js, tokens, parts). Type 13/15/16/17/20/22/24/26/28/34 + display; 4px spacing grid;
    corners 4/6/10/18/round; line height 1.5.
13. Use space well at every size; no empty strips, lone tiles, half-filled rows, or boxes in boxes; light and dark both clean.
14. Keep the WOW: when a fix could go plainer or stay bold, stay bold.
15. Short pages: main thing on the first screen at 1440x900; caps 3 screens (tools/lists), 4 (Home, Pokémon page, This Week, Meta);
    phones short with natural scrolling, no hiding content in folds. Every window size must look right, odd widths too.
16. Type symbols only with a label. Search reaches everything (no feature is done until Search finds it). Outside designs are input;
    our rules win.
17. Visual decisions need Sim's yes with pictures, unless he has given design authority (then rule in "Right now"). The recommended
    picture's label band is green (label-picture.cjs).

## Data, the score and the words
18. Real data only; never remove functionality. Live is protected: data reaches live only after GitHub's tests pass.

===== FILE: regstate.mjs (claude/design6)
// Thin-data counts for the active regulation (Phase 0.8, Nov 24): writes out/regstate.json
// ({reg, day, events, players, games, since, updated}) so pages can show "Day N of Reg M-D: X events so far".
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {pathToFileURL} from 'node:url';
import {activeReg, readReg} from './formats.mjs';

const rd = f => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return null; } };

export function buildRegState({regObj, rawReg, matches, today} = {}) {
  const RG = regObj || activeReg();
  const R = rawReg !== undefined ? rawReg : readReg();
  const d = today || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const since = (RG && RG.since) || d;
  const t0 = Date.parse(since + 'T12:00:00Z'), t1 = Date.parse(d + 'T12:00:00Z');
  const day = isFinite(t0) && isFinite(t1) ? Math.max(1, Math.floor((t1 - t0) / 864e5) + 1) : 1;
  const mOk = matches && (!matches.reg || matches.reg === RG.id);
  const seen = R && R.seen && R.seen[RG.id];
  const events = mOk && Array.isArray(matches.events) ? matches.events.length : (seen ? +seen.events || 0 : 0);
  const players = mOk && typeof matches.players === 'number' ? matches.players : (seen ? +seen.players || 0 : 0);
  const games = mOk && Array.isArray(matches.m) ? matches.m.length : 0;
  return {reg: RG.id, day, events, players, games, since, updated: new Date().toISOString()};
}

if (import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1] || '').href) {
  const out = buildRegState({matches: rd('out/matches.json')});
  fs.writeFileSync('out/regstate.json', JSON.stringify(out));
  console.log(`regstate: ${out.reg} day ${out.day} (since ${out.since}), ${out.events} events, ${out.players} players, ${out.games} games`);
}

===== FILE: reg.mjs (claude/design6)
// Regulation check (first step of the daily job, Nov 1): which regulation the newest tournaments and the ladder are played
// under, so no step ever mixes two regulations (formats.mjs has the rules). Reads:
//   - Limitless: the newest VGC tournaments of every format (3 list pages, well under its 50-requests-per-5-minutes limit)
//   - Showdown: replay searches for the next regulation's format id (e.g. gen9championsvgc2026regmd, this year and next)
// Writes out/reg.json; perf.mjs copies it into out/perf.json as "reg", so the page gets it with meta/perf.
// If both sources fail, yesterday's file stays (a ::warning:: in the log). REG_OFFLINE=1 builds it from the saved tournaments
// in data/index.json only (no network; they are all one regulation, so it can't see a new one) for local previews.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {detectReg, readReg, activeReg, nextRegIds, sdGuess, regName, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const UA = {'User-Agent': 'pokelair-data (personal team-builder stats)'}, sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
async function getJSON(url) { const c = new AbortController(), to = setTimeout(() => c.abort(), 30000);
  try { const r = await fetch(url, {headers: UA, signal: c.signal}); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(url + ' ' + r.status); return await r.json(); } finally { clearTimeout(to); } }
const prev = readReg(), act = activeReg(), today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
let list = null; const sd = {};
if (process.env.REG_OFFLINE === '1') {
  let idx = {tournaments: {}}; try { idx = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('data/index.json', 'utf8')); } catch (e) {}
  list = Object.values(idx.tournaments || {}).map(t => ({format: t.f || SITE_REG.id, date: t.d, players: t.p}));
} else {
  try { list = []; for (let page = 1; page <= 3; page++) { if (page > 1) await sleep(6500);
      const r = await getJSON(`https://play.limitlesstcg.com/api/tournaments?game=VGC&limit=100&page=${page}`); if (!Array.isArray(r) || !r.length) break; list.push(...r); }
    if (!list.some(t => t && t.format)) { console.log('::warning::reg: Limitless tournaments came back without formats'); list = null; } }
  catch (e) { console.log('::warning::reg: Limitless list failed (' + e.message + ')'); list = null; }
  // the next regulation on Showdown: one search page per candidate id (51 replays per page at most)
  const y = +today.slice(0, 4);
  for (const id of nextRegIds(act.id)) for (const yr of [y, y + 1]) { const f = sdGuess(id, yr);
    try { await sleep(400); const r = await getJSON(`https://replay.pokemonshowdown.com/search.json?format=${f}`);
      if (Array.isArray(r) && r.length) { const t = Math.min(...r.map(x => +x.uploadtime || Infinity)); sd[f] = {n: r.length, first: isFinite(t) ? new Date(t * 1000).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : null}; } }
    catch (e) { console.log(`reg: Showdown search for ${f} failed (${e.message})`); } }
}
if (!list && !Object.keys(sd).length) { console.log('::warning::reg: no source answered; kept yesterday\'s out/reg.json'); process.exit(0); }
const r = detectReg(list || [], sd, prev, today);
// a source that failed today doesn't erase what it showed yesterday
if (!list && prev) { if (prev.seen) r.seen = prev.seen; if (!r.now && prev.now && prev.now.from === 'limitless') r.now = prev.now; } /* keeps yesterday's banner too (code review, Nov 24) */
if (r.now) console.log(`::warning::reg: ${r.now.name} is being played since ${r.now.since} (${r.now.events} events, ${r.now.players} players${r.now.ladder ? ', ' + r.now.ladder + '+ Showdown replays' : ''}); numbers stay on ${r.name} until it has ${r.now.need.events} events and ${r.now.need.players} players`);
if (r.switched && r.switched.on === today) console.log(`::warning::reg: switched from ${regName(r.switched.from)} to ${r.name} (started ${r.since}); every step now counts ${r.name} events only`);
fs.writeFileSync('out/reg.json', JSON.stringify(r));
console.log(`reg: ${r.name} since ${r.since}${r.now ? `, newer ${r.now.name} since ${r.now.since}` : ''} · seen ${Object.entries(r.seen).map(([k, s]) => `${k} ${s.events} events (${s.week} this week)`).join(', ') || 'nothing'}`);

===== FILE: reg-guard.mjs (claude/design6)
// Switch-day regulation guard (Nov 24): checks every out/*.json file against activeReg().id.
// Replaces any file stamped with a different regulation (or unstamped after a switch) with its empty state.
// Never touches data/ (raw saved events).
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {activeReg, readReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';

export const EXEMPT = new Set(['reg.json', 'calendar.json', 'skips.json', 'official_perf.json',
  /* every regulation's history in one file (job 8 byReg): emptying them would lose M-C's history for good (Scout's review, Nov 24) */
  'ratings_log.json', 'scoreboard.json']);

export function emptyFor(file, reg, sd = '', now = new Date().toISOString()) {
  const day = now.slice(0, 10);
  switch (file) {
    case 'limitless.json': return {reg, updated: now, tournaments: 0, teams: 0, games: 0, base: 0.5, adj: {label: '', rows: []}, tour: {label: '', split: true, updated: day, rows: [], megas: []}, results: {source: '', teams: 0, base: 0.5, minCore: 40, mons: {}, pairs: {}, cores: [], proven: [], records: []}};
    case 'meta_tour.json': return {reg, format: 'doubles', kind: 'tour', source: '', period: '', updated: now, note: '', top: [], more: []};
    case 'info.json': return {reg, N: 0, source: '', period: '', mv: {}, it: {}, ab: {}, ntN: 0, nt: {}};
    case 'teams.json': return {reg, source: '', updated: now, teams: []};
    case 'history.json': return {reg, days: []};
    case 'matches.json': return {reg, updated: now, events: [], teams: [], players: 0, feats: [], decks: [], m: []};
    case 'replays.json': return {reg, updated: now, format: sd, source: '', games: []};
    case 'replays_bo3.json': return {reg, updated: now, format: sd ? sd + 'bo3' : '', source: '', games: []};
    case 'practice.json': return {reg, updated: now, source: '', from: null, to: null, minR: 1300, roundR: 1450, dict: [], opp: [], rounds: []};
    case 'official.json': return {reg, updated: now, source: '', attribution: '', events: [], checks: []};
    case 'official_compare.json': return {reg, previous: 'none', events: [], pass: true, summary: 'regulation switched'};
    case 'events.json': return {reg, updated: now, source: '', dict: [], events: []};
    case 'weekly.json': return {reg, v: 1, updated: now, src: '', th: {}, a: {from: '', to: '', ev: 0, n: 0}, b: {from: '', to: '', ev: 0, n: 0}, enough: false, cutA: 0, up: [], down: [], top: [], tech: [], sty: {up: [], down: []}, styAll: [], fresh: []};
    case 'model.json': return {reg, updated: now, ships: false, games: 0, events: 0, mons: {}, pairs: {}, sets: {}, team: {}, sixes: {}, leads: {}};
    case 'meta_ladder.json': return {reg, format: 'doubles', kind: 'ladder', month: null, source: '', period: '', battles: 0, updated: now, note: '', top: [], more: [], usage1760: null};
    case 'meta_ingame.json': return {reg, format: 'doubles', kind: 'ingame', source: '', period: '', updated: now, top: [], more: []};
    case 'sources.json': return {reg, updated: now, problems: []};
    case 'perf.json': return {updated: day, reg: (() => { try { const r = activeReg(); return {id: reg, name: r.name, since: r.since}; } catch (e) { return {id: reg}; } })()}; /* the banner keeps its name and start date (Scout's review) */
    case 'crosscheck.json': return {reg, updated: now, checks: []};
    case 'ratings_log.json': return {reg, updated: now, days: []};
    case 'scoreboard.json': return {reg, updated: now, today: null, history: []};
    case 'track.json': return {reg, v: 1, updated: now, since: null, snaps: 0, last: null, top: 8, ev: [], mons: [], mb: [[0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0]], fw: [], mv: null};
    default: return null;
  }
}

export function stampOf(doc) {
  if (!doc || typeof doc !== 'object') return null;
  if (typeof doc.reg === 'string' && doc.reg) return doc.reg;
  if (doc.reg && typeof doc.reg === 'object' && typeof doc.reg.id === 'string' && doc.reg.id) return doc.reg.id;
  return null;
}

export function guardOut({outDir = 'out', regFile = path.join(outDir, 'reg.json'), log = console.log} = {}) {
  const RG = activeReg(regFile), rawReg = readReg(regFile);
  const switched = !!(rawReg && rawReg.switched) || RG.id !== SITE_REG.id; /* not a hard-coded 'M-C' (Scout's review) */
  const lines = [], emptied = [], unknown = [];
  if (!fs.existsSync(outDir)) return {reg: RG.id, lines, emptied, unknown};
  const files = fs.readdirSync(outDir).filter(f => f.endsWith('.json')).sort();
  for (const f of files) {
    if (EXEMPT.has(f)) { const ln = `ok ${f}`; lines.push(ln); log(ln); continue; }
    const empty = emptyFor(f, RG.id, RG.sd);
    if (!empty) { const ln = `unknown ${f}`; lines.push(ln); unknown.push(f); log(ln); continue; }
    const fp = path.join(outDir, f);
    let doc = null; try { doc = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(fp, 'utf8')); } catch (e) {}
    const st = stampOf(doc);
    const bad = st ? st !== RG.id : switched;
    if (bad) {
      /* never lose data: the file is copied to out/archive/<its regulation>/ first (kept once; the page never reads archive/) */
      try { const ad = path.join(outDir, 'archive', st || (rawReg && rawReg.switched && rawReg.switched.from) || 'old'), af = path.join(ad, f); if (!fs.existsSync(af)) { fs.mkdirSync(ad, {recursive: true}); fs.copyFileSync(fp, af); } } catch (e) {}
      try { fs.writeFileSync(fp, JSON.stringify(empty)); } catch (e) { const ln = `could not empty ${f} (${e.message})`; lines.push(ln); log(ln); continue; } /* never stops the run */
      const ln = `emptied ${f} (was ${st || 'unstamped'})`;
      lines.push(ln); emptied.push(f); log(ln);
    } else {
      const ln = `ok ${f}`; lines.push(ln); log(ln);
    }
  }
  return {reg: RG.id, lines, emptied, unknown};
}

if (process.argv[1] && fileURLToPath(import.meta.url) === path.resolve(process.argv[1])) {
  try { guardOut(); } catch (e) { console.log(`::warning::reg-guard: ${e.message}`); } /* the guard must never stop the daily job (Scout's review) */
}

===== FILE: reg-stamp.mjs (claude/design6)
// Regulation stamp helper (Nov 24): adds {reg: "<id>"} to any out/ JSON object without altering existing fields.
import {activeReg} from './formats.mjs';
export const stampReg = (doc, id = activeReg().id) =>
  doc && typeof doc === 'object' && !Array.isArray(doc) ? {...doc, reg: id} : doc;

===== FILE: md-watch.mjs (claude/design6)
// Reg M-D watcher and Limitless alarm (Nov 24; game plan "watcher"). Read-only: it never changes the site or the data. Run daily by
// .github/workflows/md-watch.yml (and on demand); the workflow opens or closes the GitHub issues from the report this writes.
//   1. Reg M-D spotted: looks in three places, a few requests a day in all:
//      - Showdown: the raw config/formats.ts of smogon/pokemon-showdown (default branch) for a Champions format of the next regulation
//        ("Reg M-D" in a format name, or its id gen9championsvgc<year>regmd);
//      - Limitless: ONE page of the newest VGC tournaments (the API reg.mjs already uses; its limit is 50 requests per 5 minutes) for a
//        tournament whose format is the next regulation;
//      - the official news list (the in-game news feed post-news.mjs reads) for a title naming the next regulation.
//      Each place found gets its own issue "Reg M-D spotted: <where>", opened once (the workflow skips a title that exists, open or closed).
//   2. Limitless alarm: "Limitless data paused since <date>" when Limitless doesn't answer, or the newest saved tournament
//      (data/index.json) is 2+ days old; the workflow closes it when data flows again.
// The next regulation is worked out, not typed: formats.mjs nextRegIds(the active one) -> "M-D" today.
// Writes WATCH_REPORT (JSON, default watch-report.json): {next, spotted: [{where, title, body}], paused: null | {title, body}, notes}.
// Dry runs (no network for the faked part, nothing opened; WATCH_DRY=1 also prints each issue it WOULD open or close):
//   WATCH_FAKE_FOUND=showdown,limitless,news   pretend each place shows the next regulation
//   WATCH_FAKE_LIMITLESS=down | stale           pretend Limitless doesn't answer | its newest event is 5 days old
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {activeReg, nextRegIds, sdGuess, regName} from './formats.mjs';
const FEED = 'https://champions-news.pokemon-home.com/en/json/list.json'; // the same feed as post-news.mjs FEED (main has no post-news.mjs; Nov 24)
const UA = {'User-Agent': 'pokelair-data (personal team-builder stats)'}, out = process.env.WATCH_REPORT || 'watch-report.json';
const fake = new Set(String(process.env.WATCH_FAKE_FOUND || '').split(',').filter(Boolean)), fakeL = process.env.WATCH_FAKE_LIMITLESS || '';
async function get(url, json = true) { const c = new AbortController(), to = setTimeout(() => c.abort(), 30000);
  try { const r = await fetch(url, {headers: UA, signal: c.signal}); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${url} ${r.status}`); return json ? await r.json() : await r.text(); } finally { clearTimeout(to); } }
const act = activeReg(), next = nextRegIds(act.id)[0], today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), y = +today.slice(0, 4);
const R = {next: next || null, checked: today, spotted: [], paused: null, notes: []};
if (!next) { R.notes.push(`no next regulation after ${act.id}`); fs.writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(R, null, 1)); process.exit(0); }
const nextName = regName(next), ids = [y, y + 1].map(yr => sdGuess(next, yr)), letter = next.replace('-', ''); // "MD"
const nameRe = new RegExp(`Reg(ulation)?(?:\\s+Set)?\\s*${next.replace('-', '\\s*-?\\s*')}\\b`, 'i');             // Reg M-D, Regulation (Set) M-D: pokemon.com writes "Regulation Set M-C" (code review, Nov 24)
const spot = (where, detail, link) => R.spotted.push({where, title: `${nextName} spotted: ${where}`,
  body: `${detail}\n\n${link ? `Where: ${link}\n\n` : ''}Found by md-watch.mjs on ${today} (it only reads; nothing on the site or in the data changed). ` +
    `Next: docs/reg-md-plan.md Phase 1 (roster generator experiments/gen-roster.mjs, sprites experiments/add-sprites.mjs).`});
// 1a. Showdown's formats file (one request)
if (fake.has('showdown')) spot('Showdown', `FAKE (dry run): Showdown's formats list has "[Gen 9 Champions] VGC 2026 ${nextName}".`, 'https://github.com/smogon/pokemon-showdown/blob/master/config/formats.ts');
else try { const t = await get('https://raw.githubusercontent.com/smogon/pokemon-showdown/master/config/formats.ts', false);
  const lines = t.split('\n').filter(l => /name:\s*["']\[Gen 9 Champions\]/i.test(l) && (nameRe.test(l) || ids.some(i => l.toLowerCase().includes(i))));
  if (lines.length) spot('Showdown', `Showdown's formats list now has: ${lines.map(l => l.trim()).join(' | ')}`, 'https://github.com/smogon/pokemon-showdown/blob/master/config/formats.ts');
  else R.notes.push(`Showdown formats.ts: no ${nextName} format`); } catch (e) { R.notes.push(`Showdown formats.ts not read (${e.message})`); }
// 1b + 2. Limitless: one page of the newest tournaments (one request)
let list = null;
if (fakeL === 'down') R.notes.push('FAKE (dry run): Limitless did not answer');
else try { const L = 'https://play.limitlesstcg.com/api/tournaments?game=VGC&limit=100&page=1'; /* one retry after 10 s, so a single blip is not an alarm (code review, Nov 24) */
  try { list = await get(L); } catch (e) { R.notes.push(`Limitless first try failed (${e.message}); retrying once`); await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10000)); list = await get(L); } if (!Array.isArray(list)) throw new Error('not a list'); }
  catch (e) { R.notes.push(`Limitless not reached (${e.message})`); list = null; }
if (fake.has('limitless')) spot('Limitless', `FAKE (dry run): 3 Limitless VGC tournaments are marked ${next}.`, 'https://play.limitlesstcg.com/tournaments?game=VGC');
else if (list) { const md = list.filter(t => String(t && t.format || '').toUpperCase() === next);
  if (md.length) spot('Limitless', `${md.length} Limitless VGC tournament${md.length === 1 ? ' is' : 's are'} marked ${next}: ${md.slice(0, 5).map(t => `${t.name} (${String(t.date).slice(0, 10)}, ${t.players} players)`).join('; ')}`, 'https://play.limitlesstcg.com/tournaments?game=VGC');
  else R.notes.push(`Limitless: none of the newest ${list.length} tournaments is marked ${next}`); }
// 1c. the official news lists (two requests: the Champions in-game news feed post-news.mjs reads, and news.pokemon-home.com, where
//     the Dec 2 patch notes were posted); a post whose TITLE names the next regulation
const NEWS = [FEED, 'https://news.pokemon-home.com/en/json/list.json'];
if (fake.has('news')) spot('official news', `FAKE (dry run): the official news list has "Regulation ${next} begins …".`, 'https://news.pokemon-home.com/');
else { const hit = []; let n = 0;
  for (const u of NEWS) try { const f = await get(u), items = Array.isArray(f) ? f : (f && (f.data || f.list || f.items)) || []; n += items.length;
      for (const i of items) if (nameRe.test(String(i && i.title || ''))) hit.push({t: i.title, link: i.link || u}); }
    catch (e) { R.notes.push(`official news ${u} not read (${e.message})`); }
  if (hit.length) spot('official news', `The official news names ${nextName}: ${hit.slice(0, 3).map(h => `"${h.t}"`).join(' | ')}`, hit[0].link);
  else R.notes.push(`official news: ${n} posts in ${NEWS.length} lists, no title names ${nextName}`); }
// 2. Limitless alarm: no answer, or the newest saved tournament is 2+ days old
/* data/index.json is written by the daily job on main (the branch scheduled workflows run on); WATCH_INDEX reads another copy (tests) */
const IDX = process.env.WATCH_INDEX || 'data/index.json';
let newest = null; try { newest = Object.values(JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(IDX, 'utf8')).tournaments || {}).map(t => String(t.d || '').slice(0, 10)).filter(d => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(d)).sort().pop() || null; } catch (e) {}
if (fakeL === 'stale') newest = new Date(Date.now() - 5 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const age = newest ? Math.floor((Date.parse(today) - Date.parse(newest)) / 864e5) : null, down = fakeL === 'down' || !list;
if (down || age === null || age >= 2) { const since = newest || 'unknown';
  R.paused = {title: `Limitless data paused since ${since}`, body: `${down ? 'Limitless did not answer today (two tries, 10 s apart, to its tournaments API).' : age === null ? `No saved Limitless tournaments found in ${IDX}.` : `No new Limitless event since ${since} (${age} days; the alarm starts at 2).`}` +
    `\n\nNewest saved tournament: ${since} (${IDX}). Checked ${today} by md-watch.mjs; this issue closes by itself when data flows again.` +
    `\nBackup plan: docs/rebuild/gameplan-detail-nov25.md (Limitless backup).`}; }
else R.notes.push(`Limitless: newest saved tournament ${newest} (${age} day${age === 1 ? '' : 's'} old)`);
fs.writeFileSync(out, JSON.stringify(R, null, 1));
console.log(`md-watch: next ${next}; spotted ${R.spotted.map(s => s.where).join(', ') || 'nothing'}; Limitless ${R.paused ? 'PAUSED' : 'ok'}`); R.notes.forEach(n => console.log('  ' + n));
if (process.env.WATCH_DRY === '1') { for (const s of R.spotted) console.log(`\n--- WOULD OPEN (once; skipped if an issue with this title exists): ${s.title}\n${s.body}`);
  console.log(R.paused ? `\n--- WOULD OPEN OR UPDATE: ${R.paused.title}\n${R.paused.body}` : '\n--- WOULD CLOSE any open "Limitless data paused since …" issue (data flows)'); }

===== FILE: formats.mjs (claude/design6)
import fs from 'node:fs';
// Events played under extra rules (themed or restricted cups) aren't standard Reg M-C: their teams would skew usage, win rates
// and the team model (sweep, Oct 31: Spooky Cup had Incineroar at 0%, Monotype events are 72–100% single-type teams).
// Nov 8 data audit: also Monoregion/Monocolor cups (a MonoRegion event put Volcarona + Vanilluxe teams in a top-4 list).
// Every pipeline step skips them by name. The raw files stay in data/ so nothing is lost if a name is matched by mistake.
// Nov 18 data audit (docs/rebuild/research/data-integrity-nov17.md, finding 3): also UU cups, old-generation cups ("Gen 3 Edition",
// "Gen 2 Showdown"; Gen 1-8 only, so a "Gen 9"/"Gen 10" name never matches), Single Type cups, "POKÉMONAS" and "Só Evos" (themed
// rounds of one series). Checked against every event name on Nov 18: these words match 8 events and no standard one.
export const OFF_FORMAT = /off[ -]?meta|spooky|mono ?(?:type|region|color|colour)|small pok[eé]mon|ultra beast|draft|\bbst\b|-\s?1\s?%|under \d+\s?%|little cup|\bnfe\b|\buu\b|\bgen(?:eration)? ?[1-8]\b|single[ -]?type|pok[eé]monas|s[oó] evos/i;
export const offFormat = name => OFF_FORMAT.test(String(name || ''));

// The alarm for themed events whose names the filter above doesn't catch (Oct 31). Staples (Pokémon on 20%+ of all standard
// teams) always show up in a normal event: one expected on 8+ of an event's teams but on none of them is a chance of about 1 in
// 3,000, so the event is almost certainly played under extra rules. It's left out automatically and named in the daily job's
// log as a ::warning::. Tested on Oct 31 data: it flags Mudkip's Marsh Pit #7 (no Kingambit or Rillaboom on 43 teams) and
// Rising Stars S4 (no Rillaboom on 27), and no normal event. readT(id) returns the event's data/t file; "Raichu|MY" counts as Raichu.
// A second alarm for small events is at the end (Nov 18).
export function oddEvents(ids, readT, log = console.log) {
  const base = k => String(k).split('|')[0], ev = [], tot = new Map(); let n = 0;
  for (const id of ids) { const T = readT(id); if (!T || !Array.isArray(T.e)) continue; const c = new Map(); let t = 0;
    for (const [, , , ks] of T.e) { const s = [...new Set((ks || []).map(base))]; if (s.length !== 6) continue; t++; for (const x of s) c.set(x, (c.get(x) || 0) + 1); }
    ev.push({id, t, c}); n += t; for (const [x, k] of c) tot.set(x, (tot.get(x) || 0) + k); }
  const staples = [...tot].map(([x, k]) => [x, n ? k / n : 0]).filter(([, sh]) => sh >= .2), out = new Set();
  for (const e of ev) { if (e.t < 20) continue; const gone = staples.filter(([x, sh]) => sh * e.t >= 8 && !e.c.get(x)).map(([x]) => x);
    if (gone.length) { out.add(e.id); log(`::warning::left out event ${e.id}: no ${gone.join(', ')} on any of its ${e.t} teams, so it looks like a restricted format`); } }
  // Second alarm (Nov 18 data audit, finding 3): the rule above needs 20+ teams and a staple missing outright, so small themed cups
  // (UU, Gen 2/3, Halloween specials: 7-21 teams) slipped through. Here the 10 most-used Pokémon of all standard teams are
  // counted on an event's teams; a normal event shows about as many as their field-wide usage predicts (ratio near 1), an
  // event under half of that (with 8+ full teams) is left out. Measured Nov 18: a random 8 teams of one normal event fall under
  // 0.5 about 0.4% of the time (0.05% at 15 teams); the flagged events sit at 0.00-0.47, the next event at 0.55.
  const top10 = [...tot].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 10).map(([x, k]) => [x, n ? k / n : 0]), sum10 = top10.reduce((a, [, sh]) => a + sh, 0);
  for (const e of ev) { if (e.t < 8 || out.has(e.id) || !sum10) continue; const r = top10.reduce((a, [x]) => a + (e.c.get(x) || 0), 0) / (sum10 * e.t);
    if (r < .5) { out.add(e.id); log(`::warning::left out event ${e.id}: the 10 most-used Pokémon appear on its ${e.t} teams ${Math.round(100 * r)}% as often as usual, so it looks like a restricted format`); } }
  return out;
}

// ---------- regulations (Nov 1): never mix two regulations in the same numbers ----------
// The site is built for SITE_REG. reg.mjs (first step of the daily job) looks at the newest Limitless VGC tournaments of every
// format and at Showdown replays of the next regulation's format, and writes out/reg.json:
//   {id, name, since, sd}  the regulation every number is built from ("active"); all steps read it through activeReg()
//   now                    a newer regulation being played that doesn't have enough data yet (the page shows a calm banner)
//   seen                   per regulation: events, players, first and last day, events in the newest week
// While a newer one is short of REG_SWITCH, everything stays on the active regulation and nothing from the new one is counted
// (Limitless is fetched by format, Showdown by format id, RK9 events by date window). Once it has enough, the active
// regulation switches to it and every step starts over from its own events only. REG_AUTO_SWITCH=0 holds the switch
// (banner only) for a manual check.
export const SITE_REG = {id: 'M-C', sd: 'gen9championsvgc2026regmc', since: '2026-09-01'};
export const REG_SWITCH = {events: 20, players: 1200};
export const REG_AUTO_SWITCH = (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.env && process.env.REG_AUTO_SWITCH) !== '0';
export const regName = id => 'Reg ' + id;
const REG_ID = /^[A-Z]-[A-Z]$/;
// Showdown's id for a regulation: Reg M-C is gen9championsvgc2026regmc; the year part of a later one isn't known in advance
export const sdGuess = (id, year) => `gen9championsvgc${year}reg${String(id).replace('-', '').toLowerCase()}`;
export const sdReg = fmt => { const m = String(fmt || '').match(/^gen9championsvgc\d{4}reg([a-z])([a-z])$/); return m ? `${m[1].toUpperCase()}-${m[2].toUpperCase()}` : null; };
export const nextRegIds = id => { const [a, b] = String(id).split('-'); return b && b < 'Z' ? [`${a}-${String.fromCharCode(b.charCodeAt(0) + 1)}`] : []; };
export function readReg(file = 'out/reg.json') { try { const r = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8')); return r && REG_ID.test(r.id) ? r : null; } catch (e) { return null; } }
// the regulation the numbers are built from, and the window its events fall in (end = the day a newer one started, if any)
// the first day (UTC) on which a regulation no longer counts, for official events' date windows (inRegWindow)
const endDay = id => REG_END[id] ? REG_END[id].slice(0, 10) : null;
export function activeReg(file) { const r = readReg(file);
  if (!r) return {...SITE_REG, name: regName(SITE_REG.id), end: endDay(SITE_REG.id)};
  return {id: r.id, name: regName(r.id), sd: r.sd || (r.id === SITE_REG.id ? SITE_REG.sd : sdGuess(r.id, String(r.since || '').slice(0, 4))), since: r.since || SITE_REG.since, end: (() => { const e = endDay(r.id), n = r.now && r.now.id !== r.id ? r.now.since : null; return e && n ? (e < n ? e : n) : (e || n); })()} /* never past the official end (code review, Nov 24) */; }
// Official end of each regulation (Nov 17). Limitless can keep tagging events with the old regulation after it ends (checked
// Nov 17: events listed from Dec 3 tagged "Regulation Set M-C"), so an event dated at or after the end never counts for it,
// whatever its tag: it counts for the next regulation. M-C ends Dec 1, 5:59 PM PST = Dec 2 01:59 UTC; M-D runs Dec 2
// 02:00 UTC to Mar 3 2027 01:59 UTC (source of truth: Pokémon HOME news 878, checked Nov 25; pokemon.com's "Wed Dec 2 6 PM PST" is a
// slip). experiments/reg-switch-test.mjs pins this and the page's copy (V2.REG_END_MC). Add each regulation's end here when it is official.
export const REG_END = {'M-C': '2026-12-02T01:59:00Z'};
export const regAt = (f, date) => { let id = f; for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const end = REG_END[id], t = Date.parse(String(date || ''));
  if (!end || !isFinite(t) || t < Date.parse(end)) break; const nx = nextRegIds(id)[0]; if (!nx) break; id = nx; } return id; };
// an event's regulation: saved with it from Nov 1; older saved events are all Reg M-C (the only format the job ever fetched)
export const regOf = e => regAt((e && typeof e.f === 'string' && e.f) || SITE_REG.id, e && (e.d || e.date));
export const sameReg = (e, id) => regOf(e) === id;
export const inRegWindow = (date, reg) => { const d = String(date || '').slice(0, 10); return !!d && d >= reg.since && (!reg.end || d < reg.end); };
// list: Limitless tournaments of any format [{format, date, players}]; sd: {showdownFormat: {n, first}} from replay searches;
// prev: yesterday's out/reg.json. Pure, so experiments/reg-test.mjs can feed it made-up data.
export function detectReg(list, sd, prev, today) {
  today = today || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  let id = prev && REG_ID.test(prev.id) ? prev.id : SITE_REG.id, since = (prev && prev.since) || SITE_REG.since, sdId = (prev && prev.sd) || SITE_REG.sd;
  const fam = id.split('-')[0] + '-', seen = {}, rows = (Array.isArray(list) ? list : []).map(t => ({f: REG_ID.test(String(t && t.format || '')) ? regAt(String(t.format), t.date) : String(t && t.format || ''), d: String(t && t.date || '').slice(0, 10), p: +(t && t.players) || 0}))
    .filter(t => t.f.startsWith(fam) && REG_ID.test(t.f) && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(t.d));
  const last = rows.reduce((a, t) => t.d > a ? t.d : a, ''), cut = last ? new Date(Date.parse(last) - 6 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : '';
  for (const t of rows) { const s = seen[t.f] = seen[t.f] || {events: 0, players: 0, first: t.d, last: t.d, week: 0};
    s.events++; s.players += t.p; if (t.d < s.first) s.first = t.d; if (t.d > s.last) s.last = t.d; if (t.d >= cut) s.week++; }
  // the regulation being played now: most events in the newest week (a tie goes to the one that started later)
  const nowId = Object.keys(seen).sort((a, b) => seen[b].week - seen[a].week || (seen[b].first > seen[a].first ? 1 : -1))[0];
  const sdFor = r => { const k = Object.keys(sd || {}).find(f => sdReg(f) === r && +sd[f].n > 0); return k || sdGuess(r, today.slice(0, 4)); };
  let now = null;
  if (nowId && nowId !== id && seen[nowId].first >= since) now = {id: nowId, since: seen[nowId].first, events: seen[nowId].events, players: seen[nowId].players, week: seen[nowId].week, from: 'limitless'};
  // no tournaments yet, but the next regulation's ladder has started on Showdown (20+ replays): say so too
  if (!now) for (const [f, x] of Object.entries(sd || {})) { const r = sdReg(f); if (r && r !== id && !seen[r] && x && +x.n >= 20 && x.first) now = {id: r, since: String(x.first).slice(0, 10), events: 0, players: 0, week: 0, ladder: +x.n, from: 'showdown'}; }
  let switched = prev && prev.switched || null;
  if (now && REG_AUTO_SWITCH && now.events >= REG_SWITCH.events && now.players >= REG_SWITCH.players) { switched = {from: id, to: now.id, on: today}; id = now.id; since = now.since; sdId = sdFor(id); now = null; }
  return {id, name: regName(id), since, sd: sdId, site: SITE_REG.id, now: now ? {...now, name: regName(now.id), need: REG_SWITCH} : null, switched, seen, checked: today}; }

===== FILE: update.mjs (claude/design6)
// Daily PokéLair data job: pulls new Reg M-C tournaments from the Limitless public API (staying under its limit of
// 50 requests per 5 minutes), keeps every standing in data/, and rebuilds out/limitless.json:
//  - adj: each Pokémon's own share of team wins (one model over every team, so partners don't get credit)
//  - results: Pokémon win rates, proven pairs and cores, and the exact winning team lists ("proven teams")
// v2 (Oct 26): also keeps every player's full team sheet (moves, items, abilities), each tournament's pairings (who beat
// whom), and builds out/meta_tour.json (sets from real teams), out/teams.json (recent top finishes), out/history.json
// (daily usage ranks for trends) and out/matches.json (game-level results for the team model).
// PokéLair's daily refresh copies these files into the page's data. Run: node update.mjs
import {pid, hasKey} from './pid.mjs';
import {updateModes} from './tour-modes.mjs';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {topcut} from './topcut.mjs';
import {offFormat, oddEvents, activeReg, sameReg, REG_END, nextRegIds} from './formats.mjs';
import {stampReg} from './reg-stamp.mjs';
import {politeFetch} from './http.mjs';
import {lkey, lmap, reportUnknown, canonSet, canonReport, canonNature} from './names.mjs';
import {primaryStatus, fetchBackupEvents} from './sources/fallback.mjs';
import {victoryRoadAdapter} from './sources/victoryroad.mjs';
import {showdownOtsAdapter} from './sources/showdown-ots.mjs';
// the regulation every number is built from (out/reg.json from reg.mjs; Reg M-C until a newer one has enough data)
const RG = activeReg();
const API = 'https://play.limitlesstcg.com/api', FORMAT = RG.id, MAX_FETCH = +(process.env.MAX_FETCH || 40), GAP_MS = +(process.env.GAP||6500), USE_BACKUPS = process.env.BACKUP_SOURCES === '1';
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const readJSON = (f, d) => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return d; } };
fs.mkdirSync('data/t', {recursive: true}); fs.mkdirSync('data/t2', {recursive: true}); fs.mkdirSync('data/p', {recursive: true}); fs.mkdirSync('out', {recursive: true});
const index = readJSON('data/index.json', null) || {tournaments: {}};
if (!index.tournaments || typeof index.tournaments !== 'object') index.tournaments = {};
// Fail-safe (stress test, Oct 28): a missing or corrupt index.json used to make the job rebuild every output from nothing
// (0 teams) and commit it. The standings files on disk are the truth: put any tournament they hold back into the index.
{ let healed = 0; for (const f of fs.readdirSync('data/t')) { const id = f.replace(/\.json$/, ''); if (index.tournaments[id]) continue; const T = readJSON(`data/t/${f}`, null); if (!T || !T.d) continue;
    const T2 = readJSON(`data/t2/${f}`, null); index.tournaments[id] = {d: T.d, p: T.p, name: (T2 && T2.name) || '', f: T.f || undefined}; healed++; }
  if (healed) console.log(`index: restored ${healed} tournaments from data/t`); }
let calls = 0;
async function get(path) {
  for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) {
    if (calls++) await sleep(GAP_MS);
    const r = await politeFetch(API + path);
    if (r.status === 429) { await sleep(((+r.headers.get('retry-after')) || 60) * 1000 + 2000); continue; }
    if (!r.ok) throw new Error(path + ' ' + r.status);
    return r.json();
  }
  throw new Error(path + ' kept hitting the rate limit');
}
// mega form from the held item: "Raichunite X" -> "Raichu|MX"
const key = lkey; // shared with weekly.mjs and events.mjs (names.mjs)

// 1) tournament list, then standings for new ones and for anything from the last 3 days (results can still change)
const list = [];
let primaryFailed = false;
try {
  for (let page = 1; page < 30; page++) { const r = await get(`/tournaments?game=VGC&format=${FORMAT}&limit=100&page=${page}`); if (!r.length) break; list.push(...r); }
  const prevF = Object.keys(REG_END).find(k => nextRegIds(k)[0] === FORMAT);
  if (prevF) { const seenIds = new Set(list.map(t => t.id)), cut = Date.parse(REG_END[prevF]);
    for (let page = 1; page <= 3; page++) { const r = await get(`/tournaments?game=VGC&format=${prevF}&limit=100&page=${page}`); if (!r.length) break;
      const kept = r.filter(t => !seenIds.has(t.id) && Date.parse(t.date) >= cut && sameReg({f: t.format || prevF, d: t.date}, FORMAT));
      for (const t of kept) { seenIds.add(t.id); list.push(t); }
      if (!r.some(t => Date.parse(t.date) >= cut)) break; } }
} catch (err) {
  if (!USE_BACKUPS) throw err;
  primaryFailed = true;
  console.log(`::warning::update: Limitless list failed (${err.message}); checking backup sources`);
}
// v2 files (full sheets + pairings) are backfilled for older tournaments too, newest first, within MAX_FETCH.
const now = Date.now(), hasP = id => fs.existsSync(`data/p/${id}.json`);
const has2 = id => { const T = readJSON(`data/t2/${id}.json`, null); return !!(T && T.v === 3) && hasP(id); };
/* an event id becomes a file name and a URL part (Kip job 36 A #3): anything else than letters, digits, - and _ is skipped */
const safeId = id => typeof id === 'string' && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$/.test(id);
const todo = list.filter(t => safeId(t && t.id) && (!index.tournaments[t.id] || !has2(t.id) || (now - new Date(t.date)) < 3 * 864e5))
  .sort((a, b) => (index.tournaments[a.id] ? 1 : 0) - (index.tournaments[b.id] ? 1 : 0) || new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date)).slice(0, MAX_FETCH);
let fetched = 0, logged = false;
const mv = m => ({n: m.name || '', i: /^[mfMF]$/.test(m.item || '') ? '' : (m.item || ''), /* data audit Nov 8: one sheet had genders in the item field */ a: m.ability || '', mv: Array.isArray(m.attacks) ? m.attacks.map(String) : Array.isArray(m.moves) ? m.moves.map(String) : [], t: m.tera || '', nt: m.nature || ''});
for (const t of todo) {
  try { const s = await get(`/tournaments/${t.id}/standings`);
    // never replace standings we already have with an empty or much shorter list (a half-outage answers [] for a while)
    { const old = readJSON(`data/t/${t.id}.json`, null), n0 = old && Array.isArray(old.e) ? old.e.length : 0;
      if (!Array.isArray(s) || (n0 >= 4 && s.length < n0 * .5)) { console.log(`kept ${t.id}: standings came back with ${Array.isArray(s) ? s.length : 'no'} players (had ${n0})`); continue; } }
    if (!logged && s[0]) { console.log('sample standing fields:', Object.keys(s[0]).join(', ')); }  /* field names only: the Actions log is public */
    // player names become ID numbers here, before anything is saved (pid.mjs; Sim, Nov 10)
    const e = s.map(x => [pid(x.player || x.name || ''), x.record ? x.record.wins : 0, x.record ? x.record.losses : 0, (x.decklist || []).map(key)]);
    fs.writeFileSync(`data/t/${t.id}.json`, JSON.stringify({d: t.date, p: t.players, e, f: t.format || FORMAT}));
    const e2 = s.map(x => ({id: pid(x.player || ''), nm: pid(x.player || x.name || ''), pl: x.placing ?? null, w: x.record ? x.record.wins : 0, l: x.record ? x.record.losses : 0, ti: x.record ? (x.record.ties || 0) : 0, dk: (x.decklist || []).map(mv)}));
    fs.writeFileSync(`data/t2/${t.id}.json`, JSON.stringify({v: 3, d: t.date, p: t.players, name: t.name, f: t.format || FORMAT, e: e2}));
    let pr = [];
    if (hasP(t.id) && (now - new Date(t.date)) >= 3 * 864e5) { logged = true; index.tournaments[t.id] = {d: t.date, p: t.players, name: t.name, f: t.format || FORMAT}; fetched++; continue; }
    try { const P = await get(`/tournaments/${t.id}/pairings`);
      if (!logged && P[0]) console.log('sample pairing fields:', Object.keys(P[0]).join(', '));
      // winner is an account (becomes its ID; accounts can be short numbers) or a result code ('0' a draw, '-1' no result), kept as is
      const W = w => w == null ? null : String(w) === '0' || String(w) === '-1' ? String(w) : pid(w);
      pr = (Array.isArray(P) ? P : []).map(x => [x.round ?? null, pid(x.player1 ?? ''), pid(x.player2 ?? ''), W(x.winner)]); } catch (err) { console.log('no pairings', t.id, err.message); }
    // a failed or shorter pairings fetch never overwrites the pairings already saved
    { const old = readJSON(`data/p/${t.id}.json`, null); if (!(Array.isArray(old) && old.length > pr.length)) fs.writeFileSync(`data/p/${t.id}.json`, JSON.stringify(pr)); }
    logged = true;
    index.tournaments[t.id] = {d: t.date, p: t.players, name: t.name, f: t.format || FORMAT}; fetched++;
  } catch (err) { console.log('skip', t.id, err.message); }
}
if (USE_BACKUPS) {
  const st = primaryStatus({list: primaryFailed ? null : list, index, format: FORMAT});
  if (!st.ok) {
    console.log(`::warning::update: primary source ${st.reason} (newest ${st.newest || 'none'}); running backup adapters`);

      const e2 = s.map(x => ({id: pid(x.player || ''), nm: pid(x.player || x.name || ''), pl: x.placing ?? null, w: x.record ? x.record.wins : 0, l: x.record ? x.record.losses : 0, ti: x.record ? (x.record.ties || 0) : 0, dk: (x.decklist || []).map(mv)}));

  }
}
fs.writeFileSync('data/index.json', JSON.stringify(index));
console.log(`list ${list.length} tournaments, fetched ${fetched}, cached ${Object.keys(index.tournaments).length}`);
// one try per event, 30 s at most, and a rate limit ends the step for today (review Nov 17: the main job must never wait on it)
const modeGet = async path => { if (calls++) await sleep(GAP_MS); const r = await politeFetch(API + path, {signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30e3)});
  if (r.status === 429) throw Object.assign(new Error('rate limited'), {stop: true}); if (!r.ok) throw new Error(path + ' ' + r.status); return r.json(); };
// 1b) round format per event (Sim, Nov 17: single games first): best-of-1 or best-of-3 per phase into data/tour-modes.json,

// standard Reg M-C events only: themed and restricted cups are kept on disk but left out of every number below

// 2) all teams with a full six and 3+ games
const E = []; let first = null, last = null;
for (const id of KEEP) { const T = readJSON(`data/t/${id}.json`, null); if (!T) continue;
  first = !first || T.d < first ? T.d : first; last = !last || T.d > last ? T.d : last;
  for (const [pl, w, l, ks] of T.e) { const m = [...new Set(ks)]; if (m.length === 6 && w + l >= 3) E.push({m, w, l}); } }
const keys = [...new Set(E.flatMap(e => e.m))], ki = new Map(keys.map((k, i) => [k, i])); E.forEach(e => e.ix = e.m.map(k => ki.get(k)));
let W = 0, G = 0; for (const e of E) { W += e.w; G += e.w + e.l; } const base = W / G;
// Fail-safe: never write outputs built from far less data than yesterday's (or with a nonsense win rate). Exiting here
// stops the daily job before its commit, so the page keeps yesterday's data and the failed run emails a warning.
{ const prev = readJSON('out/limitless.json', null);
  // (a regulation switch starts over from the new regulation's teams, so yesterday's count doesn't apply then)
  const sameAsPrev = !prev || (prev.reg || 'M-C') === FORMAT;
  if (!(base > .4 && base < .6) || (sameAsPrev && prev && prev.teams >= 100 && E.length < prev.teams * .8)) {
    console.log(`::error::update: only ${E.length} teams (yesterday ${prev ? prev.teams : '?'}), base ${base}; outputs left unchanged`); process.exit(1); } }

// 3) teammate-adjusted share of the win: logistic model over every team, ridge-shrunk (fit with Adam)
function fit(lb, iters) { const n = keys.length, b = new Float64Array(n), mb = new Float64Array(n), vb = new Float64Array(n); let a = 0, ma = 0, va = 0;
  for (let t = 1; t <= iters; t++) { const gb = new Float64Array(n); let ga = 0;
    for (const e of E) { let eta = a; for (const j of e.ix) eta += b[j]; const d = (e.w + e.l) / (1 + Math.exp(-eta)) - e.w; ga += d; for (const j of e.ix) gb[j] += d; }
    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) gb[j] += 2 * lb * b[j];
    const c1 = 1 - .9 ** t, c2 = 1 - .999 ** t; ma = .9 * ma + .1 * ga; va = .999 * va + .001 * ga * ga; a -= .05 * (ma / c1) / (Math.sqrt(va / c2) + 1e-8);
    for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) { mb[j] = .9 * mb[j] + .1 * gb[j]; vb[j] = .999 * vb[j] + .001 * gb[j] ** 2; b[j] -= .05 * (mb[j] / c1) / (Math.sqrt(vb[j] / c2) + 1e-8); } }
  return b; }
const B = fit(30, 500);

// 4) Limitless names -> PokéLair names
const ROSTER = readJSON('roster.json', []), names = new Set(ROSTER), REG = {Hisuian: 'Hisui', Alolan: 'Alola', Galarian: 'Galar', Paldean: 'Paldea'};
/* lmap() now lives in names.mjs (shared) */
const nm = keys.map(lmap); reportUnknown('update');

// 5) per-Pokémon numbers, pairs (25+ teams), cores (40+ teams), proven team lists
const shrink = (w, g, K) => Math.round(1000 * (w + K * base) / (g + K)) / 1000;
const mon = new Map();
keys.forEach((k, j) => { const n = nm[j]; if (!n) return; let w = 0, g = 0, t = 0; for (const e of E) if (e.ix.includes(j)) { w += e.w; g += e.w + e.l; t++; }
  const o = mon.get(n) || {w: 0, g: 0, t: 0, bs: 0}; o.w += w; o.g += g; o.t += t; o.bs += B[j] * g; mon.set(n, o); });
const P = new Map(), C3 = new Map(), comp = new Map(), MG = [0, 0, 0, 0]; // MG: full sixes with 0 / 1 / 2 / 3+ Mega options (Build's Megas tip, Q13d Nov 18; experiments/mega-share.mjs re-counts it)
for (const e of E) { const m = [...new Set(e.ix.map(j => nm[j]).filter(Boolean))].sort(); if (m.length < 6) continue; const g = e.w + e.l; MG[Math.min(3, m.filter(x => /-Mega/.test(x)).length)]++;
  const ck = m.join('|'), co = comp.get(ck) || {n: 0, w: 0, g: 0}; co.n++; co.w += e.w; co.g += g; comp.set(ck, co);
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 6; j++) { const k = m[i] + '|' + m[j], o = P.get(k) || {n: 0, w: 0, g: 0}; o.n++; o.w += e.w; o.g += g; P.set(k, o);
    for (let l = j + 1; l < 6; l++) { const k3 = k + '|' + m[l], q = C3.get(k3) || {n: 0, w: 0, g: 0}; q.n++; q.w += e.w; q.g += g; C3.set(k3, q); } } }
const day = d => new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric'});
const src = `${E.length.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${RG.name} tournament teams, ${G.toLocaleString('en-US')} games (Limitless, ${KEEP.length} events, ${day(first)} – ${day(last)})`;
const out = {updated: new Date().toISOString(), reg: FORMAT, tournaments: KEEP.length, teams: E.length, games: G, base: Math.round(base * 1e4) / 1e4,
  adj: {label: `Limitless · ${KEEP.length} ${RG.name} tournaments (${day(first)} – ${day(last)}), ${E.length.toLocaleString('en-US')} teams, ${G.toLocaleString('en-US')} games · each Pokémon's own share of the win (teammates accounted for)`,
    rows: [...mon].map(([n, o]) => [n, Math.round(1000 * o.bs / (o.g || 1)) / 1000, o.g, o.t, Math.round(1000 * o.w / (o.g || 1)) / 10])},
  // the ratings' tournament input, rebuilt every day from these same teams (it used to be a hand-made Pokémon Zone snapshot):
  // [name, % of teams, game win %, games]. Megas are already their own rows (split: the page doesn't subtract them again).
  tour: {label: `Limitless · ${KEEP.length} ${RG.name} tournaments (${day(first)} – ${day(last)}), ${E.length.toLocaleString('en-US')} teams · usage and game win rate`, split: true,
    updated: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
    rows: [...mon].filter(([n]) => !/-Mega/.test(n)).map(([n, o]) => [n, Math.round(1000 * o.t / E.length) / 10, Math.round(1000 * o.w / (o.g || 1)) / 10, o.g]).sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1]),
    megas: [...mon].filter(([n]) => /-Mega/.test(n)).map(([n, o]) => [n, Math.round(1000 * o.t / E.length) / 10, Math.round(1000 * o.w / (o.g || 1)) / 10, o.g]).sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1])},
  results: {source: src, teams: E.length, base: Math.round(base * 1e4) / 1e4, minCore: 40, megaN: MG,
    mons: Object.fromEntries([...mon].map(([n, o]) => [n, [o.t, shrink(o.w, o.g, 40)]])),
    pairs: Object.fromEntries([...P].filter(([, o]) => o.n >= 25).map(([k, o]) => [k, [o.n, shrink(o.w, o.g, 40)]])),
    cores: [...C3].filter(([, o]) => o.n >= 40).map(([k, o]) => [...k.split('|'), o.n, shrink(o.w, o.g, 40)]).sort((a, b) => b[4] - a[4]),
    proven: [...comp].map(([k, o]) => [k.split('|'), o.n, shrink(o.w, o.g, 20), o.g]).filter(x => x[2] >= base + .01 && (x[1] >= 2 || x[3] >= 8)).sort((a, b) => b[2] - a[2] || b[1] - a[1]),
    // every exact six with 30+ games, winning or not: the page grades a team on its real record (sweep, Oct 31: the most-played
    // six, 45.7% won over 2,119 games, was missing because proven keeps winners only)
    records: [...comp].filter(([, o]) => o.g >= 30).map(([k, o]) => [k.split('|'), o.n, shrink(o.w, o.g, 20), o.g]).sort((a, b) => b[3] - a[3])}};
fs.writeFileSync('out/limitless.json', JSON.stringify(out));
console.log(`teams ${E.length}, games ${G}, pokemon ${mon.size}, pairs ${Object.keys(out.results.pairs).length}, cores ${out.results.cores.length}, proven ${out.results.proven.length}`);

// ---------- v2 outputs ----------
// Two readings of the same sheets (Sim, Nov 18, morning question 8: split the one-spelling fix).
//  - V2T, as typed: the grade's input only (4) matches.json's set features, read by model.mjs and six.mjs). Folding them
//    made the walk-forward grade slightly worse beyond chance on best-of-3 (docs/rebuild/research/data-fixes-effect-nov18.md).
//  - V2, folded: what players see (1) sets page, 2) top finishes, 5) top-cut evidence). Every item, Ability and move in its
//    list spelling ("protect", "Protect " -> "Protect"; names.mjs canonSet), so one set no longer shows as several rows.
// The Mega Stone reading (lkey reads "raichunite y", "Lucarionite-Z") applies to both: nameOf below uses it either way.
// Read from the raw sheets each day; the files in data/t2 stay as fetched.
const V2T = KEEP.map(id => { const T = readJSON(`data/t2/${id}.json`, null); return T ? {id, ...T} : null; }).filter(Boolean)
  .sort((a, b) => new Date(a.d) - new Date(b.d));
