canonReport('update');
const nameOf = m => lmap(key({name: m.n, item: m.i}));
const pct = (c, n, k) => [...c].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, k).map(([x, v]) => [x, Math.round(1000 * v / n) / 10]);
const DAY = 864e5, lastD = V2.length ? new Date(V2[V2.length - 1].d).getTime() : Date.now();
// 1) sets from real team sheets, last 28 days (sets drift; usage and win rates above use every event)
{ const S = new Map(); let nT = 0;
  for (const T of V2) { if (lastD - new Date(T.d) > 28 * DAY) continue;
    for (const p of T.e) { if (p.dk.length !== 6) continue; const ns = p.dk.map(nameOf); if (ns.some(n => !n)) continue; nT++;
      p.dk.forEach((m, i) => { const n = ns[i]; let o = S.get(n); if (!o) S.set(n, o = {t: 0, w: 0, g: 0, mv: new Map(), it: new Map(), ab: new Map(), tm: new Map(), na: new Map(), cs: new Map()});
        o.t++; o.w += p.w; o.g += p.w + p.l; if (m.nt) o.na.set(m.nt, (o.na.get(m.nt) || 0) + 1);
        for (const x of new Set(m.mv)) o.mv.set(x, (o.mv.get(x) || 0) + 1);
        if (m.i) o.it.set(m.i, (o.it.get(m.i) || 0) + 1); if (m.a) o.ab.set(m.a, (o.ab.get(m.a) || 0) + 1);
        /* the most common COMPLETE set (Sim, Nov 8: show the most popular moveset as one real build, not separate top picks) */
        { const mv = [...new Set(m.mv)].sort(); if (mv.length === 4) { const k = JSON.stringify([m.i || '', m.a || '', mv]); o.cs.set(k, (o.cs.get(k) || 0) + 1); } }
        ns.forEach((q, j) => { if (j !== i) o.tm.set(q, (o.tm.get(q) || 0) + 1); }); }); } }
  const rows = [...S].sort((a, b) => b[1].t - a[1].t).map(([n, o], i) => ({r: i + 1, n, u: Math.round(1000 * o.t / nT) / 10, teams: o.t, w: o.g ? Math.round(1000 * o.w / o.g) / 10 : null,
    mv: pct(o.mv, o.t, 12), it: pct(o.it, o.t, 6), ab: pct(o.ab, o.t, 4), na: pct(o.na, o.t, 4), tm: pct(o.tm, o.t, 10),
    cs: (() => { const b = [...o.cs].sort((x, y) => y[1] - x[1])[0]; if (!b) return undefined; const [it, ab, mv] = JSON.parse(b[0]); return {it, ab, mv, pct: Math.round(1000 * b[1] / o.t) / 10}; })()}));
  const recent = V2.filter(T => lastD - new Date(T.d) <= 28 * DAY);
  fs.writeFileSync('out/meta_tour.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg({format: 'doubles', kind: 'tour', source: `Limitless team sheets · ${nT.toLocaleString('en-US')} teams from ${recent.length} ${RG.name} tournaments`,
    period: recent.length ? `${day(recent[0].d)} – ${day(recent[recent.length - 1].d)}` : '', updated: new Date().toISOString(), note: 'Moves, items, abilities and natures as players registered them.',
    top: rows.slice(0, 60), more: rows.slice(60, 100)}, FORMAT)));
  console.log(`meta_tour: ${nT} teams, ${rows.length} Pokémon`); }
// 1b) info sheets (Sim approved the design Nov 22, 11:29 AM; docs/rebuild/info-sheets/HANDOFF.md): for every move, item and Ability, how many
//     of the last 28 days' full team sheets run it and who runs it. Counted straight from the raw sheets, not from the top-12 lists above
//     (those double-count: Protect would read 409%, and hide 183 moves). Each entry: [teams, top 8 users [name, how many of its teams run it, its teams], how many different Pokémon run it].
//     Same KEEP filter, 28-day window, canonSet spelling and nameOf names as meta_tour. Writes out/info.json only.
//     Natures (Dossier step 3, Nov 23): counted the same way from the sheet's nature (names.mjs canonNature: misspellings folded, unreadable
//     ones left out); a nature belongs to one Pokémon, so each nature entry also carries how many Pokémon run it (4th field), and ntN is
//     how many Pokémon had a readable nature (the share on the Nature sheet is 'of tournament Pokémon').
{ const K = {mv: new Map(), it: new Map(), ab: new Map(), nt: new Map()}, per = new Map(); let N = 0, ntN = 0;
  for (const T of V2) { if (lastD - new Date(T.d) > 28 * DAY) continue;
    for (const p of T.e) { if (p.dk.length !== 6) continue; const ns = p.dk.map(nameOf); if (ns.some(n => !n)) continue; N++;
      const seen = {mv: new Set(), it: new Set(), ab: new Set(), nt: new Set()};
      p.dk.forEach((m, i) => { const n = ns[i]; per.set(n, (per.get(n) || 0) + 1);
        const add = (k, x) => { if (!x) return; let o = K[k].get(x); if (!o) K[k].set(x, o = {t: 0, m: 0, u: new Map()}); if (!seen[k].has(x)) { seen[k].add(x); o.t++; } o.m++; o.u.set(n, (o.u.get(n) || 0) + 1); };
        for (const x of new Set(m.mv)) add('mv', x); add('it', m.i); add('ab', m.a); const nt = canonNature(m.nt); if (nt) { ntN++; add('nt', nt); } }); } }
  const pack = (M, mons) => Object.fromEntries([...M].sort((a, b) => b[1].t - a[1].t).map(([x, o]) => [x, [o.t, [...o.u].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1] || a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).slice(0, 8).map(([n, c]) => [n, c, per.get(n)]), o.u.size, ...(mons ? [o.m] : [])]]));
  const recent = V2.filter(T => lastD - new Date(T.d) <= 28 * DAY);
  fs.writeFileSync('out/info.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg({N, source: `Limitless team sheets · ${N.toLocaleString('en-US')} teams from ${recent.length} ${RG.name} tournaments`,
    period: recent.length ? `${day(recent[0].d)} – ${day(recent[recent.length - 1].d)}` : '', mv: pack(K.mv), it: pack(K.it), ab: pack(K.ab), ntN, nt: pack(K.nt, true)}, FORMAT)));
  console.log(`info: ${N} teams, ${K.mv.size} moves, ${K.it.size} items, ${K.ab.size} Abilities, ${K.nt.size} natures (${ntN} of ${6 * N} Pokémon with a readable nature)`); }
// 2) recent top finishes (events with 32+ players, last 14 days): top 4 of each, newest first, up to 60 teams
{ const out = [];
  for (const T of [...V2].reverse()) { if (lastD - new Date(T.d) > 14 * DAY || (T.p || 0) < 32) continue;
    for (const p of T.e.filter(p => p.pl != null && p.pl <= 4 && p.dk.length === 6).sort((a, b) => a.pl - b.pl)) {
      const ns = p.dk.map(nameOf); if (ns.some(n => !n)) continue;
      out.push({player: p.nm, event: `${T.name} · ${T.p} players`, record: `${p.w}-${p.l}${p.ti ? '-' + p.ti : ''} · #${p.pl}`, section: lastD - new Date(T.d) <= 7 * DAY ? 'Last 7 days' : 'Last 14 days', mons: ns}); }
    if (out.length >= 60) break; }
  // keep yesterday's official-event teams (official.mjs refreshes them next; if its sources are down they'd otherwise vanish)
  const prevT = readJSON('out/teams.json', null), off = prevT && (prevT.reg || 'M-C') === FORMAT && Array.isArray(prevT.teams) ? prevT.teams.filter(t => t && t.section === 'Official events') : [];
  fs.writeFileSync('out/teams.json', JSON.stringify({reg: FORMAT, source: `Limitless · top 4 of ${RG.name} events with 32+ players` + (off.length ? ' + ' + ((prevT.source || '').match(/\+ ((?:RK9|Victory Road) official events.*)$/) || [, 'Victory Road official events (top 8)'])[1] : ''), updated: new Date().toISOString(), teams: [...off, ...out.slice(0, 60)].slice(0, 90)}));
  console.log(`teams: ${out.length}`); }
// 3) usage-rank history for trends: one entry per day for the last 21 days, each from the 14 days before it
{ const days = [];
  for (let k = 20; k >= 0; k--) { const end = lastD - k * DAY, c = new Map(); let n = 0;
    for (const id of KEEP) { const T = readJSON(`data/t/${id}.json`, null); if (!T) continue; const t = new Date(T.d).getTime(); if (t > end || t <= end - 14 * DAY) continue;
      for (const [, w, l, ks] of T.e) { const ms = [...new Set(ks.map(lmap).filter(Boolean))]; if (ms.length !== 6) continue; n++; for (const m of ms) c.set(m, (c.get(m) || 0) + 1); } }
    if (!n) continue; days.push({d: new Date(end).toISOString().slice(0, 10), tour: Object.fromEntries([...c].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 80).map(([m], i) => [m, i + 1]))}); }
  fs.writeFileSync('out/history.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg({days}, FORMAT))); console.log(`history: ${days.length} days`); }
// 4) game-level results: every pairing with both teams known (for the team model and player skill). Sheets AS TYPED (V2T):
// the grade's set features keep the players' own spellings (split of the one-spelling fix, Nov 18; see V2T above)
{ const teamIx = new Map(), teams = [], players = new Map(), plist = [], M = [], featIx = new Map(), feats = [], deckIx = new Map(), decks = [];
  // each player's exact sets as features ("Garchomp@Choice Scarf", "Garchomp#Rough Skin", "Garchomp:Earthquake") for the win model;
  // Abilities added Nov 8 (scoreboard: a small, steady gain at all three date cut-offs)
  const fix = f => { if (!featIx.has(f)) { featIx.set(f, feats.length); feats.push(f); } return featIx.get(f); };
  const dix = (ns, dk) => { const f = []; dk.forEach((m, i) => { if (m.i) f.push(fix(ns[i] + '@' + m.i)); if (m.a) f.push(fix(ns[i] + '#' + m.a.replace(/\b\w/g, c => c.toUpperCase()))); for (const x of new Set(m.mv)) f.push(fix(ns[i] + ':' + x)); }); const k = f.sort((a, b) => a - b).join(','); if (!deckIx.has(k)) { deckIx.set(k, decks.length); decks.push(f); } return deckIx.get(k); };
  const tix = ns => { const k = [...ns].sort().join('|'); if (!teamIx.has(k)) { teamIx.set(k, teams.length); teams.push(k); } return teamIx.get(k); };
  const pix = k => { if (!players.has(k)) { players.set(k, plist.length); plist.push(k); } return players.get(k); };
  V2T.forEach((T, ti) => { const P = readJSON(`data/p/${T.id}.json`, []); if (!P.length) return; const by = new Map();
    for (const p of T.e) { if (p.dk.length !== 6) continue; const ns = p.dk.map(nameOf); if (ns.some(n => !n) || new Set(ns).size !== 6) continue; /* data audit Nov 8: a sheet listed a Pokémon twice */ by.set(p.id, {t: tix(ns), pl: pix(p.id ? 'id:' + String(p.id).toLowerCase() : p.nm.toLowerCase().trim()) /* the Limitless account, not the display name (sweep, Oct 31: 517 accounts showed under 2+ names) */, d: dix(ns, p.dk)}); }
    const seenG = new Set(); /* data audit Nov 8: skip repeated pairings (same round, same two players) and self-pairings */
    for (const [r, a, b, win] of P) { const A = by.get(a), B = by.get(b); if (!A || !B || a === b || A.pl === B.pl) continue; const gk = r + ':' + [a, b].sort().join(':'); if (seenG.has(gk)) continue; seenG.add(gk); const res = win === a ? 1 : win === b ? 0 : .5; M.push([A.t, B.t, res, ti, A.pl, B.pl, r, A.d, B.d]); } });
  fs.writeFileSync('out/matches.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg({updated: new Date().toISOString(), events: V2T.map(T => [T.id, T.d.slice(0, 10), T.p]), teams, players: plist.length, feats, decks, m: M}, FORMAT)));
  console.log(`matches: ${M.length} games, ${teams.length} distinct teams, ${plist.length} players`); }
// 5) top-cut evidence for the ratings (top-cut share, conversion, win rate, rating-weighted usage, bring/lead rates
// from replays), into results.tc of out/limitless.json. Runs last so it reads today's matches.json.
{ const R0 = readJSON('out/replays.json', null), R = R0 && R0.format && R0.format !== RG.sd ? null : R0, REP = /* another regulation's replays never reach the ratings (code review, Nov 24) */ Array.isArray(R) ? R : R ? (Object.values(R).find(Array.isArray) || []) : [];
  const tc = topcut(V2, nameOf, readJSON('out/matches.json', null), REP);
  if (tc) { out.results.tc = tc; fs.writeFileSync('out/limitless.json', JSON.stringify(out)); console.log(`topcut: ${Object.keys(tc.rows).length} Pokémon from ${tc.events} events`); } }

===== FILE: model-guard.mjs (claude/design6)
// Daily model guard (Foundation Phase 4, Nov 9): runs after model.mjs, six.mjs and ctr.mjs. Compares today's out/model.json with
// yesterday's (the committed one) and, if today's is clearly broken or worse, puts yesterday's back and exits 1 so the daily run
// records it (node model-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs ...). Checks:
//   1. complete: 250+ Pokémon, the team factors (noMega, protectLow) present and negative, calibration 0.6-1.2, the grade's
//      reference distribution present
//   2. accuracy: held-out calibrated log-loss not worse than yesterday's by more than 0.005, and better than a coin flip (0.693)
//   3. stability: the 200 most-played real sixes keep their order (rank correlation 0.85+ between yesterday's and today's strength)
// GUARD_PREV=<file> compares with that file instead of git's committed copy (for tests).
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {execSync} from 'node:child_process'; import {activeReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const F = 'out/model.json', now = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(F, 'utf8'));
let prevTxt = null; try { prevTxt = process.env.GUARD_PREV ? fs.readFileSync(process.env.GUARD_PREV, 'utf8') : execSync('git show HEAD:' + F, {encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 64e6}); } catch (e) {}
const rawPrev = prevTxt ? JSON.parse(prevTxt) : null, curReg = now.reg || activeReg().id, prevReg = rawPrev && (rawPrev.reg || SITE_REG.id);
const prev = rawPrev && prevReg === curReg ? rawPrev : null, bad = [];
if (rawPrev && !prev) console.log(`model guard: ignoring previous ${prevReg} model (active ${curReg})`);
if (!now.ships) { if (prev && prev.ships) { console.log(`::warning::model guard: ${curReg} model did not ship -> kept previous ${curReg} model`); fs.writeFileSync(F, prevTxt); process.exit(1); }
  console.log(`model guard: ${curReg} not ready (${now.games || 0} games, ${now.events || 0} events); no previous ${curReg} model, accepted`); process.exit(0); }
// 1. complete
if (Object.keys(now.mons || {}).length < 250) bad.push(`only ${Object.keys(now.mons || {}).length} Pokémon`);
for (const k of ['noMega', 'protectLow']) if (!(now.team && now.team[k] < 0)) bad.push(`team factor ${k} missing or not negative (${now.team && now.team[k]})`);
if (!(now.calib >= .6 && now.calib <= 1.2)) bad.push(`calibration ${now.calib} outside 0.6-1.2`);
if (!(now.dist && Array.isArray(now.dist.q) && now.dist.q.length > 10)) bad.push('grade reference distribution missing');
// 2. accuracy
const ll = m => m && m.eval && m.eval.calibrated_logloss ? m.eval.calibrated_logloss.with_sets : null, l1 = ll(now), l0 = ll(prev);
if (l1 == null || !(l1 < .693)) bad.push(`held-out log-loss ${l1} (coin flip 0.693)`);
else if (l0 != null && l1 > l0 + .005) bad.push(`held-out log-loss worse: ${l0} → ${l1}`);
// 3. stability
let rho = null; if (prev) { try { const M = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('out/matches.json', 'utf8')), cnt = new Map();
  for (const g of M.m) for (const t of [g[0], g[1]]) cnt.set(t, (cnt.get(t) || 0) + 1);
  const top = [...cnt].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).map(([t]) => M.teams[t].split('|')).filter(t => t.length === 6).slice(0, 200);
  const x = (m, ns) => { let v = 0; for (const n of ns) v += m.mons[n] || 0; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 6; j++) { const k = ns[i] < ns[j] ? ns[i] + '|' + ns[j] : ns[j] + '|' + ns[i]; v += (m.pairs || {})[k] || 0; } return v; };
  const rk = a => { const o = a.map((v, i) => [v, i]).sort((p, q) => p[0] - q[0]), r = new Array(a.length); o.forEach(([, i], k) => r[i] = k); return r; };
  const a = rk(top.map(t => x(prev, t))), c = rk(top.map(t => x(now, t))), n = a.length; let d2 = 0; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d2 += (a[i] - c[i]) ** 2;
  rho = 1 - 6 * d2 / (n * (n * n - 1)); if (rho < .85) bad.push(`the 200 most-played sixes reordered (rank correlation ${rho.toFixed(3)}, need 0.85+)`); } catch (e) { console.log('stability check skipped: ' + e.message); } }
console.log(`model guard: ${Object.keys(now.mons || {}).length} Pokémon · log-loss ${l0} → ${l1} · calib ${now.calib} · rank correlation ${rho == null ? '—' : rho.toFixed(3)}`);
if (bad.length) { console.log('::warning::model guard: ' + bad.join('; ') + (prev ? ' → kept yesterday\'s model' : ' (no previous model to fall back to)'));
  if (prev) fs.writeFileSync(F, prevTxt); process.exit(1); }
console.log('model guard: ok');

===== FILE: health.mjs (claude/design6)
// Data health gate, run last in the daily job, after the data is committed: good data is already saved, this only raises
// the alarm. It fails the job (GitHub emails the repo owner, and the "Daily data problem" issue is opened or refreshed by
// the next workflow step) when a file the page relies on is missing, unreadable, the wrong shape or size, or older than its
// limit. Each file is judged by the date of its own data, not the day the job ran:
//   daily sources (Limitless, tournament sets, top teams, win model, ratings inputs, weekly digest) 2 days;
//   op.gg, official events, events, the scoreboard and the event calendar 3 days; replays practice 4 days;
//   Smogon's monthly ladder stats 40 days past the end of their month.
// A single failed day therefore never alerts: the previous day's file is still within its limit. Two failed days in a row do.
// Then it prints the cross-check summary (crosscheck.mjs) and warns, without failing, when several staples disagree with an
// outside source.
// Output: the console (::error:: / ::warning:: lines), a table in the job summary ($GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY), and the same text in
// $HEALTH_REPORT (a markdown file the issue step posts). Exit 1 when anything critical is wrong.
// Dry runs: node health.mjs [--root=<folder with out/ and data/>] [--now=2026-11-10]
import fs from 'node:fs'; import path from 'node:path';
const arg = k => { const a = process.argv.find(x => x.startsWith('--' + k + '=')); return a ? a.slice(k.length + 3) : null; };
const ROOT = arg('root') || '.', now = arg('now') ? Date.parse(arg('now') + 'T12:00:00Z') : Date.now(), DAY = 864e5;
const problems = [], rows = [];
const size = f => { try { return fs.statSync(path.join(ROOT, 'out', f)).size; } catch (e) { return -1; } };
const rd = f => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'out', f), 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return null; } };
const RG = rd('reg.json'), RS = rd('regstate.json'), REG = (RG && RG.id) || 'M-C';
const regDay = (() => { if (RS && RS.reg === REG && typeof RS.day === 'number') return RS.day; const s = RG && RG.since; if (!s) return 999;
  const t0 = Date.parse(s + 'T12:00:00Z'); return isFinite(t0) ? Math.max(1, Math.floor((now - t0) / DAY) + 1) : 999; })();
const inLaunch = regDay >= 1 && regDay <= 14, todayIso = new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const stampOf = d => d && (typeof d.reg === 'string' ? d.reg : (d.reg && typeof d.reg.id === 'string' ? d.reg.id : null));
// a date ("2026-10-31", an ISO time) or a month ("2026-09", counted from the end of that month)
const age = v => { const s = String(v || ''); if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) { const [y, m] = s.split('-').map(Number); return (now - Date.UTC(y, m, 1)) / DAY; } const t = Date.parse(s.length === 10 ? s + 'T12:00:00Z' : s); return isNaN(t) ? NaN : (now - t) / DAY; };
const old = (label, v, days) => { const a = age(v); if (!(a <= days)) { problems.push(`${label}: ${isNaN(a) ? 'no date' : `data from ${String(v).slice(0, 10)}, ${Math.floor(a)} days old (limit ${days})`}`); return false; } return true; };
const arr = (x, min = 1) => Array.isArray(x) && x.length >= min;
// file, label, max age in days, where its data date is, size bounds (bytes), and a shape test (true = fine, or a reason)
const rlDays = d => d && (d.byReg ? d.byReg[REG] : d.days);
const FILES = [
  ['limitless.json', 'Limitless tournaments', 2, d => d.updated, [50e3, 5e6], d => !!(d.results && typeof d.results === 'object' && d.adj && +d.teams > 0) || 'no results, win shares or team count', [20, d => !!(d.results && typeof d.results === 'object' && d.adj)]],
  ['meta_tour.json', 'Tournament sets', 2, d => d.updated, [5e3, 2e6], d => arr(d.top, 10) || 'fewer than 10 Pokémon', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.top)]],
  ['teams.json', 'Top teams', 2, d => d.updated, [2e3, 2e6], d => arr(d.teams, 5) || 'fewer than 5 teams', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.teams)]],
  ['history.json', 'Usage history', 2, d => (arr(d.days) && d.days[d.days.length - 1].d) || (inLaunch ? d.updated || (RG && RG.checked) || todayIso : ''), [1e3, 2e6], d => arr(d.days, 2) || 'fewer than 2 days', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.days)]],
  ['model.json', 'Win model', 2, d => d.updated, [5e3, 2e6], d => !!(d.mons && typeof d.mons === 'object') || 'no Pokémon', [80, d => !!(d.mons && typeof d.mons === 'object')]],
  ['meta_ingame.json', 'op.gg in-game rankings (paused Nov 18: op.gg terms)', 9999, d => d.updated, [1e3, 1e6], d => arr(d.top, 10) || 'fewer than 10 Pokémon'],
  ['meta_ladder.json', 'Smogon monthly ladder stats', 40, d => d.month || (inLaunch ? d.updated || (RG && RG.checked) || todayIso : ''), [5e3, 2e6], d => arr(d.top, 10) || 'fewer than 10 Pokémon', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.top)]],
  ['events.json', 'Events', 3, d => d.updated, [10e3, 5e6], d => arr(d.events, 5) || 'fewer than 5 events', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.events)]],
  ['official.json', 'Official events (Victory Road + VR Pastes, top 64)', 3, d => d.updated, [1e3, 5e6], d => Array.isArray(d.events) || 'no event list', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.events)]],
  ['perf.json', 'Ratings inputs', 2, d => d.updated, [5e3, 2e6], d => !!(d.tour && d.adj) || 'no Limitless table', [20, d => !!(d.reg && d.reg.id === REG)]],
  ['practice.json', 'Team preview practice', 4, d => d.updated, [5e3, 2e6], d => arr(d.rounds, 20) || 'fewer than 20 games', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.rounds)]],
  ['weekly.json', 'This week vs last week', 2, d => d.updated, [200, 1e6], d => d.v === 1 || 'unknown version', [20, d => d.v === 1]],
  ['calendar.json', 'Event calendar', 3, d => d.checked || d.updated, [200, 1e6], d => Array.isArray(d.ev) || 'no event list'],
  ['track.json', 'Ratings track record', 2, d => d.updated, [200, 1e6], d => d.v === 1 || 'unknown version', [20, d => d.v === 1]],
  ['sources.json', 'Outside sources', 2, d => d.updated, [200, 2e6], () => true, [20, () => true]],
  ['crosscheck.json', 'Cross-check', 2, d => d.updated, [200, 2e6], d => Array.isArray(d.checks) || 'no checks', [20, d => Array.isArray(d.checks)]],
  ['ratings_log.json', 'Ratings snapshots', 2, d => d.updated, [200, 5e6], d => arr(rlDays(d)) || 'no snapshots', [20, d => Array.isArray(rlDays(d))]],
  ['scoreboard.json', 'Ratings scoreboard', 3, d => d.updated, [200, 2e6], () => true, [20, () => true]]];
for (const [file, label, days, dateOf, [lo, hi], shape, thinRule] of FILES) {
  const n = size(file), d = n < 0 ? null : rd(file); let date = '', status = 'ok', why = '';
  if (n < 0) { status = 'missing'; why = 'file missing'; }
  else if (!d) { status = 'broken'; why = 'does not parse as JSON'; }
  else { try { date = String(dateOf(d) || ''); } catch (e) {} let s; try { s = shape(d); } catch (e) { s = 'unexpected shape'; }
    const st = stampOf(d);
    if (st && st !== REG && file !== 'meta_ingame.json') { status = 'broken'; why = `wrong regulation (${st}, expected ${REG})`; }
    else if (s === true && n >= lo && n <= hi) { /* ok */ }
    else if (inLaunch && thinRule && n >= thinRule[0] && n <= hi && thinRule[1](d) === true) { status = 'thin'; why = `day ${regDay} of ${REG}, data building`; }
    else if (s !== true) { status = 'broken'; why = s; }
    else { status = 'broken'; why = `size ${(n / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB, expected ${(lo / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB – ${(hi / 1048576).toFixed(1)} MB`; } }
  if (status !== 'ok' && status !== 'thin') problems.push(`${label}: ${file} ${why}`);
  else if (!old(label, date, days)) status = 'stale';
  const a = age(date); rows.push({label, file, date: date.slice(0, 10), age: isNaN(a) ? '' : Math.max(0, Math.floor(a)), days, status, why}); }
// official_perf.json (every team and match of the official events) is frozen since RK9 reads stopped on Nov 1: not checked
// for freshness; perf.mjs marks it frozen and the page drops it 28 days after its newest event.
// every part of the ratings inputs, by the date of its own data
const PF = rd('perf.json');
if (PF) { const want = {tour: ['Limitless table', 2], adj: ['Limitless win shares', 2], ingame: ['op.gg order (paused Nov 18)', 9999], official: ['Official events table (frozen)', 28], smogon: ['Smogon 1760+ usage', 40], ladder: ['Showdowntier ladder', 3]};
  for (const [k, [label, days]] of Object.entries(want)) { if (!PF[k]) { if (k === 'official' || inLaunch) {} /* retired Nov 1, or thin in first 14 days */ else problems.push(`Ratings inputs: no ${label} (${k})`); continue; }
    if (k === 'official' && PF[k].frozen) { const a = age(PF[k].asOf || PF[k].updated); if (a > 21) console.log(`::warning::Ratings inputs: the frozen official-events table (newest event ${PF[k].asOf}) leaves the page in ${Math.max(0, Math.ceil(28 - a))} days; no permitted whole-field source yet`); continue; }
    old(`Ratings inputs, ${label}`, PF[k].updated, days); }
  for (const L of (PF.outside && PF.outside.lists) || []) old(`Outside tier list ${L.name}`, L.date, 3);
  if (!PF.outside && !inLaunch) problems.push('Ratings inputs: no outside tier lists'); }
const S = rd('sources.json'); if (S && S.problems && S.problems.length) console.log(`::warning::Outside sources with problems today: ${S.problems.join(' | ')}`);
// the newest tournament should be recent too (catches Limitless returning nothing new while the file still gets a new date)
try { const idx = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'data/index.json'), 'utf8')); const last = Math.max(...Object.values(idx.tournaments).map(t => Date.parse(t.d)));
  if ((now - last) / DAY > 4) problems.push(`Limitless: newest tournament is ${Math.floor((now - last) / DAY)} days old (limit 4)`); } catch (e) { problems.push('Limitless index (data/index.json) unreadable'); }
// the event calendar should list something still to come (catches Victory Road's calendar page changing shape)
{ const C = rd('calendar.json'); if (C && Array.isArray(C.ev)) { const today = new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  if (!C.ev.some(e => String(e.e || e.s || '') >= today)) problems.push('Event calendar: no upcoming events listed'); } }
// cross-check summary
const X = rd('crosscheck.json');
if (X) for (const c of X.checks || []) { if (c.skipped) { console.log(`cross-check ${c.id}: skipped (${c.skipped})`); continue; }
  if (c.id === 'official-vs-liquipedia') { console.log(`cross-check ${c.id}: ${c.flagged} of ${c.rows.length} events with a disagreement${c.flagged ? ' · ' + c.rows.filter(r => r.flags.length).map(r => `${r.n}: ${r.flags.slice(0, 3).join('; ')}`).join(' | ') : ''}`);
    if (c.staplesFlagged.length) console.log(`::warning::Official events: Liquipedia disagrees with Victory Road on placements or dates (${c.staplesFlagged.join(', ')}). See out/crosscheck.json.`); continue; }
  console.log(`cross-check ${c.id}: ${c.flagged} of ${c.rows.length} beyond tolerance${c.staplesFlagged.length ? ` · staples: ${c.staplesFlagged.join(', ')}` : ''}`);
  // infoOnly: two sources measuring different player groups (crosscheck.mjs) can't agree by design: information, not a warning
  if (c.infoOnly) { if (c.staplesFlagged.length) console.log(`cross-check ${c.id} (information, different player groups by design): ${c.staplesFlagged.length} staples differ`); continue; }
  if (c.staplesFlagged.length > 3) console.log(`::warning::Cross-check ${c.id}: ${c.staplesFlagged.length} of the 12 most used Pokémon disagree with ${c.theirs} (${c.staplesFlagged.join(', ')}). Known differences: ${c.known}`); }
// page storage limit (Nov 10): every document the morning refresh writes must fit in 262,144 bytes. split-docs.mjs splits any
// document over 200,000 bytes into two parts; alarm when a part (or an unsplit document) passes 240,000, before the refresh fails.
{ const X = rd('split/index.json');
  if (!X) problems.push('split-docs: out/split/index.json missing (the page-size guard did not run)');
  else { for (const [doc, n] of Object.entries(X.sizes || {})) { const sp = X.split && X.split[doc]; const parts = sp ? sp.parts : [n];
      parts.forEach((b, i) => { if (b > 240000) problems.push(`page storage: ${doc}${sp ? ' part ' + (i + 1) : ''} is ${b} bytes (limit 262,144; needs a third part or less data)`); }); }
    rows.push({label: 'Page storage (' + (Object.keys(X.split || {}).length ? 'split in two: ' + Object.entries(X.split).map(([d, v]) => `${d} ${v.parts.map(b => Math.round(b / 1024) + ' KB').join(' + ')}`).join(', ') : 'every document fits in one part') + ')', file: 'split/index.json', date: String(X.made || '').slice(0, 10), age: 0, days: 2, status: problems.some(p => p.startsWith('page storage')) ? 'broken' : 'ok', why: ''}); } }
// steps skipped two runs in a row (Nov 7): skip.mjs records every skipped step in out/skips.json. One flaky day stays quiet;
// the same step skipped today and yesterday is a step that has stopped working, so it raises the "Daily data problem" issue
// (the ratings snapshot failed quietly on Nov 6 because a skipped step only printed a warning).
{ const SK = rd('skips.json'), today = new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10), yday = new Date(now - DAY).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const on = d => new Set(((SK && SK.runs) || []).filter(r => r.day === d).flatMap(r => r.steps || []));
  const t = on(today), y = on(yday); for (const st of t) if (y.has(st)) problems.push(`Daily job: the "${st}" step was skipped two days in a row (${yday} and ${today}); see the run log for why`); }

const day = new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10), ICON = {ok: '✅', thin: '🟡 thin', stale: '🟠 stale', missing: '❌ missing', broken: '❌ broken'};
const md = [problems.length ? `## ❌ Daily data problem (${day})\n\nThe page keeps showing the last good data and says so on Home and Meta, but these need a look:\n\n${problems.map(p => `- ${p}`).join('\n')}\n`
  : `## ✅ Daily data healthy (${day})\n`,
  '| Source | File | Data date | Days old | Limit | Status |', '|---|---|---|---|---|---|',
  ...rows.map(r => `| ${r.label} | \`${r.file}\` | ${r.date || '—'} | ${r.age === '' ? '—' : r.age} | ${r.days} | ${ICON[r.status]}${r.why ? ' · ' + r.why : ''} |`),
  '', 'Good data from today was committed before this check ran; a broken source keeps its previous file.'].join('\n');
try { if (process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY) fs.appendFileSync(process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY, md + '\n'); } catch (e) {}
try { if (process.env.HEALTH_REPORT) fs.writeFileSync(process.env.HEALTH_REPORT, md + '\n'); } catch (e) {}
if (!process.env.GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY) console.log('\n' + md + '\n');
if (problems.length) { for (const p of problems) console.log(`::error::${p}`); process.exit(1); }
console.log('data health: all sources current');

===== FILE: perf.mjs (claude/design6)
// The ratings' outside inputs, in one file the morning refresh copies whole into the page's meta/perf:
//   tour     Limitless tournament usage and game win rate per Pokémon (update.mjs, limitless.json "tour")
//   adj      Limitless: each Pokémon's own share of team wins (update.mjs, limitless.json "adj")
//   ingame   op.gg's in-game Ranked Doubles order (opgg.mjs, meta_ingame.json)
//   official official events, every Masters team and match: frozen. Read from RK9 (rk9-field.mjs, out/official_perf.json)
//            until Nov 1, when RK9 reads stopped (its terms forbid automated extraction). No permitted source gives every
//            team and match yet (Victory Road lists the top ~128 with Swiss records, not the matches), so the last values
//            are kept as they are, marked frozen with the date of their newest event; the page shows them for 28 days
//            after that event (16-outside-performance-data.js), then leaves them out.
//   smogon   Smogon usage stats, 1760+ ladder, latest month (ladder.mjs, meta_ladder.json "usage1760")
//   ladder   Showdowntier: Showdown ladder usage, win rate and average rating, last 14 days (sources.mjs)
//   outside  other sites' tier lists for each Pokémon's sheet: Showdowntier (sources.mjs; Pokémon Zone refuses automated reads)
//   reg      the regulation the numbers come from and any newer one being played (reg.mjs: out/reg.json)
// Every part carries the date of its data ("updated", YYYY-MM-DD; a month for Smogon) and its label says it, so the page
// can show the date and leave out anything older than its cadence. A part whose source failed today is left out.
// Usage: node perf.mjs   (after update.mjs, ladder.mjs, opgg.mjs and sources.mjs)
import fs from 'node:fs';
const rd = f => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('out/' + f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return null; } };
const day = t => String(t || '').slice(0, 10), today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const L = rd('limitless.json'), G = rd('meta_ingame.json'), O = rd('official_perf.json'), Lad = rd('meta_ladder.json'), S = rd('sources.json') || {}, out = {updated: today};
if (L && L.tour && Array.isArray(L.tour.rows) && L.tour.rows.length >= 50) out.tour = Object.assign({}, L.tour, {updated: L.tour.updated || day(L.updated)});
else console.log('::warning::perf: no tournament table in limitless.json; tour left out');
if (L && L.adj && Array.isArray(L.adj.rows) && L.adj.rows.length >= 50) out.adj = Object.assign({}, L.adj, {updated: day(L.updated)});
if (G && Array.isArray(G.top)) { const rows = [...G.top, ...(G.more || [])].sort((a, b) => a.r - b.r), d = day(G.updated);
  if (rows.length >= 100) out.ingame = {label: `${G.source}${d ? ` · ${d}` : ''} (all ${rows.length} Pokémon ranked)`, names: rows.map(x => x.n), season: G.period || '', updated: d};
  else console.log(`::warning::perf: in-game order has only ${rows.length} Pokémon; ingame left out`); }
/* Nov 1: RK9's terms forbid automated extraction, so the whole-field table read from RK9 is no longer shown (set
   SHOW_RK9_FROZEN=1 to bring it back). Ratings don't read it, so no rating changes. */
if (process.env.SHOW_RK9_FROZEN === '1' && O && Array.isArray(O.rows) && O.rows.length >= 30 && O.teams >= 300) { const evs = O.events || [], ds = evs.map(e => e.date).filter(Boolean).sort(), asOf = ds[ds.length - 1] || day(O.updated);
  const M = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'], md = d => `${M[+d.slice(5, 7) - 1]} ${+d.slice(8, 10)}`;
  out.official = {label: `RK9 (read before Nov 1, now frozen) · ${evs.length} official VGC events (${ds.length ? `${md(ds[0])} – ${md(asOf)}` : ''}), all ${Number(O.teams).toLocaleString('en-US')} Masters teams, ${Number(O.matches).toLocaleString('en-US')} matches`,
    rows: O.rows, megas: [], split: true, teams: O.teams, matches: O.matches, events: evs, frozen: true, asOf, updated: asOf}; }
else console.log('perf: official-events table retired (RK9, Nov 1); left out as planned');
if (Lad && Lad.usage1760 && Array.isArray(Lad.usage1760.rows) && Lad.usage1760.rows.length >= 30) out.smogon = {label: `Smogon usage stats · 1760+ rating, ${Lad.usage1760.month} (${Number(Lad.usage1760.battles || 0).toLocaleString('en-US')} battles)`, rows: Lad.usage1760.rows, updated: Lad.usage1760.month};
else console.log('::warning::perf: no Smogon 1760+ usage (meta_ladder.json usage1760); smogon left out');
if (S.sdt) out.ladder = {label: `Showdowntier · ${Number(S.sdt.battles || 0).toLocaleString('en-US')} ladder battles, ${S.sdt.from} – ${S.sdt.to}`, rows: S.sdt.rows.map(r => [r.n, r.u, r.w, r.r]), updated: S.sdt.date || S.sdt.to};

// (Charizard -> Charizard-Mega-Y), as before. A site's own Mega list is filed under each Mega.
const tourU = {}; if (L && L.tour) for (const r of [...(L.tour.rows || []), ...(L.tour.megas || [])]) tourU[r[0]] = r[1];
const ROSTER = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('roster.json', 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return []; } })();
const famOf = n => { const b = n === 'Floette-Eternal' ? 'Floette' : n; return [n, ...ROSTER.filter(x => x.startsWith(b + '-Mega'))]; };
function tierList(k, name, how, url, note, src, megaSrc) { if (!src) return null;
  const order = [...new Set([...src.rows, ...((megaSrc && megaSrc.rows) || [])].map(r => r.tier).filter(Boolean))];
  if (!order.length) return null; const idx = t => order.length - order.indexOf(t), data = {};
  for (const r of (megaSrc && megaSrc.rows) || []) if (r.tier && !data[r.n]) data[r.n] = idx(r.tier);
  for (const r of src.rows) { if (!r.tier) continue; const fam = famOf(r.n), best = fam.slice().sort((a, b) => (tourU[b] || 0) - (tourU[a] || 0))[0];
    const key = data[best] == null ? best : r.n; if (data[key] == null) data[key] = idx(r.tier); }
  return {list: {k, name, how, url, note, date: src.date || today, tiers: Object.fromEntries(order.map(t => [idx(t), t]))}, data}; }
const T = [tierList('showdowntier', 'Showdowntier', 'Showdown ladder usage + win rate', 'https://showdowntier.com/formats/dmc/index.html', 'lists Pokémon with 1%+ ladder usage', S.sdt)].filter(Boolean);
if (T.length) out.outside = {updated: today, lists: T.map(t => t.list), data: Object.fromEntries(T.map(t => [t.list.k, t.data]))};
// the regulation every number here is built from, and a newer one being played (reg.mjs, formats.mjs): the page's banner
{ const R = rd('reg.json'); if (R && typeof R.id === 'string') out.reg = {id: R.id, name: R.name, since: R.since, site: R.site, now: R.now || null, switched: R.switched || null, seen: R.seen || {}, checked: R.checked}; }
fs.writeFileSync('out/perf.json', JSON.stringify(out));
console.log(`perf: ${['tour', 'adj', 'ingame', 'official', 'smogon', 'ladder'].map(k => `${k} ${out[k] ? (out[k].rows || out[k].names).length + ' (' + out[k].updated + ')' : 'none'}`).join(', ')}, outside ${T.map(t => t.list.k + ' ' + Object.keys(t.data).length).join('/') || 'none'}`);

===== FILE: tour-modes.mjs (claude/design6)
// Round format per event (Sim, Nov 17: "single games first"; data helpers' finding: 88% of the games the Strength Score learns
// from are best-of-3 rounds and the format of every event since Nov 9 was unknown). For each Limitless event we hold, the daily job
// asks /tournaments/{id}/details once and keeps its phases as [type, rounds, mode] in data/tour-modes.json, e.g.
// [["SWISS",6,"BO1"],["SINGLE_BRACKET",1,"BO3"]]. Structural only: no results are read here.
// updateModes(index, get, opts) -> {modes, added, tried}; `get` is update.mjs's polite Limitless fetch.
export function phasesOf(details) {
  const P = details && Array.isArray(details.phases) ? details.phases : null;
  if (!P || !P.length) return null;
  const out = P.slice().sort((a, b) => (+a.phase || 0) - (+b.phase || 0)).map(p => [String(p.type || ''), +p.rounds || 0, String(p.mode || '').toUpperCase()]);
  return out.every(p => /^BO\d$/.test(p[2]) && p[1] > 0) ? out : null; // only complete answers are kept; a partial one is asked again next day
}
// Safety (review Nov 17): the step must never cost the daily job its run. It stops at `budgetMs` of its own time, and an error
// marked {stop: true} (e.g. rate-limited) ends it for the day; everything not asked waits for the next run.
export async function updateModes(index, get, {modes = {}, max = 40, now = Date.now(), log = () => {}, budgetMs = 20 * 60e3, clock = () => Date.now()} = {}) {
  const t0 = clock();
  const T = (index && index.tournaments) || {};
  // newest first, only events that have started (their date is past), only those without a stored format
  const need = Object.keys(T).filter(id => !modes[id] && Date.parse(T[id].d) <= now).sort((a, b) => Date.parse(T[b].d) - Date.parse(T[a].d)).slice(0, max);
  let added = 0;
  for (const id of need) {
    if (clock() - t0 > budgetMs) { log(`round formats: time budget used, ${need.length - need.indexOf(id)} left for the next run`); break; }
    try { const ph = phasesOf(await get(`/tournaments/${id}/details`)); if (ph) { modes[id] = ph; added++; } else log(`no round format yet for ${id}`); }
    catch (e) { log(`no details for ${id}: ${e.message}`); if (e && e.stop) { log('round formats: stopped for today'); break; } }
  }
  return {modes, added, tried: need.length};
}

===== FILE: events.mjs (claude/design6)
// Tournament pages (step 5): out/events.json, one entry per recent event with its top finishers' full team sheets.
// Limitless events with 32+ players from the last 30 days (top 8 each) and official events (top 16 each, Victory Road +
// VR Pastes via official.mjs). Compact on purpose: the page keeps it in one document (256 KiB cap). Run after update.mjs and official.mjs.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {offFormat, oddEvents, activeReg, sameReg, inRegWindow} from './formats.mjs';
import {stampReg} from './reg-stamp.mjs';
import {lkey, lmap, reportUnknown} from './names.mjs';
const RG = activeReg(); // one regulation only (formats.mjs)
const readJSON = (f, d) => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return d; } };
const ROSTER = new Set(readJSON('roster.json', []));
const DAY = 864e5, LIM_TOP = +(process.env.EV_TOP || 8), OFF_TOP = 16, DAYS = 30, MIN_PLAYERS = 32, CAP = 240 * 1024;
// Limitless names -> NastyPlot names: the same rules as update.mjs (key() reads the Mega from the item, lmap() the form)
const REG = {Hisuian: 'Hisui', Alolan: 'Alola', Galarian: 'Galar', Paldean: 'Paldea'}, names = ROSTER;
const key = lkey; // shared (names.mjs)
/* lmap() now lives in names.mjs (shared) */
// the event's own calendar day: Limitless times are UTC, and evening events in the Americas start after midnight UTC
// (sweep, Oct 31: 72 of 379 showed the next day). Six hours back puts them on the day they were played.
const evDay = d => { const t = new Date(d).getTime(); return isFinite(t) ? new Date(t - 6 * 3600e3).toISOString().slice(0, 10) : String(d).slice(0, 10); };
const lname = m => lmap(key({name: m.n, item: m.i}));
// every name, item, ability, nature and move is stored once in dict and referenced by its index
const dict = [], dix = new Map(), D = x => { x = String(x || ''); if (!dix.has(x)) { dix.set(x, dict.length); dict.push(x); } return dix.get(x); };
// Nov 16: one Limitless sheet (Nov 14) had a mistyped move, "VoltSwitchFeint"; the page's checks refused it and that stopped every day's
// data from reaching live. Each move is now checked against the site's own move list (app/src/data/data.js MOVES): a known move stays; a
// glued or mistyped one is read as the real move it starts with when that move isn't already on the Pokémon; anything else is left out.
// Every repair is printed in the job log.
const MOVE_NAMES = (() => { try { const s = fs.readFileSync('app/src/data/data.js', 'utf8'), i = s.indexOf('const MOVES'), j = s.indexOf(']];', i);
  return new Set(Function('return ' + s.slice(s.indexOf('[', i), j + 2))().map(m => m[0])); } catch (e) { return null; } })();
const sq = x => String(x).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''), SQ = MOVE_NAMES ? new Map([...MOVE_NAMES].map(n => [sq(n), n])) : null;
const fixMoves = (mv, who) => { if (!MOVE_NAMES || !MOVE_NAMES.size) return mv; const out = [];
  for (const x of mv) { if (MOVE_NAMES.has(x)) { out.push(x); continue; } const k = sq(x); let best = SQ.get(k) || null;
    if (!best) for (const [q, n] of SQ) if (q.length >= 4 && k.startsWith(q) && (!best || q.length > sq(best).length)) best = n;
    if (!best && k.length >= 6) { const c = [...SQ].filter(([q]) => q.startsWith(k)); if (c.length === 1) best = c[0][1]; } /* a dropped last letter: "Last Respect" */
    if (best && !mv.includes(best) && !out.includes(best)) { out.push(best); console.log(`events: ${who}: move "${x}" read as "${best}"`); }
    else console.log(`events: ${who}: move "${x}" left out (not a move${best ? ', and ' + best + ' is already there' : ''})`); }
  return out; };
const sheet = (n, m) => [D(n), D(m.i), D(m.a), D(m.nt), fixMoves((m.mv || []).slice(0, 4), n).map(D)];
const out = [];
// official events first (biggest and most watched)
const OFF = readJSON('out/official.json', {events: []}), offSrc = /^RK9/.test(OFF.source || '') ? 'RK9 official events' : 'Victory Road official events';
for (const e of (OFF.events || [])) { if (!inRegWindow(e.date, RG)) continue;
  const top = e.teams.filter(t => t.st <= OFF_TOP && t.mons.length === 6 && t.mons.every(m => ROSTER.has(m.n))).sort((a, b) => a.st - b.st)
    .map(t => [t.player, t.st, '', t.mons.map(m => sheet(m.n, m))]);
  if (top.length) out.push({id: 'off-' + e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''), name: e.name, date: e.date, players: e.players, src: 'official', top}); }
// Limitless
const index = readJSON('data/index.json', {tournaments: {}});
const ids0 = Object.keys(index.tournaments).filter(id => sameReg(index.tournaments[id], RG.id) && !offFormat(index.tournaments[id].name)), odd = oddEvents(ids0, id => readJSON(`data/t/${id}.json`, null), () => {});
const ids = ids0.filter(id => !odd.has(id)); // standard Reg M-C only (formats.mjs: by name, and by the staples alarm)
const lastD = Math.max(...ids.map(id => new Date(index.tournaments[id].d).getTime()).filter(x => x > 0), 0);
for (const id of ids) { const T = readJSON(`data/t2/${id}.json`, null); if (!T || (T.p || 0) < MIN_PLAYERS || lastD - new Date(T.d) > DAYS * DAY) continue;
  const top = T.e.filter(p => p.pl != null && p.pl <= LIM_TOP && p.dk.length === 6).sort((a, b) => a.pl - b.pl).map(p => { const ns = p.dk.map(lname);
    return ns.every(Boolean) ? [p.nm, p.pl, `${p.w}-${p.l}${p.ti ? '-' + p.ti : ''}`, p.dk.map((m, i) => sheet(ns[i], m))] : null; }).filter(Boolean);
  // what the whole field brought: the 8 most common Pokémon among every full team
  const c = new Map(); let n = 0;
  for (const p of T.e) { if (p.dk.length !== 6) continue; const ns = p.dk.map(lname); if (!ns.every(Boolean)) continue; n++; for (const x of new Set(ns)) c.set(x, (c.get(x) || 0) + 1); }
  const field = [...c].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8).map(([x, k]) => [D(x), Math.round(1000 * k / n) / 10]);
  if (top.length >= 4) out.push({id, name: T.name, date: evDay(T.d), players: T.p, src: 'limitless', top, field}); }
out.sort((a, b) => (b.date > a.date ? 1 : b.date < a.date ? -1 : b.players - a.players));
// keep under the document cap: drop the oldest small events first
console.log("qualifying", out.length);
let doc = {updated: new Date().toISOString(), source: `Limitless (${MIN_PLAYERS}+ players, last ${DAYS} days, top ${LIM_TOP}) + ${offSrc} (top ${OFF_TOP})`, dict, events: out};
while (Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(doc)) > CAP && doc.events.length > 10) { let k = -1;
  for (let i = doc.events.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (doc.events[i].src !== 'official') { if (k < 0 || doc.events[i].players < doc.events[k].players) k = i; if (doc.events.length - i > 15) break; }
  doc.events.splice(k, 1); }
// say how far back the kept events really go (the cap can trim the oldest ones)
{ const lim = doc.events.filter(e => e.src !== 'official'); if (lim.length) { const old = lim.reduce((a, e) => e.date < a ? e.date : a, lim[0].date), days = Math.round((lastD - new Date(old + 'T12:00:00Z')) / DAY) + 1;
  if (days < DAYS) doc.source = doc.source.replace(`last ${DAYS} days`, `last ${days} days`); } }
// fail-safe (stress test, Oct 28): never replace yesterday's events with far fewer (a broken index or a bad day of data)
{ const prev = readJSON('out/events.json', null), n0 = prev && Array.isArray(prev.events) ? prev.events.length : 0;
  if ((!prev || (prev.reg || 'M-C') === RG.id) && n0 >= 10 && doc.events.length < n0 * .5) { console.log(`::error::events: only ${doc.events.length} events (had ${n0}); kept the existing file`); process.exit(1); } }
fs.writeFileSync('out/events.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg(doc, RG.id)));
console.log(`events: ${doc.events.length} (${doc.events.filter(e => e.src === 'official').length} official), ${Math.round(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(doc)) / 1024)} KiB`);

reportUnknown('events');

===== FILE: official.mjs (claude/design6)
// Official events (step 4): open team sheets of finished official VGC events (Regionals, Internationals, Specials, Worlds)
// in the current regulation, Masters division only. Replaces rk9.mjs (RK9's terms, rk9.gg/terms 5.3, forbid automated
// data extraction). Sources, each read politely (our User-Agent, at most one request a second per site, cached):
//   Victory Road   victoryroad.pro, WordPress REST API: the event page lists Masters standings (placement, player,
//                  country, the six Pokémon) and links each team's open team sheet on VR Pastes
//   VR Pastes      vrpastes.com, Victory Road's paste site (robots.txt: Allow /, crawl-delay 1). Its pages fill in the team
//                  in the browser from the site's own JSON backend (vrpaste-backend.vercel.app/api/paste/<id>, no
//                  robots.txt), so that JSON is read, one paste a second: species, item, ability, nature, moves
//                  (open team sheets hide stat points). The server-rendered page is the fallback when it has the team.
//   Liquipedia     liquipedia.net/pokemon, MediaWiki API only (one batched query a run, 2 s apart, never HTML pages):
//                  checks placements, player count and start date. Text CC-BY-SA 3.0 (attribution in the output).
// Writes out/official.json (same shape rk9.mjs wrote: events[{name, date, players, teams[{player, st, mons[{n,i,a,nt,mv}]}]}]),
// puts each event's top 8 at the front of out/teams.json, keeps data/official/ (one file per event; pastes are read once),
// and out/official_compare.json (every run's check of the new output against the previous out/official.json).
// Fail-safe: if the new output disagrees with the previous file beyond the gate, or shrinks, the previous file is kept.
//   OFFICIAL_TOP    teams kept per event (default 64, as rk9.mjs)
//   OFFICIAL_CACHE  replay folder: answer requests from <dir>/<sha1(url)>.txt and never touch the network (tests)
import fs from 'node:fs'; import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {readJSON, dec, ROSTER, mapMon, validItem, bad} from './names.mjs';
import {opid, okeys, keyScore} from './pid.mjs';
import {stampReg} from './reg-stamp.mjs';
export const UA = 'NastyPlot data job (github.com/theresearchmode-glitch/pokelair-data; hello@nastyplot.org)';
const TOP = +(process.env.OFFICIAL_TOP || 64), REG = process.env.OFFICIAL_REG || 'M-C', REG_START = '2026-09-01', CACHE = process.env.OFFICIAL_CACHE || '';
const DIR = 'data/official', VR = 'https://victoryroad.pro/wp-json/wp/v2/pages', LQ = 'https://liquipedia.net/pokemon/api.php';
const GAP = {'victoryroad.pro': 1100, 'www.vrpastes.com': 1100, 'vrpaste-backend.vercel.app': 1100, 'liquipedia.net': 2100}, GAP0 = process.env.GAP != null ? +process.env.GAP : null;  /* GAP=0 only for the offline stress test */
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const last = {}; let calls = 0;
const key = u => crypto.createHash('sha1').update(u).digest('hex').slice(0, 24) + '.txt';
// one request at a time per site, GAP ms apart; 429/5xx wait (Retry-After when given) and retry twice
async function get(url) {
  if (CACHE) { const f = `${CACHE}/${key(url)}`; if (!fs.existsSync(f)) throw new Error('not in cache: ' + url); return fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'); }
  const host = new URL(url).host, wait = (last[host] || 0) + (GAP0 != null ? GAP0 : GAP[host] || 1100) - Date.now(); if (wait > 0) await sleep(wait);
  for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { last[host] = Date.now(); calls++;
    const r = await fetch(url, {headers: {'User-Agent': UA, 'Accept-Encoding': 'gzip'}});
    if (r.ok) return r.text();
    if (r.status === 429 || r.status >= 500) { await sleep(Math.min(120, +(r.headers.get('retry-after') || 30)) * 1000); continue; }
    throw new Error(url + ' ' + r.status); }
  throw new Error(url + ' failed'); }
const strip = h => dec(String(h || '').replace(/<br\s*\/?>/g, ' ').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '')).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
// "2027 Puebla Regional Championships" -> the official name "2027 Puebla Pokémon Regional Championships"
const offName = t => t.replace(/ (Regional|International|Special|World) Championships$/, ' Pokémon $1 Championships');
const MONTHS = ['january', 'february', 'march', 'april', 'may', 'june', 'july', 'august', 'september', 'october', 'november', 'december'];
// "24–25 October 2026", "31 October – 1 November 2026", "7 March 2027" -> first day, YYYY-MM-DD
export function startDate(s) { const m = String(s).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').match(/^(\d{1,2})(?: ([A-Za-z]+))?(?: ?[–-] ?\d{1,2})?(?: ([A-Za-z]+))? (\d{4})/); if (!m) return null;
  const mo = MONTHS.indexOf((m[2] || m[3] || '').toLowerCase()); if (mo < 0) return null;
  let y = +m[4]; if (m[2] && m[3] && mo > MONTHS.indexOf(m[3].toLowerCase())) y--;  // "30 December – 2 January 2027"
  return `${y}-${String(mo + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(+m[1]).padStart(2, '0')}`; }
// A Victory Road event page: infobox facts and the Masters standings (every table before the Seniors/Juniors part)
export function parseEventPage(html) {
  const info = {}; for (const m of html.matchAll(/<tr><th[^>]*>([^<]+)<\/th><th[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/th><\/tr>/g)) info[strip(m[1])] = strip(m[2]);
  const cut = html.search(/<h[1-4][^>]*>[^<]*(?:<[^>]+>)*[^<]*(Senior|Junior)/); const M = cut > 0 ? html.slice(0, cut) : html;
  const rows = new Map();
  for (const t of M.matchAll(/<table class="infobox2[^"]*">([\s\S]*?)<\/table>/g)) {
    const head = strip((t[1].match(/<thead>([\s\S]*?)<\/thead>/) || [])[1]); if (!/Player/.test(head) || !/Team/.test(head) || !/OTS/.test(head)) continue;
    for (const r of t[1].matchAll(/<tr>([\s\S]*?)<\/tr>/g)) { const td = [...r[1].matchAll(/<td[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/td>/g)].map(x => x[1]); if (td.length < 7) continue;
      const st = parseInt(strip(td[0]), 10); if (!(st > 0) || rows.has(st)) continue;
      rows.set(st, {st, rec: strip(td[1]), country: (td[2].match(/title="([^"]+)"/) || [])[1] || '', player: strip(td[3].split(/<br\s*\/?>/)[0]),
        sprites: [...td[5].matchAll(/<img [^>]*title="([^"]+)"[^>]*class="champsprite"/g)].map(x => dec(x[1])), paste: ((td[6].match(/vrpastes\.com\/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)/) || [])[1]) || null}); } }
  const ma = s => { const m = String(s || '').match(/([\d,]+)\s*(?:players\s*)?\(?MA\b/); return m ? +m[1].replace(/,/g, '') : null; };
  return {info, date: startDate(info.Date || ''), masters: ma(info.Attendance), capacity: ma(info.Capacity), game: info.Videogame || '', season: info.Season || '', rows: [...rows.values()].sort((a, b) => a.st - b.st)}; }
// The six Pokémon from the event page's sprite titles ("Charizard Mega Y", "Arcanine Hisui", "Kommo O") as roster names
export function spriteName(t) { const s = String(t).trim(), d = s.replace(/ /g, '-');
  for (const c of [d, d.replace(/-O$/, '-o'), d.replace(/-Mega-?([XYZ])?$/, (_, x) => '-Mega' + (x ? '-' + x : ''))]) if (ROSTER.has(c)) return c;
  const b = s.split(' ')[0]; return ROSTER.has(b) ? b : null; }
const NATURES = new Set(['Hardy', 'Lonely', 'Brave', 'Adamant', 'Naughty', 'Bold', 'Docile', 'Relaxed', 'Impish', 'Lax', 'Timid', 'Hasty', 'Serious', 'Jolly', 'Naive', 'Modest', 'Mild', 'Quiet', 'Bashful', 'Rash', 'Calm', 'Gentle', 'Sassy', 'Careful', 'Quirky']);
// A VR Pastes open team sheet (server-rendered HTML): per Pokémon the item and species images, "Ability:", four moves
// (each after its type icon), the nature. Returns null when the page has no team yet (a client-side loading shell).
export function parsePaste(html) {
  const t = dec(html.replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>|<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/g, '')
    .replace(/<img[^>]*alt="([^"]*)"[^>]*src="[^"]*\/(items|pokemons|pokemon|types)\/[^"]*"[^>]*>/g, '\n\u0001$2:$1\n').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, '\n')).split('\n').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const mons = []; let item = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++) {
    if (t[i].startsWith('\u0001items:')) { item = t[i].slice(7); continue; }
    if (!/^\u0001pokemons?:/.test(t[i])) continue;
    const sp = t[i].replace(/^\u0001pokemons?:/, ''); let j = i + 1, a = '', nt = ''; const mv = [];
    if (t[j] === 'Ability:') { a = t[j + 1] || ''; j += 2; }
    for (; j < t.length && !/^\u0001(pokemons?|items):/.test(t[j]); j++) {
      if (t[j].startsWith('\u0001types:')) { if (mv.length < 4 && t[j + 1] && t[j + 1][0] !== '\u0001') mv.push(t[++j]); continue; }
      if (NATURES.has(t[j]) && !nt) { nt = t[j]; break; } }
    mons.push({sp, it: item, n: mapMon(sp, item), i: validItem(item), a, nt, mv}); item = ''; i = j; }
  const title = strip((html.match(/<title>([^<]*)/) || [])[1]);
  return mons.length === 6 && mons.every(m => m.a && m.mv.length) ? {title, mons} : null; }
// The same sheet from VR Pastes' JSON backend: {teams: [{species, item, ability, nature, moves}]}
export function parsePasteJson(j) { const T = j && Array.isArray(j.teams) ? j.teams : [];
  const mons = T.map(t => ({sp: t.species || t.name, it: t.item || '', n: mapMon(t.species || t.name, t.item || ''), i: validItem(t.item), a: t.ability || '', nt: t.nature || '', mv: (t.moves || []).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 4)}));
  return mons.length === 6 && mons.every(m => m.a && m.mv.length) && !j.is_encrypted ? {title: j.title || '', mons} : null; }
async function readPaste(id) { let X = null;
  try { X = parsePasteJson(JSON.parse(await get(`https://vrpaste-backend.vercel.app/api/paste/${id}?lang=english`))); } catch (e) { console.log(`official: paste ${id} (JSON) ${e.message}`); }
  if (!X) { try { X = parsePaste(await get(`https://www.vrpastes.com/${id}`)); } catch (e) {} }
  return X; }
// ---- Liquipedia: placements, player count and start date (wikitext through the API) ----
const lqTitle = name => { const m = name.match(/^(\d{4}) (.+?) (Regional|International|Special|World) Championships$/); if (!m) return null;
  return `Pokemon_Championships/${m[3] === 'World' ? 'World' : m[3]}/${m[2].replace(/ /g, '_')}/${m[1]}/VGC`; };
export function parseLiquipedia(wt) {
  const g = k => (wt.match(new RegExp(`\\|${k}=([^\\n|]*)`)) || [])[1] || null;
  const place = new Map(), norm = s => s.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
  for (const part of wt.split('{{Slot|place=').slice(1)) { const m = part.match(/^(\d+)(?:-(\d+))?/); if (!m) continue;
    for (const o of part.split(/\{\{Slot\||\n==/)[0].matchAll(/\{\{Opponent\|([^|}]+)/g)) place.set(o[1].trim(), [+m[1], +(m[2] || m[1])]); }
  // top cut from the bracket: the last round is the final; the round before, the semifinals
  const R = {}; for (const m of wt.matchAll(/\|R(\d+)M(\d+)=\{\{Match([\s\S]*?)\n\}\}/g)) { const os = [...m[3].matchAll(/opponent\d=\{\{SoloOpponent\|([^|}]+)[^}]*?score=([^|}]*)/g)].map(x => [x[1].trim(), x[2].trim()]);
    if (os.length === 2) (R[+m[1]] = R[+m[1]] || []).push(os); }
  const win = ([a, b]) => { const s = x => x[1] === 'w' ? 1e3 : x[1] === 'l' ? -1 : +x[1] || 0; return s(a) > s(b) ? [a[0], b[0]] : s(b) > s(a) ? [b[0], a[0]] : null; };
  const rs = Object.keys(R).map(Number).sort((a, b) => b - a);
  if (rs.length >= 2 && R[rs[0]].length === 1) { const f = win(R[rs[0]][0]); if (f) { place.set(f[0], [1, 1]); place.set(f[1], [2, 2]); }
    if (R[rs[1]].length === 2) for (const m of R[rs[1]]) { const w = win(m); if (w) place.set(w[1], [3, 4]); }
    if (rs.length >= 3 && R[rs[2]].length === 4) for (const m of R[rs[2]]) { const w = win(m); if (w && !place.has(w[1])) place.set(w[1], [5, 8]); } }
  return {players: g('player_number') ? +g('player_number') : null, sdate: g('sdate'), place}; }
// ---- the run ----
const nkey = s => String(s || '').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
// The same person written two ways (pid.mjs keyScore): player names are never stored (Sim, Nov 10), only an ID and keyed
// versions of the name's words, which still tell two spellings of one person apart from two people. Used only next to
// another key (same placement, or best of a list). a and b: a name (in memory, straight from a download) or stored keys.
export const personScore = (a, b) => keyScore(a, b);
export const samePerson = (a, b) => personScore(a, b) > 0;
// the best match among [keys, value] pairs (highest score), or undefined
export const findPerson = (pairs, name, min = 1) => { let best, bs = min - 1; for (const [k, v] of pairs) { const sc = personScore(k, name); if (sc > bs) { bs = sc; best = v; } } return best; };
// Liquipedia's page reduced to what the checks use, with names turned into keys (the page text itself is not kept)
const lqStore = Q => ({players: Q.players, sdate: Q.sdate, place: [...Q.place].map(([n, r]) => [okeys(n), r])});
async function run() {
  fs.mkdirSync(DIR, {recursive: true});
  const index = readJSON(`${DIR}/index.json`, {events: {}});
  const now = new Date(), season = now.getUTCMonth() >= 8 ? now.getUTCFullYear() + 1 : now.getUTCFullYear();
  // 1) the season's event pages (one request); only new or edited pages are read again
  let list; try { list = JSON.parse(await get(`${VR}?search=${season}&per_page=100&_fields=id,slug,modified,title`)); if (!Array.isArray(list)) throw new Error('not a page list'); }
  catch (e) { console.log(`::warning::official: Victory Road page list unavailable (${e.message}); kept the previous out/official.json`); return; }
  const pages = list.map(p => ({slug: String(p.slug || ''), modified: p.modified, title: strip(p.title && p.title.rendered)})).filter(p => /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,80}$/.test(p.slug) /* a slug becomes a file name (Kip job 36 A #3) */ && /^\d{4} .+ (Regional|International|Special|World) Championships$/.test(p.title));
  for (const p of pages) { const E = index.events[p.slug];
    if (E && E.modified === p.modified && (E.done || E.skip || !E.failed)) continue;  /* unchanged page: read again only to retry failed pastes */
    try { const j = JSON.parse(await get(`${VR}?slug=${p.slug}&_fields=id,slug,modified,title,content`))[0]; if (!j) continue;
      const P = parseEventPage(j.content.rendered);
      const base = {title: p.title, name: offName(p.title), modified: p.modified, date: P.date, players: P.masters};
      if (!/Champions/.test(P.game) || !new RegExp(`Regulation Set ${REG}\\b`).test(P.season)) { index.events[p.slug] = {...base, skip: `not Pokémon Champions Reg ${REG}`}; continue; }
      if (!P.date || P.date < REG_START) { index.events[p.slug] = {...base, skip: 'before Reg ' + REG}; continue; }
      const rows = P.rows.filter(r => r.st <= TOP);
      if (rows.length < Math.min(TOP, 16) || !rows.every(r => r.paste)) { index.events[p.slug] = {...base, done: false, note: `no Masters standings with team sheets yet (${rows.length} rows)`}; continue; }
      // pastes: read once each; a team keeps its parsed sheet while its paste link is unchanged
      const prev = readJSON(`${DIR}/${p.slug}.json`, {teams: []}), had = new Map(prev.teams.map(t => [t.paste, t]));
      const teams = []; let failed = 0;
      for (const r of rows) { let T = had.get(r.paste);
        if (!T) { const X = await readPaste(r.paste); if (X) T = {mons: X.mons}; else console.log(`official: paste ${r.paste} (${p.slug} #${r.st}) not readable yet`); }
        if (!T) { failed++; continue; }
        teams.push({st: r.st, player: opid(r.player), pk: okeys(r.player), rec: r.rec, paste: r.paste, sprites: r.sprites.map(spriteName), mons: T.mons}); }
      fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/${p.slug}.json`, JSON.stringify({slug: p.slug, name: base.name, date: P.date, players: P.masters, capacity: P.capacity, teams}));
      index.events[p.slug] = {...base, done: !failed, teams: teams.length, failed};
      console.log(`official: ${base.name} (${P.date}), ${P.masters} Masters, ${teams.length}/${rows.length} team sheets${failed ? `, ${failed} to retry next run` : ''}`);
    } catch (e) { console.log(`official: ${p.slug} skipped: ${e.message}`); } }
  fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/index.json`, JSON.stringify(index));
  // 2) Liquipedia check, one batched API query for the events not yet confirmed (an event is re-checked for 14 days)
  const evSlugs = Object.keys(index.events).filter(s => index.events[s].teams && !index.events[s].skip);
  const lq = readJSON(`${DIR}/liquipedia.json`, {});
  const want = evSlugs.filter(s => { const L = lq[s]; return !L || (!L.missing && (Date.now() - Date.parse(index.events[s].date + 'T12:00:00Z')) / 864e5 < 14 && L.fetched !== now.toISOString().slice(0, 10)); });
  const titles = new Map(want.map(s => [lqTitle(index.events[s].title), s]).filter(x => x[0]));
  if (titles.size) { try {
    const url = `${LQ}?action=query&prop=revisions&rvprop=content|timestamp&rvslots=main&format=json&formatversion=2&redirects=1&titles=${[...titles.keys()].join('|')}`;
    const J = JSON.parse(await get(url)), norm = t => t.replace(/ /g, '_');
    const back = new Map(((J.query && J.query.redirects) || []).map(r => [norm(r.to), norm(r.from)]));
    for (const pg of (J.query && J.query.pages) || []) { const t = norm(pg.title), s = titles.get(t) || titles.get(back.get(t)); if (!s) continue;
      lq[s] = pg.missing ? {missing: true, title: t} : {title: t, rev: pg.revisions[0].timestamp, fetched: now.toISOString().slice(0, 10), q: lqStore(parseLiquipedia(pg.revisions[0].slots.main.content))}; }
    fs.writeFileSync(`${DIR}/liquipedia.json`, JSON.stringify(lq));
  } catch (e) { console.log(`official: Liquipedia check skipped: ${e.message}`); } }
  // 3) the output, newest first, and its checks
  const evs = evSlugs.map(s => ({s, E: readJSON(`${DIR}/${s}.json`, null)})).filter(x => x.E && x.E.teams.length).sort((a, b) => b.E.date < a.E.date ? -1 : 1);
  const checks = [];
  // players: Victory Road's attendance; when its page gives none, Liquipedia's player count, else the venue's Masters capacity
  for (const {s, E} of evs) { const L = lq[s], Q = L && L.q ? L.q : L && L.wt ? lqStore(parseLiquipedia(L.wt)) : null;
    E.playersFrom = E.players != null ? 'Victory Road' : Q && Q.players != null ? 'Liquipedia' : E.capacity != null ? 'Victory Road (capacity)' : '';
    if (E.players == null) E.players = Q && Q.players != null ? Q.players : E.capacity != null ? E.capacity : null;
    const c = {event: E.name, date: E.date, players: E.players, playersFrom: E.playersFrom, teams: E.teams.length, flags: []};
    for (const t of E.teams) { const a = t.mons.map(m => m.n).sort().join(), b = t.sprites.slice().sort().join(); if (a !== b) c.flags.push(`#${t.st}: sheet ${a} vs page ${b}`); }
    if (!L || L.missing || !Q) c.liquipedia = L && L.missing ? 'no Liquipedia page' : 'not checked';
    else { c.liquipedia = {title: L.title, rev: L.rev, players: Q.players, sdate: Q.sdate, placed: 0, agree: 0};
      // player counts differ by a few between sites (late drops, who counts a no-show): a note within 1% (at least 3), a flag beyond
      if (Q.players != null && E.playersFrom === 'Victory Road' && Q.players !== E.players) (Math.abs(Q.players - E.players) > Math.max(3, .01 * E.players) ? c.flags : (c.notes = c.notes || [])).push(`players: Victory Road ${E.players}, Liquipedia ${Q.players}`);
      if (Q.sdate && Q.sdate !== E.date) c.flags.push(`start date: Victory Road ${E.date}, Liquipedia ${Q.sdate}`);
      for (const t of E.teams) { const r = findPerson(Q.place, t.pk || t.player, 2); if (!r) continue;  /* across a whole list, no loose (score 1) matches */ c.liquipedia.placed++; if (t.st >= r[0] && t.st <= r[1]) c.liquipedia.agree++; else c.flags.push(`#${t.st}: Liquipedia places ${r[0] === r[1] ? r[0] : r.join('–')}`); } }
    checks.push(c); }
  const out = {updated: now.toISOString(), source: `Victory Road + VR Pastes · official VGC events (Masters top ${TOP}) · checked against Liquipedia`,
    attribution: 'Standings and team sheets: Victory Road (victoryroad.pro, vrpastes.com). Placement check: Liquipedia (liquipedia.net), CC-BY-SA 3.0.',
    events: evs.map(({E}) => ({name: E.name, date: E.date, players: E.players, teams: E.teams.map(t => ({player: opid(t.player), pk: okeys(t.pk || t.player), st: t.st, mons: t.mons.map(m => ({n: m.n || m.sp, i: m.i, a: m.a, nt: m.nt, mv: m.mv}))}))})),
    checks};
  // 4) gate: the new file against the previous out/official.json, team by team (same event date and placement)
  const prev = readJSON('out/official.json', null), cmp = compare(prev, out);
  fs.writeFileSync('out/official_compare.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg(cmp, REG)));
  console.log(`official: vs previous file (${cmp.previous}): ${cmp.summary}`);
  const prevTeams = prev ? (prev.events || []).reduce((a, e) => a + (e.teams || []).length, 0) : 0, newTeams = out.events.reduce((a, e) => a + e.teams.length, 0);
  if (!out.events.length) { console.log('::warning::official: no events read; kept the previous out/official.json'); return; }
  if (prevTeams && newTeams < prevTeams * .8) { console.log(`::warning::official: only ${newTeams} teams (had ${prevTeams}); kept the previous out/official.json`); return; }
  if (!cmp.pass) { console.log(`::warning::official: the new sheets disagree with the previous file beyond the gate (${cmp.summary}); kept the previous out/official.json`); return; }
  fs.writeFileSync('out/official.json', JSON.stringify(stampReg(out, REG)));
  // each event's top 8 at the front of out/teams.json (as rk9.mjs did)
  const T = readJSON('out/teams.json', {teams: []}), off = [];
  for (const e of out.events.slice(0, 4)) for (const t of e.teams.filter(t => t.st <= 8 && t.mons.every(m => ROSTER.has(m.n)))) off.push({player: t.player, event: `${e.name} · ${e.players} players`, record: `#${t.st} · official`, section: 'Official events', mons: t.mons.map(m => m.n)});
  T.teams = [...off, ...(T.teams || []).filter(t => t.section !== 'Official events')].slice(0, 90);
  T.source = (T.source || '').replace(/ \+ (RK9|Victory Road) official events.*$/, '') + (off.length ? ' + Victory Road official events (top 8)' : '');
  fs.writeFileSync('out/teams.json', JSON.stringify(T));
  const flagged = checks.filter(c => c.flags.length);
  console.log(`official: ${out.events.length} events, ${newTeams} teams, ${calls} requests${bad.items ? `, ${bad.items} items not on the Reg ${REG} list left blank` : ''}; checks: ${flagged.length ? flagged.map(c => `${c.event}: ${c.flags.length} (${c.flags.slice(0, 2).join('; ')})`).join(' | ') : 'all agree'}`);
  if (flagged.some(c => c.flags.some(f => /Liquipedia places|players:|start date/.test(f)))) console.log('::warning::official: Liquipedia disagrees with Victory Road on some placements, player counts or dates (see out/official.json checks)'); }
// Per team (matched by event date + placement): player (by ID and name keys), six Pokémon, and each Pokémon's item, ability, nature, moves.
export function compare(A, B) {
  const r = {previous: A ? A.source || 'unknown' : 'none', events: [], pass: true};
  if (!A) { r.summary = 'no previous file'; return r; }
  const tot = {teams: 0, player: 0, six: 0, mons: 0, item: 0, ability: 0, nature: 0, moves: 0};
  for (const b of B.events) { const a = (A.events || []).find(e => e.date === b.date && nkey(e.name.replace(/Pokémon /, '')) === nkey(b.name.replace(/Pokémon /, ''))); if (!a) continue;
    const E = {event: b.name, players: [a.players, b.players], teams: 0, player: 0, six: 0, mons: 0, item: 0, ability: 0, nature: 0, moves: 0, examples: []};
    const byS = new Map(a.teams.map(t => [t.st, t]));
    for (const t of b.teams) { const o = byS.get(t.st); if (!o) continue; E.teams++;
      if (samePerson(o.pk || o.player, t.pk || t.player)) E.player++; else if (E.examples.length < 8) E.examples.push(`#${t.st} player: a different player`);
      const six = o.mons.map(m => m.n).sort().join() === t.mons.map(m => m.n).sort().join(); if (six) E.six++; else if (E.examples.length < 8) E.examples.push(`#${t.st} six: ${o.mons.map(m => m.n).join('/')} vs ${t.mons.map(m => m.n).join('/')}`);
      for (const m of t.mons) { const p = o.mons.find(x => x.n === m.n); if (!p) continue; E.mons++;
        for (const [k, f] of [['item', 'i'], ['ability', 'a'], ['nature', 'nt']]) { if ((p[f] || '') === (m[f] || '')) E[k]++; else if (E.examples.length < 8) E.examples.push(`#${t.st} ${m.n} ${k}: ${p[f] || '—'} vs ${m[f] || '—'}`); }
        if ((p.mv || []).slice().sort().join() === (m.mv || []).slice().sort().join()) E.moves++; else if (E.examples.length < 8) E.examples.push(`#${t.st} ${m.n} moves: ${(p.mv || []).join('/')} vs ${(m.mv || []).join('/')}`); } }
    for (const k in tot) tot[k] += E[k]; r.events.push(E); }
  r.total = tot; const pc = (x, n) => n ? +(100 * x / n).toFixed(1) : null;
  r.rates = {player: pc(tot.player, tot.teams), six: pc(tot.six, tot.teams), item: pc(tot.item, tot.mons), ability: pc(tot.ability, tot.mons), nature: pc(tot.nature, tot.mons), moves: pc(tot.moves, tot.mons)};
  // the gate: 95%+ of placements name the same player and 98%+ of teams hold the same six; sets 97%+ the same
  r.pass = !tot.teams || (r.rates.player >= 95 && r.rates.six >= 98 && ['item', 'ability', 'nature', 'moves'].every(k => r.rates[k] >= 97));
  r.summary = tot.teams ? `${tot.teams} teams in ${r.events.length} events: player ${r.rates.player}%, six Pokémon ${r.rates.six}%, items ${r.rates.item}%, abilities ${r.rates.ability}%, natures ${r.rates.nature}%, moves ${r.rates.moves}% (${tot.mons} Pokémon)` : 'no overlapping events';
  return r; }
if (process.argv[1] && process.argv[1].endsWith('official.mjs')) { try { await run(); } catch (e) { console.log(`::warning::official: ${e.message}; kept the previous out/official.json`); process.exitCode = 1; } }

===== FILE: scoreboard.mjs (claude/design6)
// Results board (step 7, after the model): one place that scores every model the same way each day, and keeps the history,
// so a change that makes anything worse shows up the next morning instead of weeks later.
//  - Win model: its own held-out test (accuracy, calibrated log-loss, events where it beat a coin flip).
//  - Team strength on real events: within each recent event's top cut, how often the team the model rates stronger
//    finished higher (the same model numbers as the page's Team Score, without the page's checklist cut).
//  - Showdown replays: how many games we hold, and how many from 1300+ rated players.
// Writes out/scoreboard.json (today + up to a year of daily history) and warns when a number falls clearly below its
// recent level. Never fails the job: it only reports.
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {activeReg, sdReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const rd = (f, d = null) => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return d; } };
const reg = activeReg().id, same = d => d && (!d.reg || d.reg === reg);
const M = rd('out/model.json'), E = rd('out/events.json'), R = rd('out/replays.json'), today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), row = {date: today, reg};
if (M && M.eval && same(M)) { const e = M.eval;
  row.model = {heldOutAcc: e.model_teams_only.acc, calibratedLogloss: e.calibrated_logloss && (e.calibrated_logloss.with_sets ?? e.calibrated_logloss.species_only),
    eventsBeatCoinFlip: e.events_beating_coin_flip ? `${e.events_beating_coin_flip.beat_coin_flip}/${e.events_beating_coin_flip.events}` : null, rawWinRateAcc: e.raw_win_rates.acc, games: M.games}; }
// team strength x the way the page computes it (species + pairs + clamped set effects, two strongest Megas, untried-mix cut)
if (M && M.ships && same(M) && E && E.events && same(E)) { const D = E.dict, sets = M.sets || {}, fq = M.setFreq || {}, tested = new Set(M.tested || []), spF = new Map();
  for (const k of Object.keys(sets)) { const sp = k.split(/[@:]/)[0]; if (!spF.has(sp)) spF.set(sp, []); spF.get(sp).push(k); }
  const setOne = (n, item, moves) => { if (!M.center) return 0; let u = 0; const mv = new Set(moves);
    for (const k of spF.get(n) || []) { const it = k.indexOf('@') === n.length, has = it ? k.slice(n.length + 1) === item : mv.has(k.slice(n.length + 1)); u += sets[k] * ((has ? 1 : 0) - (fq[k] || 0)); }
    const r = M.setRange && M.setRange[n]; return r ? Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], u)) : u; };
  const x = mons => { const names = mons.map(m => m.n); let v = 0;
    const val = m => (M.mons[m.n] || 0) + setOne(m.n, m.it, m.mv), megas = mons.filter(m => /-Mega/.test(m.n)).sort((a, b) => val(b) - val(a)), drop = new Set(megas.slice(2).map(m => m.n));
    for (const m of mons) v += drop.has(m.n) ? 0 : val(m);
    let k = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 6; j++) { const a = names[i] < names[j] ? names[i] + '|' + names[j] : names[j] + '|' + names[i]; v += (M.pairs || {})[a] || 0; if (tested.has(a)) k++; }
    const mx = M.meanX || 0, d = v - mx; return d > 0 ? mx + d * (.5 + .5 * Math.min(1, k / (M.supK || 6))) : v; };
  let right = 0, pairs = 0, evs = 0;
  for (const ev of E.events) { const ts = (ev.top || []).map(t => ({place: t[1], mons: (t[3] || []).map(m => ({n: D[m[0]], it: D[m[1]], mv: (m[4] || []).map(i => D[i])}))})).filter(t => t.mons.length === 6 && t.mons.every(m => m.n));
    if (ts.length < 2) continue; evs++; const xs = ts.map(t => ({p: t.place, x: x(t.mons)}));
    for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < xs.length; j++) { if (xs[i].p === xs[j].p || xs[i].x === xs[j].x) continue; pairs++; if ((xs[i].x > xs[j].x) === (xs[i].p < xs[j].p)) right++; } }
  row.teamStrengthOnEvents = {acc: pairs ? +(right / pairs).toFixed(4) : null, pairs, events: evs}; }
// Ratings, forward test: each saved snapshot 14+ days old, judged on the Limitless games played after it was taken
// (team with the higher total rating should win). The oldest such snapshot gives the longest look ahead.
{ const RL = rd('out/ratings_log.json'), MM = rd('out/matches.json');
  const rlDays = RL ? (RL.byReg ? (RL.byReg[reg] || []) : (same(RL) ? (RL.days || []) : [])) : [];
  if (rlDays.length && MM && same(MM)) { const teams = MM.teams.map(k => k.split('|')), evD = MM.events.map(e => e[1]), now = Date.now(), outs = [];
    for (const snap of rlDays) { if ((now - Date.parse(snap.d)) / 864e5 < 14) continue; let right = 0, n = 0;
      for (const g of MM.m) { if (g[2] === .5 || evD[g[3]] <= snap.d) continue; const s = t => teams[t].reduce((a, m) => a + (snap.r[m] || 0), 0), a = s(g[0]), b = s(g[1]); if (a === b) continue; n++; if ((a > b) === (g[2] === 1)) right++; }
      if (n >= 300) outs.push({d: snap.d, acc: +(right / n).toFixed(4), games: n}); }
    if (outs.length) row.ratingsForward = {snapshot: outs[0].d, acc: outs[0].acc, games: outs[0].games, snapshots: outs.length, avgAcc: +(outs.reduce((a, x) => a + x.acc, 0) / outs.length).toFixed(4)}; } }
const rReg = R && (R.reg || sdReg(R.format) || SITE_REG.id);
if (R && Array.isArray(R.games) && rReg === reg) row.replays = {games: R.games.length, rated1300: R.games.filter(g => Math.min(g.r[0] || 0, g.r[1] || 0) >= 1300).length};
const board = rd('out/scoreboard.json', {history: []}), byReg = board.byReg && typeof board.byReg === 'object' ? {...board.byReg} : {};
if (!Object.keys(byReg).length && Array.isArray(board.history) && board.history.length) byReg[board.reg || SITE_REG.id] = board.history;
const hist = (Array.isArray(byReg[reg]) ? byReg[reg] : []).filter(h => h.date !== today);
// warn when today is clearly below the median of the last 14 days
const med = a => { const s = a.filter(v => v != null).sort((p, q) => p - q); return s.length ? s[Math.floor(s.length / 2)] : null; }, recent = hist.slice(-14), warns = [];
const cmp = (label, now, past, worseIfLower, tol) => { const m = med(past); if (now == null || m == null) return; if (worseIfLower ? now < m - tol : now > m + tol) warns.push(`${label}: ${now} vs ${m} recently`); };
cmp('Win model held-out accuracy', row.model && row.model.heldOutAcc, recent.map(h => h.model && h.model.heldOutAcc), true, .01);
cmp('Win model calibrated log-loss', row.model && row.model.calibratedLogloss, recent.map(h => h.model && h.model.calibratedLogloss), false, .003);
cmp('Team strength on real events', row.teamStrengthOnEvents && row.teamStrengthOnEvents.acc, recent.map(h => h.teamStrengthOnEvents && h.teamStrengthOnEvents.acc), true, .015);
row.warnings = warns;
byReg[reg] = [...hist, row].slice(-365);
fs.writeFileSync('out/scoreboard.json', JSON.stringify({updated: new Date().toISOString(), reg, today: row, byReg, history: byReg[reg]}, null, 1));
console.log('scoreboard', JSON.stringify(row)); for (const w of warns) console.log(`::warning::${w}`);

===== FILE: track.mjs (claude/design6)
// Track record (step 9, after ratings-snapshot.mjs and scoreboard.mjs): how right have the ratings been?
// ratings-snapshot.mjs saves every Pokémon's rating each morning (out/ratings_log.json). Here each recent event in
// out/events.json (Limitless 32+ players, RK9 official events) is checked against the NEWEST snapshot saved BEFORE the
// event's day, so the ratings could not have seen it: which Pokémon made its top 8, and what we had rated them.
// Writes out/track.json for the page's Track record (meta/track). Checked events are kept here even after events.json
