// drops them (it only holds 30 days), so the record keeps growing. Never fails the job.
//   ev   [id, date, name, players, official 0/1, snapshot date, top-8 teams, [Pokémon per band], [top-8 slots per band], tops]
//        tops = [[name, rating, top-8 teams, best place], ...] (null for the oldest events once the file nears its cap)
//   mons per Pokémon over the events whose snapshot is still in the log: [name, events judged, average rating, top-8 teams,
//        event wins, best event name] (only Pokémon rated 70+ on average or seen in a top 8)
//   mb   per band: [Pokémon whose average rating is in the band, of them seen in a top 8]
//   fw   scoreboard.mjs's games check (snapshot 14+ days old, 300+ later games): [date, accuracy, games]
// Bands (player review 3h, Sim's yes Nov 17): 80+, 70-79, 60-69, under 60 on the saved rating (index 0..3) = shown 90+ (S), 80-89 (A),
// 70-79 (B), under 70: each group is a tier players know. Was 80 / 60 / 40 (shown 90+, 70-89, 53-69, under 53). The cut-offs are
// written to track.json as "bands", and every stored event is regrouped from its snapshot, so the page's labels always match the data.
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {activeReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const rd = f => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('out/' + f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return null; } };
const reg = activeReg().id, L = rd('ratings_log.json'), E = rd('events.json'), SB = rd('scoreboard.json'), prev = rd('track.json') || {};
const prevReg = prev.reg || SITE_REG.id;
if (prev && (Array.isArray(prev.ev) || Array.isArray(prev.mons)) && prevReg !== reg) fs.writeFileSync(`out/track_${prevReg}.json`, JSON.stringify(prev));
const rawDays = L ? (L.byReg ? (L.byReg[reg] || []) : ((!L.reg || L.reg === reg) ? (L.days || []) : [])) : [];
const CAP = 240 * 1024, TOP = 8, BANDS = [80, 70, 60], band = r => r >= BANDS[0] ? 0 : r >= BANDS[1] ? 1 : r >= BANDS[2] ? 2 : 3;
if (!rawDays.length) { const empty = {v: 1, reg, bands: BANDS, updated: new Date().toISOString(), since: null, snaps: 0, last: null, top: 8, ev: [], mons: [], mb: [[0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0]], fw: [], mv: null};
  fs.writeFileSync('out/track.json', JSON.stringify(empty)); console.log(`track (${reg}): no ratings snapshots yet, wrote empty stamped track.json`); process.exit(0); }
const snaps = rawDays.filter(s => s && s.d && s.r).sort((a, b) => a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1), sByD = new Map(snaps.map(s => [s.d, s]));
const snapFor = d => { let s = null; for (const x of snaps) if (x.d < d) s = x; return s; };
const samePrev = prevReg === reg;
const ev = new Map((samePrev && Array.isArray(prev.ev) ? prev.ev : []).filter(e => Array.isArray(e) && e[0]).map(e => [e[0], e]));
// events still in events.json are (re)checked: results of the last few days can still change
if (E && (!E.reg || E.reg === reg) && Array.isArray(E.events) && Array.isArray(E.dict)) for (const e of E.events) {
  const s = snapFor(e.date); if (!s || e.date < snaps[0].d) continue;
  const top = (e.top || []).filter(t => t[1] >= 1 && t[1] <= TOP && Array.isArray(t[3]) && t[3].length === 6); if (top.length < 4) continue;
  const c = new Map(); for (const t of top) for (const n of new Set(t[3].map(m => E.dict[m[0]]))) { const o = c.get(n) || {c: 0, p: 99}; o.c++; o.p = Math.min(o.p, t[1]); c.set(n, o); }
  const bs = [0, 0, 0, 0], ap = [0, 0, 0, 0]; for (const r of Object.values(s.r)) bs[band(r)]++;
  const tops = [...c].filter(([n]) => s.r[n] != null).map(([n, o]) => { ap[band(s.r[n])] += o.c; return [n, s.r[n], o.c, o.p]; }).sort((a, b) => b[2] - a[2] || b[1] - a[1]);
  ev.set(e.id, [e.id, e.date, String(e.name).slice(0, 80), e.players, e.src === 'official' ? 1 : 0, s.d, top.length, bs, ap, tops]); }
// events checked before the cut-offs changed (or with other cut-offs) are regrouped from their own snapshot and their top-8 list
const prevBands = JSON.stringify(prev.bands || [80, 60, 40]); if (prevBands !== JSON.stringify(BANDS)) for (const e of ev.values()) {
  const s = sByD.get(e[5]); if (!s || !Array.isArray(e[9])) continue; const bs = [0, 0, 0, 0], ap = [0, 0, 0, 0];
  for (const r of Object.values(s.r)) bs[band(r)]++; for (const t of e[9]) ap[band(t[1])] += t[2]; e[7] = bs; e[8] = ap; }
const evs = [...ev.values()].sort((a, b) => a[1] < b[1] ? -1 : a[1] > b[1] ? 1 : b[3] - a[3]);
// per Pokémon, over the events whose snapshot is still kept (120 days)
const M = new Map(), seen = evs.filter(e => e[9] && sByD.has(e[5]));
for (const e of seen) { const s = sByD.get(e[5]), hit = new Map(e[9].map(x => [x[0], x]));
  for (const [n, r] of Object.entries(s.r)) { const o = M.get(n) || {n: 0, r: 0, c: 0, w: 0, best: null}; o.n++; o.r += r; const h = hit.get(n);
    if (h) { o.c += h[2]; if (h[3] === 1) { o.w++; o.best = e[2]; } else if (!o.best) o.best = e[2]; } M.set(n, o); } }
const mb = [[0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0]], mons = [];
for (const [n, o] of M) { const r = o.r / o.n, b = band(Math.round(r)); mb[b][0]++; if (o.c) mb[b][1]++; if (r >= 70 || o.c) mons.push([n, o.n, Math.round(r), o.c, o.w, o.best]); }
const sbHist = SB ? (SB.byReg ? (SB.byReg[reg] || []) : ((!SB.reg || SB.reg === reg) ? (SB.history || []) : [])) : [];
const fw = (Array.isArray(sbHist) ? sbHist : []).filter(h => h && h.ratingsForward).map(h => [h.date, h.ratingsForward.acc, h.ratingsForward.games]);
// mv (Sim, Nov 5): the week's biggest rating moves for Home's "This week" line. Newest snapshot against the oldest one from the
// last 7 days; only Pokémon rated 60+ now that moved 2+ points. [from date, to date, [[name, rating now, change], ...]]
// Nov 12: up to 12 kept (was 4) so the new Home can fill its "This week in the ratings" list (Sim: "add a few more"); today's Home still shows 4.
const mv = (() => { const z = snaps[snaps.length - 1], from = new Date(Date.parse(z.d + 'T12:00:00Z') - 7 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const a = snaps.find(x => x.d >= from && x.d < z.d); if (!a) return null;
  const ch = Object.keys(z.r).filter(n => n in a.r && z.r[n] >= 60 && Math.abs(z.r[n] - a.r[n]) >= 2).map(n => [n, z.r[n], z.r[n] - a.r[n]])
    .sort((x, y) => Math.abs(y[2]) - Math.abs(x[2]) || y[1] - x[1]).slice(0, 12);
  return ch.length ? [a.d, z.d, ch] : null; })();
const out = {v: 1, reg, bands: BANDS, updated: new Date().toISOString(), since: snaps[0].d, snaps: snaps.length, last: snaps[snaps.length - 1].d, top: TOP, ev: evs, mons, mb, fw, mv};
// under the cap: the oldest events lose their per-Pokémon lists first (their band totals stay)
for (let i = 0; Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(out)) > CAP && i < evs.length - 20; i++) evs[i][9] = null;
fs.writeFileSync('out/track.json', JSON.stringify(out));
console.log(`track: since ${out.since}, ${out.snaps} snapshots, ${evs.length} events checked, ${Math.round(Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(out)) / 1024)} KiB`);

===== FILE: ratings-snapshot.mjs (claude/design6)
// Daily ratings snapshot (step 8, after the data and model): every Pokémon's NastyPlot rating as the page computes it
// today, kept for 120 days in out/ratings_log.json. scoreboard.mjs later checks each snapshot against the tournament
// games played AFTER it was taken, which the ratings could not have seen: the fairest test of whether they're right.
// Needs Playwright (installed by the op.gg step); if it's missing the step just skips.
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process'; import {activeReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const rd = f => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('out/' + f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return null; } };
let chromium; try { ({chromium} = await import('playwright')); } catch (e) { console.log('ratings snapshot: playwright not installed, skipped'); process.exit(0); }
execFileSync('node', ['app/build.mjs'], {stdio: 'ignore'});
const page = 'app/dist/snapshot-ratings.html';
fs.writeFileSync(page, '<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8">' + fs.readFileSync('app/dist/page.html', 'utf8').replace('render();setView(view,false);', 'window.__ev=c=>eval(c);render();setView(view,false);'));
// meta/perf as the morning refresh writes it: all of perf.json (tour, adj, ingame, official, smogon, ladder, outside)
const L = rd('limitless.json'), P = rd('perf.json'), D = {'meta/results': L && L.results, 'meta/perf': L && Object.assign({adj: L.adj}, P || {}, {updated: L.updated.slice(0, 10)}), 'meta/doubles_tour': rd('meta_tour.json'), 'meta/teams': rd('teams.json'),
  'meta/history': rd('history.json'), 'meta/model': rd('model.json'), 'meta/doubles': rd('meta_ladder.json'), 'meta/doubles_ingame': rd('meta_ingame.json'), 'meta/events': rd('events.json')};
const b = await chromium.launch(), pg = await (await b.newContext()).newPage();
await pg.addInitScript(D => { window.claude = {use: async n => n === 'db' ? {doc: p => ({onSnapshot: cb => { setTimeout(() => cb({exists: !!D[p], data: () => D[p]}), 5); return () => {}; }, get: async () => ({exists: false}), set: async () => {}})} : null}; }, D);
await pg.goto('file://' + process.cwd() + '/' + page); await pg.waitForTimeout(4000);
const r = await pg.evaluate(() => window.__ev('Object.fromEntries(ROSTER.map(p => [p.n, rating(p).total]))')); await b.close(); fs.unlinkSync(page);
const reg = activeReg().id, log = rd('ratings_log.json') || {}, d = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
const byReg = log.byReg && typeof log.byReg === 'object' ? {...log.byReg} : {};
if (!Object.keys(byReg).length && Array.isArray(log.days) && log.days.length) byReg[log.reg || SITE_REG.id] = log.days;
const prevDays = Array.isArray(byReg[reg]) ? byReg[reg] : [];
byReg[reg] = [...prevDays.filter(x => x.d !== d), {d, r}].slice(-120);
fs.writeFileSync('out/ratings_log.json', JSON.stringify({reg, updated: new Date().toISOString(), byReg}));
console.log(`ratings snapshot (${reg}): ${Object.keys(r).length} Pokémon saved for ${d} (${byReg[reg].length} days kept)`);

===== FILE: six.mjs (claude/design6)
// Each six's own record, with player skill and opponent strength taken out (Grade v2, Nov 7; tested in
// experiments/grade-v2.mjs: the grade built this way picked the winner of 56.1% of later games, against 53.6% for the old grade).
// Runs after model.mjs. For every exact six seen in tournament games:
//   P(A wins) = σ( calib·(xA + δA − xB − δB) + sA − sB )
// with x the shipped team model (held fixed), s each player's skill and δ the six's own deviation, both shrunk toward 0
// (δ ~ N(0, 0.1²), s ~ N(0, 0.6²); τ was .35 until Nov 8: with each set counted fully, the scoreboard found .1 as
// good on log-loss as no record at all and a little better at picking winners, at all three date cut-offs), so a few games barely move a six and hundreds of games move it a lot.
// Adds to out/model.json: sixes = {"Mon1|Mon2|…" (sorted): [δ, games]} for sixes with 10+ games. The page adds δ to its own
// team strength for that exact six; every other team is graded on the model alone.
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {activeReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const MF = process.env.MODEL_FILE || 'out/model.json'; // MATCHES / MODEL_FILE: experiments
const M = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.env.MATCHES || 'out/matches.json', 'utf8')), MD = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(MF, 'utf8'));
const curReg = activeReg().id, mReg = M.reg || SITE_REG.id, mdReg = MD.reg || SITE_REG.id;
if (mReg !== mdReg || mReg !== curReg) { console.log(`six: reg mismatch (matches ${mReg}, model ${mdReg}, active ${curReg}), skipped`); process.exit(0); }
if (!MD.ships || !MD.mons) { console.log('six: model not shipped, nothing added'); process.exit(0); }
const TAU = .1, SIG_S = .6, ITERS = 300, MIN_G = 10, calib = MD.calib; // 10+ games: keeps meta/model under the 256 KB the page database takes per write
const teams = M.teams.map(k => k.split('|'));
const spF = new Map(); for (const f of Object.keys(MD.sets || {})) { const sp = f.split(/[@:#]/)[0]; if (!spF.has(sp)) spF.set(sp, []); spF.get(sp).push(f); }
const xOf = (t, d) => { const m = teams[t]; let v = 0; for (const n of m) v += MD.mons[n] || 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < m.length; j++) { const k = m[i] < m[j] ? m[i] + '|' + m[j] : m[j] + '|' + m[i]; v += (MD.pairs || {})[k] || 0; }
  if (d == null || !M.decks || !M.decks[d]) return v; const has = new Set(M.decks[d].map(f => M.feats[f]));
  for (const n of m) { let u = 0; for (const f of spF.get(n) || []) u += MD.sets[f] * ((has.has(f) ? 1 : 0) - ((MD.setFreq || {})[f] || 0)); const r = (MD.setRange || {})[n]; v += r ? Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], u)) : u; }
  return v; };
const G = M.m.filter(g => g[2] !== .5 && g[0] !== g[1] && teams[g[0]].length === 6 && teams[g[1]].length === 6), X = G.map(g => [xOf(g[0], g[7]), xOf(g[1], g[8])]);
const s = new Float64Array(M.players), dl = new Float64Array(M.teams.length), ms = new Float64Array(M.players), vs = new Float64Array(M.players), md = new Float64Array(M.teams.length), vd = new Float64Array(M.teams.length), gs = new Float64Array(M.players), gd = new Float64Array(M.teams.length);
const lr = .05, b1 = .9, b2 = .999;
for (let it = 1; it <= ITERS; it++) { gs.fill(0); gd.fill(0);
  G.forEach((g, i) => { const eta = calib * (X[i][0] + dl[g[0]] - X[i][1] - dl[g[1]]) + s[g[4]] - s[g[5]], e = (g[2] === 1 ? 1 : 0) - 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-eta)); gs[g[4]] += e; gs[g[5]] -= e; gd[g[0]] += calib * e; gd[g[1]] -= calib * e; });
  for (let j = 0; j < s.length; j++) { const gr = gs[j] - s[j] / (SIG_S * SIG_S); ms[j] = b1 * ms[j] + (1 - b1) * gr; vs[j] = b2 * vs[j] + (1 - b2) * gr * gr; s[j] += lr * (ms[j] / (1 - b1 ** it)) / (Math.sqrt(vs[j] / (1 - b2 ** it)) + 1e-8); }
  for (let j = 0; j < dl.length; j++) { const gr = gd[j] - dl[j] / (TAU * TAU); md[j] = b1 * md[j] + (1 - b1) * gr; vd[j] = b2 * vd[j] + (1 - b2) * gr * gr; dl[j] += lr * (md[j] / (1 - b1 ** it)) / (Math.sqrt(vd[j] / (1 - b2 ** it)) + 1e-8); } }
// one entry per six (the same six can appear under more than one team id): games added up, δ weighted by games
const per = new Map(); const ng = new Map(); for (const g of G) { ng.set(g[0], (ng.get(g[0]) || 0) + 1); ng.set(g[1], (ng.get(g[1]) || 0) + 1); }
for (const [t, n] of ng) { const k = teams[t].slice().sort().join('|'), o = per.get(k) || {g: 0, d: 0}; o.g += n; o.d += dl[t] * n; per.set(k, o); }
const sixes = {}; for (const [k, o] of per) if (o.g >= MIN_G) sixes[k] = [+(o.d / o.g).toFixed(4), o.g];
MD.sixes = sixes; MD.sixesNote = `each six's own record, player skill and opponents taken out (six.mjs): [δ, games]; page strength = model x + δ; τ ${TAU}, ${G.length} games`;
fs.writeFileSync(MF, JSON.stringify(MD));
const top = Object.entries(sixes).sort((a, b) => b[1][1] - a[1][1]).slice(0, 3).map(([k, v]) => `${v[1]} games δ ${v[0]}`);
console.log(`six: ${Object.keys(sixes).length} sixes with ${MIN_G}+ games added to out/model.json (most played: ${top.join(', ')})`);

===== FILE: ctr.mjs (claude/design6)
// Matchups learned from real games (Nov 8, Sim: "absolutely go with the real results"). Runs after model.mjs and six.mjs.
// Which Pokémon do well against which, read from every tournament game with each team's strength and each player's skill
// taken out:  P(A wins) = σ( calib·(xA − xB) + sA − sB + Σ_{a∈A, b∈B} c[a][b] ),  c[a][b] = −c[b][a], shrunk toward 0.
// Tested in experiments/counters.mjs at three date cut-offs: against a known opponent it picked later winners about half a
// point better (56.3% → 56.8%) with better-calibrated chances; averaged over the whole field it added nothing, so the page
// uses it only when the opponent is known (Battle: Plan a Match), never in the team grade.
// Adds to out/model.json: ctr = {sp: [Pokémon on 1%+ of teams], v: [c for each pair a<b, in thousandths, row by row]}.
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {activeReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const MF = process.env.MODEL_FILE || 'out/model.json';
const M = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.env.MATCHES || 'out/matches.json', 'utf8')), MD = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(MF, 'utf8'));
const curReg = activeReg().id, mReg = M.reg || SITE_REG.id, mdReg = MD.reg || SITE_REG.id;
if (mReg !== mdReg || mReg !== curReg) { console.log(`ctr: reg mismatch (matches ${mReg}, model ${mdReg}, active ${curReg}), skipped`); process.exit(0); }
if (!MD.ships || !MD.mons) { console.log('ctr: model not shipped, nothing added'); process.exit(0); }
const LAM = 300, ITERS = 250, calib = MD.calib, sig = z => 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-z)), teams = M.teams.map(k => k.split('|'));
const spF = new Map(); for (const f of Object.keys(MD.sets || {})) { const sp = f.split(/[@:#]/)[0]; if (!spF.has(sp)) spF.set(sp, []); spF.get(sp).push(f); }
const xOf = (t, d) => { const m = teams[t]; let v = 0; for (const n of m) v += MD.mons[n] || 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < m.length; j++) { const k = m[i] < m[j] ? m[i] + '|' + m[j] : m[j] + '|' + m[i]; v += (MD.pairs || {})[k] || 0; }
  if (d == null || !M.decks || !M.decks[d]) return v; const has = new Set(M.decks[d].map(f => M.feats[f]));
  for (const n of m) { let u = 0; for (const f of spF.get(n) || []) u += MD.sets[f] * ((has.has(f) ? 1 : 0) - ((MD.setFreq || {})[f] || 0)); const r = (MD.setRange || {})[n]; v += r ? Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], u)) : u; }
  return v; };
const G = M.m.filter(g => g[2] !== .5 && g[0] !== g[1] && teams[g[0]].length === 6 && teams[g[1]].length === 6), X = G.map(g => calib * (xOf(g[0], g[7]) - xOf(g[1], g[8])));
const cnt = new Map(); for (const g of G) for (const t of [g[0], g[1]]) for (const n of teams[t]) cnt.set(n, (cnt.get(n) || 0) + 1);
const sp = [...cnt].filter(([, c]) => c / (G.length * 2) >= .01).map(([n]) => n).sort(), ix = new Map(sp.map((n, i) => [n, i])), K = sp.length;
const idx = t => teams[t].map(n => ix.get(n)).filter(i => i != null), PI = G.map(g => [idx(g[0]), idx(g[1])]);
// player skill first (so a strong player's wins don't read as a counter), then the matchups with skill held fixed
const S = new Float64Array(M.players); { const m1 = new Float64Array(M.players), v1 = new Float64Array(M.players), gs = new Float64Array(M.players);
  for (let it = 1; it <= 200; it++) { gs.fill(0); G.forEach((g, i) => { const e = (g[2] === 1 ? 1 : 0) - sig(X[i] + S[g[4]] - S[g[5]]); gs[g[4]] += e; gs[g[5]] -= e; });
    for (let j = 0; j < S.length; j++) { const gr = gs[j] - S[j] / .36; m1[j] = .9 * m1[j] + .1 * gr; v1[j] = .999 * v1[j] + .001 * gr * gr; S[j] += .05 * (m1[j] / (1 - .9 ** it)) / (Math.sqrt(v1[j] / (1 - .999 ** it)) + 1e-8); } } }
const C = new Float64Array(K * K); { const m1 = new Float64Array(K * K), v1 = new Float64Array(K * K), gC = new Float64Array(K * K);
  for (let it = 1; it <= ITERS; it++) { gC.fill(0);
    G.forEach((g, i) => { const [A, B] = PI[i]; let z = X[i] + S[g[4]] - S[g[5]]; for (const a of A) for (const b of B) z += C[a * K + b];
      const e = (g[2] === 1 ? 1 : 0) - sig(z); for (const a of A) for (const b of B) { gC[a * K + b] += e; gC[b * K + a] -= e; } });
    for (let a = 0; a < K; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < K; b++) { const j = a * K + b, gr = (gC[j] - gC[b * K + a]) / 2 - C[j] * LAM; m1[j] = .9 * m1[j] + .1 * gr; v1[j] = .999 * v1[j] + .001 * gr * gr;
      C[j] += .02 * (m1[j] / (1 - .9 ** it)) / (Math.sqrt(v1[j] / (1 - .999 ** it)) + 1e-8); C[b * K + a] = -C[j]; } } }
const v = []; for (let a = 0; a < K; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < K; b++) v.push(Math.round(C[a * K + b] * 1000));
MD.ctr = {sp, v, lam: LAM, games: G.length};
MD.ctrNote = 'matchups learned from real games (ctr.mjs): c[a][b] in thousandths of a logit for each pair a<b (row by row), c[b][a] = −c[a][b]; used only against a known opponent';
// size guard: the page database takes at most 256 KB per write. If the model grows past 245 KB, the least-played sixes' own
// records go first (they count least: a six's record is shrunk hard toward 0 when it has few games)
{ let js = JSON.stringify(MD); if (js.length > 245 * 1024 && MD.sixes) { const e = Object.entries(MD.sixes).sort((a, b) => a[1][1] - b[1][1]); let dropped = 0;
    while (js.length > 245 * 1024 && e.length) { for (let i = 0; i < 50 && e.length; i++) { delete MD.sixes[e.shift()[0]]; dropped++; } js = JSON.stringify(MD); }
    console.log(`ctr: size guard dropped the ${dropped} least-played sixes' own records to stay under the 256 KB page limit`); } }
fs.writeFileSync(MF, JSON.stringify(MD));
const top = []; for (let a = 0; a < K; a++) for (let b = 0; b < K; b++) if (C[a * K + b] > 0) top.push([sp[a], sp[b], C[a * K + b]]); top.sort((x, y) => y[2] - x[2]);
console.log(`ctr: ${K} Pokémon, ${v.length} pairs from ${G.length} games; model now ${(JSON.stringify(MD).length / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB; strongest: ${top.slice(0, 4).map(([a, b, c]) => `${a} > ${b} +${(sig(c) * 100 - 50).toFixed(1)}pt`).join(', ')}`);

===== FILE: model.mjs (claude/design6)
// Team strength model trained on real games: every pairing where both teams are known.
// P(A beats B) = σ( skillA − skillB + x(A) − x(B) ),
// x(team) = Σ mon strength + Σ pair synergy (pairs on 30+ teams) + Σ set features (items and moves seen on 40+ teams).
// Player skill is fitted alongside, so a strong player's wins don't inflate their Pokémon. Ridge-shrunk, fitted with Adam.
// Tested on held-out recent events before anything ships: set features are kept only if they beat the species-only model,
// and the model ships only if it beats raw win rates. Writes out/model.json with the numbers and the test scores.
// v2 (Oct 26): set features are centered within each species (has feature − how often that species' decks have it), so a
// Pokémon's strength lives in one shrunk number and sets only say how a build differs from the usual one. Before this, a
// rarely used Pokémon with one fixed set could pile up credit across 5 set features (Mega Camerupt rated as the best
// Pokémon in the game). Also ships the held-out calibration slope and the spread of real teams, so the page can score a
// team against real tournament teams instead of a fixed range that any odd team could max out.
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {pilotTest} from './pilot.mjs'; import {deckBasics} from './team-basics.mjs'; import {activeReg, SITE_REG} from './formats.mjs';
const M = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.env.MATCHES || 'out/matches.json', 'utf8')); // MATCHES=file: experiments (e.g. out/matches_ab.json)
const curReg = M.reg || activeReg().id;
const teams = (M.teams || []).map(k => k.split('|')), G = (M.m || []).filter(g => g[2] !== .5 && g[0] !== g[1]);
// On early days of a new regulation (0 games or too few events to split/fit), write a stamped not-ready model instead of keeping an old regulation's model
{ const dest = process.env.MODEL_OUT || (process.env.TRAIN_ONLY === '1' ? 'out/model_train.json' : 'out/model.json');
  let prev = null; try { prev = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(dest, 'utf8')); } catch (e) {}
  const prevReg = prev && (prev.reg || SITE_REG.id);
  if (G.length === 0 || (M.events || []).length < 5) {
    if (prev && prev.ships && prev.mons && prevReg === curReg) { console.log(`model: NOT shipped today (${G.length} games), so ${dest} keeps the previous ${curReg} model`); process.exit(1); }
    fs.writeFileSync(dest, JSON.stringify({reg: curReg, updated: new Date().toISOString(), ships: false, games: G.length, events: (M.events || []).length, v: 3, mons: {}, pairs: {}, sets: {}, note: 'too few games to fit model yet'}));
    console.log(`model: ${curReg} not ready (${G.length} games, ${(M.events || []).length} events); wrote stamped not-ready model`); process.exit(0); } }
const monIx = new Map(), mons = [];
teams.flat().forEach(n => { if (!monIx.has(n)) { monIx.set(n, mons.length); mons.push(n); } });
// pair features: pairs on 30+ distinct teams in games
const pc = new Map(), seen = new Set();
for (const g of G) for (const t of [g[0], g[1]]) { if (seen.has(t)) continue; seen.add(t); const m = teams[t];
  for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < m.length; j++) { const k = m[i] + '|' + m[j]; pc.set(k, (pc.get(k) || 0) + 1); } }
const pairIx = new Map([...pc].filter(([, n]) => n >= 30).map(([k], i) => [k, i])), pairs = [...pairIx.keys()];
const tf = teams.map(m => { const f = m.map(n => monIx.get(n)), p = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < m.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < m.length; j++) { const q = pairIx.get(m[i] + '|' + m[j]); if (q != null) p.push(q); } return {f, p}; });
// set features (older matches.json has none): items and moves seen on 40+ distinct decks in games
const hasSets = Array.isArray(M.decks) && G.length && G[0].length >= 9;
let setNames = [], deckF = [];
if (hasSets) { const cnt = new Map(), used = new Set(); for (const g of G) { used.add(g[7]); used.add(g[8]); }
  for (const d of used) if (d != null && M.decks[d]) for (const f of M.decks[d]) cnt.set(f, (cnt.get(f) || 0) + 1);
  const keep = new Map([...cnt].filter(([, n]) => n >= 40).map(([f], i) => [f, i])); setNames = [...keep.keys()].map(f => M.feats[f]);
  deckF = M.decks.map(fs_ => fs_.map(f => keep.get(f)).filter(j => j != null)); }
// centering: how often each kept feature appears among decks that carry its species
const featSp = setNames.map(f => f.split(/[@:#]/)[0]), spFeats = new Map(), freq = new Float64Array(setNames.length);
if (hasSets) { const spDecks = new Map(), fd = new Float64Array(setNames.length), used = new Set(); for (const g of G) { used.add(g[7]); used.add(g[8]); }
  for (const d of used) { if (d == null || !M.decks[d]) continue; for (const sp of new Set(M.decks[d].map(f => M.feats[f].split(/[@:#]/)[0]))) spDecks.set(sp, (spDecks.get(sp) || 0) + 1); for (const j of deckF[d]) fd[j]++; }
  setNames.forEach((f, j) => { freq[j] = fd[j] / (spDecks.get(featSp[j]) || 1); if (!spFeats.has(featSp[j])) spFeats.set(featSp[j], []); spFeats.get(featSp[j]).push(j); }); }
// pair bonuses pulled 4× harder toward 0 (Deep Check, Nov 8: pairs add no accuracy on later games; Sim chose "shrunk"),
// locked in model-settings.json like the other settings
const PAIR_SHRINK_LOCK = (() => { try { return +JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('model-settings.json', 'utf8')).pairShrink || 1; } catch (e) { return 1; } })();
const nP = M.players;
// v6 (Deep Check, Nov 8): team-wide basics, learned from the same rounds, so each one's size comes from real results.
// Sim, Nov 8: a no-Mega team graded 98 while real no-Mega teams win 34%; the grade only ever counted Pokémon, pairs and sets.
// On by default since Sim approved the preview (Nov 9); TEAM_FEATS=0 turns them off for experiments. Move-based ones count only
// on sheets that list 16+ moves (else unknown = 0).
// Kept: the two that expert guides AND real results agree on (broken-team tests, Nov 8). 'no speed control' and 'no Fake Out'
// came out as small bonuses; the grade never rewards lacking a basic, so they're left out.
const TEAMF = process.env.TEAM_FEATS === '0' ? [] : ['noMega', 'protectLow', ...(process.env.TR_FEAT === '1' ? ['trNoSlow'] : [])];
// Nov 9 (Sim: Farigiraf kept being suggested for fast teams): Trick Room on the sheet but fewer than 2 slow teammates (base Speed
// 60 or less, not counting the Trick Room users). Real Farigiraf-with-Trick-Room teams won 46.6% with 0 slow teammates, 49.1% with 1,
// 50.8% with 2+ (all games, in-sample). TESTED Nov 9 on later games (dev, before/after Oct 27): it made predictions worse (log-loss
// +0.0005, 95% range +0.0002 to +0.0009, both halves); in those later games Farigiraf Trick Room teams with <2 slow teammates won 49.7%
// while today's grade expected 47.0%, so the grade already rates them lower and real results don't support a bigger penalty.
// Kept as an experiment only: TR_FEAT=1 turns it on. Speeds from the site's roster (app/src/data/data.js).
// the basics of each sheet: team-basics.mjs (shared with the page's check, experiments/suggest-gaps.mjs)
const deckTF = !TEAMF.length || !hasSets ? [] : M.decks.map(fs_ => deckBasics(fs_.map(f => M.feats[f])));
const tvCache = new Map(), teamVec = (t, d) => { if (!TEAMF.length) return []; const key = t * 100000 + (d ?? 99999); let v = tvCache.get(key); if (v) return v;
  v = TEAMF.map(k => k === 'noMega' ? (teams[t].some(n => /-Mega/.test(n)) ? 0 : 1) : (d != null && deckTF[d] ? deckTF[d][k] : 0)); tvCache.set(key, v); return v; };
// time split: last 20% of events (by date) held out
const evDates = M.events.map(e => e[1]); const order = [...evDates.keys()].sort((a, b) => evDates[a] < evDates[b] ? -1 : 1);
// TEST_FROM / TEST_TO (experiments only): hold out an earlier slice instead, training only on events before it
const TF = +(process.env.TEST_FROM || .8), TT = +(process.env.TEST_TO || 1), lo = Math.floor(order.length * TF), hi = Math.floor(order.length * TT);
// TEST_DATE (experiments, Nov 8 item 5): split by date instead; held out = tournament events on or after it, trained on every
// event before it. Ladder-game "events" (id sd-…, experiments/matches-rp.mjs) are never held out, and those after it are dropped.
const TD = process.env.TEST_DATE, isSD = i => String(M.events[i][0]).startsWith('sd-');
const cut = TD ? new Set(order.filter(i => evDates[i] >= TD && !isSD(i))) : new Set(order.slice(lo, hi)), before = TD ? new Set(order.filter(i => evDates[i] < TD)) : new Set(order.slice(0, lo));
const train = G.filter(g => before.has(g[3])), test = G.filter(g => cut.has(g[3]));
// v3 (Oct 27): recent games count more. A game's weight halves every `half` days before the newest game in the fit
// (0 = all games equal); the half-life is picked on the validation slice. Tested on held-out events: recency plus
// set features called 56.0% of games vs 54.9%, beat 50% in 56 of 66 events vs 52, and lowered calibrated log-loss.
const day = s => new Date(s + 'T00:00:00Z').getTime() / 864e5;
// v5 (Oct 27): evidence shrink. After fitting, each Pokémon's effect is scaled by n/(n+1000) and each pair's by
// n/(n+300), n = its team appearances in the games fitted, so a Pokémon a few strong players brought can't carry a team
// on a big number from a small sample. Tested on held-out events (TRAIN_ONLY + experiments/ts-audit.mjs, Oct 27):
// log-loss 0.6828 vs 0.6830 without it, the same separation of top-8 teams from the rest (AUC 0.60), and a six of rare
// high-number Pokémon (Annihilape, Kommo-o, Scrafty, Mega Venusaur, Hydreigon, Mega Charizard Y) fell from 68 to 28.
// EVID_K / EVID_KP env vars override (0 = off).
const EVID_K = +(process.env.EVID_K ?? 1000), EVID_KP = +(process.env.EVID_KP ?? 300);
// v7 (Nov 18): steady team-wide basics. 'noMega' is learned from only about 200 no-Mega-vs-Mega games; with the 14-day recency of
// the rest of the model a handful of new games moved it from -0.62 to -0.49 in a day (Nov 17 to 18), and the weakest six crossed 60.
// TEAM_HALF = the recency half-life (days) used for the team-wide basics only (players, Pokémon, pairs and sets keep `half`):
// unset = the same as the rest (the model before Nov 18), 0 = every game counts the same. Locked in model-settings.json (teamHalf).
// TEAM_PRIOR = K (experiments): keep the recency fit for the basics but pull them toward their no-recency value with the weight of
// K games (a minimum amount of evidence). Each env var overrides the locked setting.
const SETTINGS = (() => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('model-settings.json', 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return {}; } })();
const TEAM_HALF = process.env.TEAM_HALF != null && process.env.TEAM_HALF !== '' ? +process.env.TEAM_HALF : (SETTINGS.teamHalf != null ? +SETTINGS.teamHalf : null);
const TEAM_PRIOR = +(process.env.TEAM_PRIOR || 0);
function fit(games, opt) { let {useSkill = true, usePairs = true, useSets = false, iters = +(process.env.ITERS || 250), lb = 4, lp = 15, ls = 1.5, lc = 40, half = 0, teamHalf, teamPrior} = opt || {};
  if (teamHalf == null) teamHalf = TEAM_HALF != null ? TEAM_HALF : half;
  // TEAM_PRIOR: the long-run (no-recency) value of the basics first, then the recency fit is pulled toward it
  if (TEAM_PRIOR > 0 && TEAMF.length && teamPrior == null && teamHalf > 0) teamPrior = {T0: Array.from(fit(games, {...opt, teamHalf: 0, teamPrior: false}).T), lam: TEAM_PRIOR * .1};
  // experiments (Deep Check, Nov 8): NO_PAIRS=1 fits without pair bonuses; PAIR_SHRINK=k pulls pair bonuses k× harder toward 0
  if (process.env.NO_PAIRS === '1') usePairs = false; lp *= +(process.env.PAIR_SHRINK || PAIR_SHRINK_LOCK);
  const b = new Float64Array(mons.length), q = new Float64Array(pairs.length), s = new Float64Array(nP), c = new Float64Array(setNames.length), T = new Float64Array(TEAMF.length);
  const P = [b, q, s, c, T], Mm = P.map(a => new Float64Array(a.length)), V = P.map(a => new Float64Array(a.length)), L = [lb, lp, ls, lc, 1];
  // weights are scaled to average 1, so the shrinkage means the same thing with or without recency
  let last = -Infinity; for (const g of games) last = Math.max(last, day(evDates[g[3]]));
  const W = games.map(g => half ? Math.pow(.5, (last - day(evDates[g[3]])) / half) : 1), Wn = games.length / (W.reduce((a, x) => a + x, 0) || 1);
  // the team-wide basics' own game weights (v7): the same as W unless teamHalf differs from half
  const WT = teamHalf === half ? W : games.map(g => teamHalf ? Math.pow(.5, (last - day(evDates[g[3]])) / teamHalf) : 1), WTn = games.length / (WT.reduce((a, x) => a + x, 0) || 1);
  // a deck's set features as [feature, value]: 1 − freq when the deck has it, −freq when the species is there without it
  const dv = new Map(), deckVec = (t, d) => { if (!useSets || d == null) return []; const key = t * 100000 + d; let v = dv.get(key); if (v) return v; v = []; const has = new Set(deckF[d]);
    for (const n of teams[t]) for (const j of (spFeats.get(n) || [])) v.push([j, (has.has(j) ? 1 : 0) - freq[j]]); dv.set(key, v); return v; };
  const x = (t, d) => { let v = 0; for (const j of tf[t].f) v += b[j]; if (usePairs) for (const j of tf[t].p) v += q[j]; for (const [j, w] of deckVec(t, d)) v += c[j] * w; const tv = teamVec(t, d); for (let k = 0; k < tv.length; k++) v += T[k] * tv[k]; return v; };
  for (let it = 1; it <= iters; it++) { const Gr = P.map(a => new Float64Array(a.length));
    for (let gi = 0; gi < games.length; gi++) { const [A, B, r, , pa, pb, , dA, dB] = games[gi]; const eta = x(A, dA) - x(B, dB) + (useSkill ? s[pa] - s[pb] : 0), e0 = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-eta)) - r, d = e0 * W[gi] * Wn, dT = e0 * WT[gi] * WTn;
      for (const j of tf[A].f) Gr[0][j] += d; for (const j of tf[B].f) Gr[0][j] -= d;
      if (usePairs) { for (const j of tf[A].p) Gr[1][j] += d; for (const j of tf[B].p) Gr[1][j] -= d; }
      if (useSkill) { Gr[2][pa] += d; Gr[2][pb] -= d; }
      for (const [j, w] of deckVec(A, dA)) Gr[3][j] += d * w; for (const [j, w] of deckVec(B, dB)) Gr[3][j] -= d * w;
      if (TEAMF.length) { const ta = teamVec(A, dA), tb = teamVec(B, dB); for (let k = 0; k < ta.length; k++) Gr[4][k] += dT * (ta[k] - tb[k]); } }
    const c1 = 1 - .9 ** it, c2 = 1 - .999 ** it;
    for (let k = 0; k < P.length; k++) { const a = P[k], g = Gr[k], m = Mm[k], v = V[k];
      for (let j = 0; j < a.length; j++) { const gg = g[j] + 2 * L[k] * a[j] + (k === 4 && teamPrior ? 2 * teamPrior.lam * (a[j] - teamPrior.T0[j]) : 0); m[j] = .9 * m[j] + .1 * gg; v[j] = .999 * v[j] + .001 * gg * gg; a[j] -= .03 * (m[j] / c1) / (Math.sqrt(v[j] / c2) + 1e-8); } } }
  // how sure each number is (Nov 8, step 5 "show how sure the grade is"): the spread a ridge fit implies, 1/(Σ w·p(1−p) + 2λ)
  // over the games it appears in (the diagonal of the curvature; partners' overlap is ignored, so it is checked on later games)
  const hb = new Float64Array(b.length), hq = new Float64Array(q.length);
  for (let gi = 0; gi < games.length; gi++) { const [A, B, , , pa, pb, , dA, dB] = games[gi], eta = x(A, dA) - x(B, dB) + (useSkill ? s[pa] - s[pb] : 0), pp = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-eta)), h = pp * (1 - pp) * W[gi] * Wn;
    for (const t of [A, B]) { for (const j of tf[t].f) hb[j] += h; if (usePairs) for (const j of tf[t].p) hq[j] += h; } }
  const vb = Array.from(hb, (h, j) => 1 / (h + 2 * lb)), vq = Array.from(hq, (h, j) => 1 / (h + 2 * lp));
  // evidence shrink: each Pokémon's / pair's effect scaled by n/(n+K), n = its team appearances in the games fitted
  if (EVID_K > 0 || EVID_KP > 0) { const nb = new Float64Array(mons.length), nq = new Float64Array(pairs.length);
    for (const [A, B] of games) for (const t of [A, B]) { for (const j of tf[t].f) nb[j]++; for (const j of tf[t].p) nq[j]++; }
    if (EVID_K > 0) for (let j = 0; j < b.length; j++) b[j] *= nb[j] / (nb[j] + EVID_K);
    if (EVID_KP > 0) for (let j = 0; j < q.length; j++) q[j] *= nq[j] / (nq[j] + EVID_KP); }
  return {b, q, s, c, T, x, useSets, vb, vq}; }
function score(model, games, skillToo) { let ll = 0, acc = 0;
  for (const [A, B, r, , pa, pb, , dA, dB] of games) { const eta = model.x(A, dA) - model.x(B, dB) + (skillToo ? model.s[pa] - model.s[pb] : 0), p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-eta));
    ll -= r * Math.log(p) + (1 - r) * Math.log(1 - p); acc += (p > .5) === (r === 1) ? 1 : 0; }
  return {logloss: +(ll / games.length).toFixed(4), acc: +(acc / games.length).toFixed(4), n: games.length}; }
// baseline like the old score: each Pokémon's raw team win rate
function rawBaseline(games) { const w = new Float64Array(mons.length), g = new Float64Array(mons.length);
  for (const [A, B, r] of games) { for (const j of tf[A].f) { w[j] += r; g[j]++; } for (const j of tf[B].f) { w[j] += 1 - r; g[j]++; } }
  const lg = j => Math.log((w[j] + 10) / (g[j] - w[j] + 10)); return {x: t => tf[t].f.reduce((a, j) => a + lg(j), 0) / 2, s: new Float64Array(nP)}; }
const t0 = Date.now();
// pick the shrinkage on a validation slice (the latest 15% of training events), never on the test events
const trEv = [...new Set(train.map(g => g[3]))].filter(i => !isSD(i)).sort((a, b) => evDates[a] < evDates[b] ? -1 : 1), vcut = new Set(trEv.slice(Math.floor(trEv.length * .85)));
const fitPart = train.filter(g => !vcut.has(g[3])), val = train.filter(g => vcut.has(g[3]));
// FAST=K,HALF,SKILL,SETS (experiments): skip the validation grid and use these settings
// ITERS=n (training steps per fit, default 250) and K_GRID=1,3,5,… (shrink values to try): Nov 8 "train our models more" tests
// Settings are LOCKED (model-settings.json, Nov 8: stability): the daily run uses them as is. RETUNE=1 (or K_GRID) searches the
// grid again, for the monthly check; FAST=… still overrides for experiments.
const LOCK = (() => { if (process.env.RETUNE === '1' || process.env.K_GRID) return null; try { const L = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync('model-settings.json', 'utf8')); return [L.shrink, L.half, L.skill, L.sets]; } catch (e) { return null; } })();
const FAST = process.env.FAST ? process.env.FAST.split(',').map(Number) : LOCK;
let bestL = FAST ? {k: FAST[0], sc: 0} : null; if (!FAST) for (const k of (process.env.K_GRID ? process.env.K_GRID.split(',').map(Number) : [1, 3, 10, 30, 100])) { const m = fit(fitPart, {lb: 4 * k, lp: 15 * k, ls: 1.5 * k}); const sc = score(m, val, false).logloss; console.log('shrink', k, sc); if (!bestL || sc < bestL.sc) bestL = {k, sc}; }
// recency half-life, picked on the validation slice (0 = no recency, kept if nothing beats it)
let bestH = {half: FAST ? FAST[1] : 0, sc: bestL.sc}; if (!FAST) for (const half of [14, 21, 30]) { const m = fit(fitPart, {lb: 4 * bestL.k, lp: 15 * bestL.k, ls: 1.5 * bestL.k, half}); const sc = score(m, val, false).logloss; console.log('recency half-life', half, sc); if (sc < bestH.sc) bestH = {half, sc}; }
// v4 (Oct 27): how much of a result to put down to the player rather than the team, picked on the validation slice.
// Fitting player skill stops a top player's wins inflating their Pokémon, but strong players also pick strong teams, so
// separating them too hard throws away real signal. 0 = no player term at all. The full adjustment stays unless another
// setting is clearly better (by 0.002 log-loss): tested Oct 27, a 10x lighter one gained only 0.0009 (noise) while letting
// rare Pokémon that strong players brought rate higher (an odd six rose from below 90 to 92), so it was not shipped.
let bestS = {sm: FAST ? FAST[2] : 1, sc: bestH.sc}; if (!FAST) for (const sm of [.3, 3, 10, 0]) { const m = fit(fitPart, {lb: 4 * bestL.k, lp: 15 * bestL.k, ls: 1.5 * bestL.k * sm, useSkill: sm > 0, half: bestH.half}); const sc = score(m, val, false).logloss; console.log('player-skill shrink', sm, sc); if (sc < bestH.sc - .002 && sc < bestS.sc) bestS = {sm, sc}; }
const K = bestL.k, HALF = bestH.half, LAM = {lb: 4 * K, lp: 15 * K, ls: 1.5 * K * (bestS.sm || 1), useSkill: bestS.sm > 0, half: HALF};
bestH.sc = bestS.sc;
let LC = FAST && FAST[3] ? {lc: FAST[3], sc: 0} : null; if (!FAST && hasSets && setNames.length) for (const lc of [20, 60, 200]) { const m = fit(fitPart, {...LAM, useSets: true, lc}); const sc = score(m, val, false).logloss; console.log('sets shrink', lc, sc); if (sc < bestH.sc && (!LC || sc < LC.sc)) LC = {lc, sc}; }
const species = FAST && LC ? null : fit(train, LAM), withSets = LC ? fit(train, {...LAM, useSets: true, lc: LC.lc}) : null;
// calibrated log-loss: the page always stretches or shrinks the model's edges by the held-out calibration slope, so
// whether set features help is judged on the odds the page will actually show
const calibOf = (model, games) => { const pts = games.map(([A, B, r, , , , , dA, dB]) => [model.x(A, dA) - model.x(B, dB), r]); let w = 1;
  for (let k = 0; k < 30; k++) { let g = 0, h = 0; for (const [e, r] of pts) { const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-w * e)); g += (p - r) * e; h += p * (1 - p) * e * e; } w -= g / (h || 1); }
  const ll = pts.reduce((a, [e, r]) => { const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-w * e)); return a - (r * Math.log(p) + (1 - r) * Math.log(1 - p)); }, 0) / (pts.length || 1); return {w, ll: +ll.toFixed(5)}; };
// EXPORT_SPLITS=1: also save Pokémon strengths fitted only on older data (the validation-fit part, and all training events),
// so the ratings can be tested on events nothing in them has seen (experiments/rating-fit.mjs)
if (process.env.EXPORT_SPLITS === '1') { const fp = fit(fitPart, LAM), mm = m => Object.fromEntries(mons.map((n, j) => [n, +m.b[j].toFixed(4)]));
  fs.writeFileSync('out/model_splits.json', JSON.stringify({fitPart: mm(fp), train: mm(species)})); console.log('wrote out/model_splits.json'); }
const evS = species ? score(species, test, false) : null, evW = withSets ? score(withSets, test, false) : null, cS = species ? calibOf(species, test) : {ll: 9}, cW = withSets ? calibOf(withSets, test) : null;
const useSets = !!(cW && cW.ll < cS.ll), full = useSets ? withSets : species;
const noSkill = FAST ? full : fit(train, {...LAM, useSkill: false, useSets, lc: LC ? LC.lc : 40}), noPairs = FAST ? full : fit(train, {...LAM, usePairs: false, useSets, lc: LC ? LC.lc : 40}), raw = rawBaseline(train);
// how many held-out events the model calls better than a coin flip (so one big event can't carry the result)
const evWins = (() => { const by = new Map(); for (const g of test) { const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(full.x(g[0], g[7]) - full.x(g[1], g[8])))); const e = by.get(g[3]) || [0, 0]; e[0] += (p > .5) === (g[2] === 1) ? 1 : 0; e[1]++; by.set(g[3], e); }
  const v = [...by.values()]; return {beat_coin_flip: v.filter(([a, n]) => a / n > .5).length, events: v.length}; })();
const ev = {train_games: train.length, test_games: test.length, test_events: cut.size, recency_half_life_days: HALF || null, player_skill_shrink: bestS.sm,
  calibrated_logloss: {species_only: species ? cS.ll : null, with_sets: cW ? cW.ll : null}, events_beating_coin_flip: evWins,
  model_teams_only: score(full, test, false), species_only: evS, with_sets: evW, model_with_known_players: score(full, test, true),
  without_player_skill: score(noSkill, test, false), without_pairs: score(noPairs, test, false), raw_win_rates: score(raw, test, false), coin_flip: {logloss: .6931, acc: .5}};
console.log('held-out test', JSON.stringify(ev));
// ship only if it beats the raw win-rate score on held-out games; then refit on everything
// (Nov 8, foundation plan: stability) judged on the CALIBRATED log-loss, the way the page uses the model (it always scales the
// edges by calib). Raw log-loss failed the Oct 28 dev cut by .0006 while the model picked 56.4% vs 54.9% for raw win rates, and
// an unshipped model makes every grade on the site fall back to the old check overnight.
const calFull = calibOf(full, test), ships = Math.min(ev.model_teams_only.logloss, calFull.ll) < ev.raw_win_rates.logloss;
ev.model_teams_only_calibrated = {logloss: +calFull.ll.toFixed(4), slope: +calFull.w.toFixed(3)};
// TRAIN_ONLY=1 (experiments): fit the shipped-style model on the training events only and write out/model_train.json,
// so Team Scores can be checked on held-out events the model never saw
const TRAIN_ONLY = process.env.TRAIN_ONLY === '1', GF = TRAIN_ONLY ? train : G;
const F = ships ? fit(GF, {...LAM, useSets, lc: LC ? LC.lc : 40}) : null;
if (F && TRAIN_ONLY) { ev.train_only_fit = {...score(F, test, false), calib: +calibOf(F, test).w.toFixed(3), calib_ll: calibOf(F, test).ll}; console.log('train-only fit on held-out', JSON.stringify(ev.train_only_fit)); }
let meanX = 0; if (F) { let n = 0; for (const [A, B, , , , , , dA, dB] of GF) { meanX += F.x(A, dA) + F.x(B, dB); n += 2; } meanX /= n; }
// calibration on the held-out events: how far the train-only model's edges should be stretched or shrunk (1 = right as is)
const calib = (() => { const pts = test.map(([A, B, r, , , , , dA, dB]) => [full.x(A, dA) - full.x(B, dB), r]); let w = 1;
  for (let k = 0; k < 30; k++) { let g = 0, h = 0; for (const [e, r] of pts) { const p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-w * e)); g += (p - r) * e; h += p * (1 - p) * e * e; } w -= g / (h || 1); } return w; })();
// how far a build can move a Pokémon. Down: to its weakest real deck. Up: only as far as the best build that real players
// actually run on 5+ decks (Nov 8 scoreboard: the old 10th-percentile floor and 20-deck ceiling threw away real signal;
// this range scored as well as no cap at all at three date cut-offs); hand-picking the four moves with the biggest numbers
// can't beat that. The page clamps every build to this range, and the real-team spread below uses the same clamp.
const setRange = (() => { if (!F || !useSets) return {}; const vals = new Map(), sig = new Map(), seenTD = new Set();
  for (const [A, B, , , , , , dA, dB] of GF) for (const [t, d] of [[A, dA], [B, dB]]) { const k = t + ',' + d; if (seenTD.has(k) || d == null) continue; seenTD.add(k); const has = new Set(deckF[d]);
    for (const n of teams[t]) { const fj = spFeats.get(n) || []; let v = 0; for (const j of fj) v += F.c[j] * ((has.has(j) ? 1 : 0) - freq[j]);
      if (!vals.has(n)) { vals.set(n, []); sig.set(n, new Map()); } vals.get(n).push(v); const key = fj.filter(j => has.has(j)).join(','); const g = sig.get(n).get(key) || {c: 0, v}; g.c++; sig.get(n).set(key, g); } }
  const out = {}; for (const [n, a] of vals) { if (!spFeats.has(n)) continue; a.sort((x, y) => x - y); const builds = [...sig.get(n).values()], need = 5;
    const common = builds.filter(b => b.c >= need); const top = (common.length ? common : [builds.sort((x, y) => y.c - x.c)[0]]).reduce((m, b) => Math.max(m, b.v), -1e9);
    const lo = a.length ? a[0] : Math.min(0, top); out[n] = [+Math.min(lo, top).toFixed(4), +top.toFixed(4)]; } return out; })();
// tested pairs: two Pokémon brought together on 15+ distinct tournament teams. A team whose pairs are mostly untested gets
// its above-average edge cut: the model knows each Pokémon's results, but not how an untried mix plays together.
// (Only upside is cut; a weak team is not pulled up.)
// Credit = 0.5 + 0.5 × min(1, tested pairs / 6); a quarter of real teams have fewer than 6, most have 10+.
const SUP_K = 6, tested = new Set(), teamSup = new Map();
{ const pcAll = new Map(); for (const m of new Set(GF.flatMap(g => [g[0], g[1]]))) { const t = teams[m]; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 6; j++) { const k = t[i] < t[j] ? t[i] + '|' + t[j] : t[j] + '|' + t[i]; pcAll.set(k, (pcAll.get(k) || 0) + 1); } }
  for (const [k, n] of pcAll) if (n >= 15) tested.add(k); }
const supOf = t => { if (teamSup.has(t)) return teamSup.get(t); const m = teams[t]; let k = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < 6; j++) if (tested.has(m[i] < m[j] ? m[i] + '|' + m[j] : m[j] + '|' + m[i])) k++; teamSup.set(t, k); return k; };
const credit = t => .5 + .5 * Math.min(1, supOf(t) / SUP_K);
// a team's x the way the page computes it: species + pairs + each member's set effect clamped to its real range
const xClamp = (t, d) => { let v = 0; for (const j of tf[t].f) v += F.b[j]; for (const j of tf[t].p) v += F.q[j]; { const tv = teamVec(t, d); for (let k = 0; k < tv.length; k++) v += F.T[k] * tv[k]; } if (!useSets || d == null) return v; const has = new Set(deckF[d]);
  for (const n of teams[t]) { let u = 0; for (const j of (spFeats.get(n) || [])) u += F.c[j] * ((has.has(j) ? 1 : 0) - freq[j]); const r = setRange[n]; v += r ? Math.max(r[0], Math.min(r[1], u)) : u; } return v; };
// the average tournament team, measured the same way
if (F) { let n = 0; meanX = 0; for (const [A, B, , , , , , dA, dB] of GF) { meanX += xClamp(A, dA) + xClamp(B, dB); n += 2; } meanX /= n; }
// the spread of real teams: x for each distinct team + set played in the last 45 days, as 101 percentile points
const dist = (() => { if (!F) return null; const last = GF.reduce((a, g) => evDates[g[3]] > a ? evDates[g[3]] : a, ''), since = new Date(new Date(last).getTime() - 45 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const seenTD = new Set(), xs = []; for (const [A, B, , ev, , , , dA, dB] of GF) { if (evDates[ev] < since) continue; for (const [t, d] of [[A, dA], [B, dB]]) { const k = t + ',' + d; if (seenTD.has(k)) continue; seenTD.add(k); { const dv = xClamp(t, d) - meanX; xs.push(meanX + (dv > 0 ? dv * credit(t) : dv)); } } }
  xs.sort((a, b) => a - b); return {since, n: xs.length, q: Array.from({length: 101}, (_, i) => +xs[Math.round(i / 100 * (xs.length - 1))].toFixed(4))}; })();
const out = {reg: curReg, updated: new Date().toISOString(), ships, shrink: K, setShrink: useSets ? LC.lc : null, eval: ev, games: G.length, events: M.events.length,
  note: 'x(team) = sum of mons + sum of pairs + sum over each member\'s set features of coef * (has − setFreq) ("Mon@Item", "Mon:Move"); P(A beats B) = 1/(1+exp(-calib*(x(A)-x(B)))). Player skill removed. meanX = average tournament team; dist = percentiles of x over real teams in the last 45 days.',
  meanX: +meanX.toFixed(4), v: 3, half: HALF || null, teamHalf: TEAM_HALF != null ? TEAM_HALF : (HALF || null), ...(TEAM_PRIOR ? {teamPrior: TEAM_PRIOR} : {}), center: !!useSets, calib: +calib.toFixed(3), dist, setRange, supK: SUP_K, tested: [...tested].sort(),
  setFreq: F && useSets ? Object.fromEntries(setNames.map((k, j) => [k, +freq[j].toFixed(3)])) : {},
  // team-wide basics (v6): added to x when the team lacks the basic
  team: F && TEAMF.length ? Object.fromEntries(TEAMF.map((k, i) => [k, +F.T[i].toFixed(4)])) : {},
  mons: F ? Object.fromEntries(mons.map((n, j) => [n, +F.b[j].toFixed(4)])) : {},
  pairs: F ? Object.fromEntries(pairs.map((k, j) => [k, +F.q[j].toFixed(4)]).filter(([, v]) => Math.abs(v) >= .005)) : {},
  // spread of each Pokémon's and each shipped pair's number (standard deviation, model units), for the grade's likely range
  monSd: F ? Object.fromEntries(mons.map((n, j) => [n, +Math.sqrt(F.vb[j]).toFixed(3)])) : {},
  pairSd0: F ? +Math.sqrt(1 / (2 * LAM.lp)).toFixed(3) : null, // spread of a pair with no shipped number (little or no evidence)
  pairSd: F ? Object.fromEntries(pairs.map((k, j) => [k, Math.abs(F.q[j]) >= .005 ? +Math.sqrt(F.vq[j]).toFixed(3) : null]).filter(([, v]) => v != null)) : {},
  sets: F && useSets ? Object.fromEntries(setNames.map((k, j) => [k, +F.c[j].toFixed(4)])) : {},
  // the "same players without it" test for each Pokémon (pilot.mjs): [games with, win % with, games without, win % without, players]
  pilot: TRAIN_ONLY ? {} : pilotTest(M)};
// never replace a working model with an unshipped one (the daily job keeps yesterday's; update.yml reports the skip)
{ const dest = process.env.MODEL_OUT || (TRAIN_ONLY ? 'out/model_train.json' : 'out/model.json'); let prev = null; try { prev = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(dest, 'utf8')); } catch (e) {}
  const prevReg = prev && (prev.reg || SITE_REG.id);
  if (!ships && prev && prev.ships && prev.mons && prevReg === curReg) { console.log(`model: NOT shipped today, so ${dest} keeps the previous ${curReg} model (from ${String(prev.updated || '').slice(0, 10)})`); process.exit(1); }
  fs.writeFileSync(dest, JSON.stringify(out)); }
console.log(`calibration slope ${calib.toFixed(3)} · real-team spread p50 ${dist && dist.q[50]} p99 ${dist && dist.q[99]}`);
console.log(`model: ${ships ? 'ships' : 'NOT shipped (no better than raw win rates)'}, ${mons.length} Pokémon, ${pairs.length} pairs, ${useSets ? Object.keys(out.sets).length + ' set features' : 'no set features (they did not help)'}, ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);

===== FILE: .github/workflows/update.yml (claude/design6)
name: Update Limitless data
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '7 9 * * *'   # daily, about 5:07 AM New York time in summer, 4:07 AM in winter (GitHub can start it up to an hour late), before NastyPlot's 6:52 refresh
    # Data every 4 hours, READY TO FLIP (Nov 24, game plan #7): these five extra times only do work when the repository variable
    # FAST_DATA is 1 (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions > Variables). Off (missing or anything else): they start and stop at once
    # (shown as "skipped"), so the job stays daily. With the 9:07 run they make 1, 5, 9, 13, 17, 21 UTC.
    - cron: '7 1,5,13,17,21 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:
permissions:
  contents: write   # commit the day's data
  issues: write     # open, refresh or close the one "Daily data problem" issue (last step)
# one run at a time: a 4-hourly run waits for the one before it (the job can take up to 150 minutes) instead of overlapping it
concurrency:
  group: update-data
  cancel-in-progress: false
jobs:
  update:
    # the daily run and runs by hand always; the extra 4-hourly times only with FAST_DATA=1
    if: github.event_name != 'schedule' || github.event.schedule == '7 9 * * *' || vars.FAST_DATA == '1'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 150
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v5
        with:
          node-version: '22'
      # No player names stored anywhere (Sim, Nov 10): every name in the saved data becomes an ID number ("P" + 10 digits,
      # pid.mjs) made with a key kept only in this repository's secrets (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions >
      # NP_PID_KEY; Sim saved it as The_NP_PID_Key, so both names are read). The first run converts everything saved before the switch; after that it finds nothing to change.
      # Without the key this step fails and the job stops, so yesterday's data stays and no name is ever saved.
      - run: node scrub-names.mjs
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
      # which regulation the newest events are played under (out/reg.json): every step below counts only that regulation
      - run: node reg.mjs || node skip.mjs "regulation check skipped"
      - run: node replays.mjs || node skip.mjs "replays skipped"   # first, so today's bring/lead rates reach the ratings (was a day behind)
      # open-team-sheet single games (Showdown's best-of-3 Champions format, each game counted once, with full sets and ratings;
      # Focus plan, Sim Nov 10: grow the only data that has all three). Optional: if it fails, yesterday's file stays.
      - run: REPLAY_FMT=bo3 node replays.mjs || node skip.mjs "open-sheet replays skipped"
      # team preview practice: a few hundred real games and what strong players brought (out/practice.json, page key meta/practice)
      - run: node practice.mjs || node skip.mjs "practice skipped"
      - run: node update.mjs
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
          MAX_FETCH: ${{ github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' && '400' || '40' }}
      # Official events (Reg M-C Regionals/Internationals, Masters top 64 open team sheets): Victory Road's event pages and
      # VR Pastes, checked against Liquipedia's API. Polite: one request a second per site, our User-Agent, only new or
      # edited events, every paste read once. If anything fails or disagrees with yesterday's file, out/official.json is kept.
      # (RK9 was read here until Nov 1; its terms, rk9.gg/terms 5.3, forbid automated extraction, so rk9.mjs and
      # rk9-field.mjs were removed. The whole-field table they made is kept frozen in out/official_perf.json.)
      - run: node official.mjs || node skip.mjs "official events skipped (previous out/official.json kept)"
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
      - run: node events.mjs || node skip.mjs "events skipped"
      # this week vs last week from the team sheets already on disk (out/weekly.json; no network)
      - run: node weekly.mjs || node skip.mjs "weekly digest skipped"
      # upcoming official VGC events from Victory Road's season calendar (out/calendar.json; keeps yesterday's file on failure)
      - run: node calendar.mjs || node skip.mjs "calendar skipped"
      # The Champions Dispatch and Mystery Gifts (Nov 18, switch blocker 5): new headlines from the official in-game news feed go into
      # app/src/data/post.js and out/post.json, the page's daily document meta/post, so News updates without a republish (headline, date,
      # type and link only; gifts are only suggested, in out/post-suggest.json). A feed that fails or changed shape keeps yesterday's list.
      - run: node post-news.mjs || node skip.mjs "news feed skipped (yesterday's list kept)"
      # then the list is checked (every link https, dates parse, codes cited; out/post.json fits one document); if it fails, yesterday's
      # post.js and out/post.json are put back, so a bad day never reaches the page and the rest of the day's data is still saved
      - run: node experiments/post-data-test.mjs || { git checkout -- app/src/data/post.js; git checkout -- out/post.json 2>/dev/null || rm -f out/post.json; node skip.mjs "news check skipped (the list failed post-data-test; yesterday's list put back)"; }
      - run: node model.mjs || node skip.mjs "model skipped"
      # each six's own record with player skill and opponents taken out, added to out/model.json for grade v2 (Nov 7)
      - run: node six.mjs || node skip.mjs "six records skipped"
      # which Pokémon beat which in real games, added to out/model.json for Plan a Match (Nov 8)
      - run: node ctr.mjs || node skip.mjs "learned matchups skipped"
      # Phase 4 (Nov 9): if today's model is broken, less accurate on held-out games or reorders the most-played sixes, keep
      # yesterday's (committed) model and flag it in the run and in out/skips.json
      - run: node model-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs "new model skipped (model guard kept yesterday's model; reasons in this step's log)"
      # which Pokémon win more when they lead, from ladder replays, for Battle's lead pick (Nov 8)
      - run: node leads.mjs || node skip.mjs "lead values skipped"
      - run: node ladder.mjs || node skip.mjs "ladder stats skipped"
      # other sites' numbers: Showdowntier, Pikalytics (rating inputs, tier lists, cross-check)
      - run: node sources.mjs || node skip.mjs "outside sources skipped"
      # op.gg read PAUSED (Nov 18): op.gg's Terms of Use ban automated collection and copying without written permission
      # (docs/rebuild/research/opgg-terms-check-nov18.md). The last good meta_ingame.json stays; the browser install stays
      # because the ratings and win snapshots below use it. To resume: put "&& node opgg.mjs" back once op.gg agrees in writing.
      - run: (npm install --no-save --no-package-lock playwright@1.49.1 && npx playwright install --with-deps chromium) || node skip.mjs "browser install skipped"
      # the ratings' daily inputs (all of out/perf.json) for the morning refresh; checked by health.mjs
      # perf.mjs copies other files into perf.json: guard them first, so it never copies the last regulation's (Scout's review, Nov 24)
      - run: node reg-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs "regulation guard (before perf) failed"
      - run: node perf.mjs || node skip.mjs "perf skipped"
      # thin-data counts for the active regulation (out/regstate.json)
      - run: node regstate.mjs || node skip.mjs "regstate skipped"
      # our numbers against the outside sources (out/crosscheck.json; summary and warnings in the Data health step)
      - run: node crosscheck.mjs || node skip.mjs "crosscheck skipped"
      - run: node ratings-snapshot.mjs || node skip.mjs "ratings snapshot skipped"
      # the checklist's 'recent winners' numbers, worked out once here instead of on every visitor's phone (code review B-C1, Nov 16;
      # stored in out/events.json as ckwin; the page uses them only when their stamps match today's data)
      - run: node ckwin-snapshot.mjs || node skip.mjs "checklist numbers skipped (the page works them out itself)"
      - run: node scoreboard.mjs || node skip.mjs "scoreboard skipped"
      # the ratings' track record: each saved snapshot against the events played after it (out/track.json, page meta/track)
      - run: node track.mjs || node skip.mjs "track record skipped"
      # last guard before saving: nothing that was a name may be committed (converts any straggler, then checks)
      - name: No player names
        run: node scrub-names.mjs && node scrub-names.mjs --check
        env:
          NP_PID_KEY: ${{ secrets.NP_PID_KEY || secrets.THE_NP_PID_KEY }}
      # page storage limit (Nov 10; after the name guard, so the parts are made from files with IDs only): any document the morning refresh writes that is over 200 KB is split into two parts in
      # out/split/ (nothing dropped; the page joins them back). The refresh writes those parts instead of the whole file.
      # switch-day regulation guard (Nov 24, HH): any out/ file stamped with another regulation (or unstamped after a switch) is
      # replaced by its empty state, so Commit never ships two regulations side by side. Never fails the run. Runs BEFORE
      # split-docs.mjs, so out/split/ is built from the guarded files.
      - name: Regulation guard
        run: node reg-guard.mjs || node skip.mjs "regulation guard failed"
      - run: node split-docs.mjs || node skip.mjs "page-size split skipped (a document too big even in two parts)"
      - name: Commit
        run: |
          git config user.name "pokelair-bot"
          git config user.email "pokelair-bot@users.noreply.github.com"
          git add -A
          git diff --cached --quiet && exit 0
          git commit -m "Limitless data $(date -u +%F)"
          # code can change on main while the job runs; replay the data commit on top and retry
          for i in 1 2 3; do git pull --rebase origin main && git push && exit 0; sleep 10; done
          exit 1
      # Data health gate: runs after the commit, so whatever good data the day produced is already saved. It fails the run
      # (GitHub emails the owner about failed runs) when a file the page relies on is missing, broken, or past its age limit
      # (limits in health.mjs: daily sources 2 days, so one flaky day never alerts), and writes a table to the job summary.
      - name: Data health
        id: health
        if: always()
        env:
          HEALTH_REPORT: ${{ runner.temp }}/health.md
        run: node health.mjs
      # One "Daily data problem" issue, reused: opened when the run fails, its text refreshed each failing day (a comment only
      # when the list of problems changes, so a repeat doesn't email again), closed with a note on the first healthy run.
      - name: Daily data problem issue
        if: always()
        uses: actions/github-script@v8
        env:
          HEALTH_REPORT: ${{ runner.temp }}/health.md
          JOB_STATUS: ${{ job.status }}
          HEALTH_OUTCOME: ${{ steps.health.outcome }}
        with:
          script: |
            const fs = require('fs'), title = 'Daily data problem';
            let report = ''; try { report = fs.readFileSync(process.env.HEALTH_REPORT, 'utf8'); } catch (e) {}
            const run = `${context.serverUrl}/${context.repo.owner}/${context.repo.repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}`;
            const healthy = process.env.JOB_STATUS === 'success' && process.env.HEALTH_OUTCOME === 'success';
            const open = (await github.paginate(github.rest.issues.listForRepo, {...context.repo, state: 'open', per_page: 100}))
              .filter(i => !i.pull_request && i.title === title);
            const issue = open[0];
            if (healthy) {
              if (issue) { await github.rest.issues.createComment({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, body: `Fixed: today's data check passed. ${run}`});
                await github.rest.issues.update({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, state: 'closed', state_reason: 'completed'}); }
              return; }
            const lead = process.env.HEALTH_OUTCOME === 'success' ? `A step of the daily job failed (see the run log), although every data file is still within its age limit.\n\n` : '';
            const body = `${lead}${report || 'The data check did not run; see the run log.'}\n\nLast checked: ${run}`;
            const list = b => (String(b || '').match(/^- .*$/gm) || []).join('\n');
            if (!issue) { await github.rest.issues.create({...context.repo, title, body}); return; }
            const changed = list(issue.body) !== list(body);
            await github.rest.issues.update({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, body});
            if (changed) await github.rest.issues.createComment({...context.repo, issue_number: issue.number, body: `The list of problems changed today. ${run}`});

===== FILE: .github/workflows/md-watch.yml (claude/design6)
name: Reg M-D watcher and Limitless alarm
# md-watch.mjs (Nov 24): once a day, after the daily data job, and on demand. Read-only: it never changes the site or the data.
#   - "Reg M-D spotted: <where>" (Showdown formats, Limitless tournaments, official news): opened ONCE per place; never a duplicate
#     (a title that exists, open or closed, is skipped).
#   - "Limitless data paused since <date>": opened when Limitless doesn't answer or has no new event for 2+ days; its text refreshed
#     while it lasts (a comment only when the date changes); closed with a note when data flows again.
# Run it by hand (Actions > this workflow > Run workflow) with "dry" ticked to see the issue texts in the log without opening anything;
# fake_found / fake_limitless pretend a sighting or an outage (the dry runs in docs/rebuild/test-runs/watcher-nov24.md).
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '23 13 * * *'   # daily, after the data job (9:07 UTC) and its refresh
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      dry:
        description: 'Dry run: print the issues it would open or close, open nothing'
        type: boolean
        default: true
      fake_found:
        description: 'Pretend a sighting (showdown,limitless,news), for testing'
        default: ''
      fake_limitless:
        description: 'Pretend Limitless is down or stale (down | stale), for testing'
        default: ''
concurrency:
  group: md-watch   # a manual run and the daily run never overlap, so an issue can't open twice (code review, Nov 24)
  cancel-in-progress: false
permissions:
  contents: read
  issues: write
jobs:
  watch:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v5
        with:
          node-version: '22'
      - name: Look for Reg M-D and check Limitless
        env:
          WATCH_REPORT: ${{ runner.temp }}/watch.json
          WATCH_DRY: ${{ inputs.dry && '1' || '' }}
          WATCH_FAKE_FOUND: ${{ inputs.fake_found }}
          WATCH_FAKE_LIMITLESS: ${{ inputs.fake_limitless }}
        run: node md-watch.mjs
      - name: Issues
        if: ${{ !inputs.dry && !inputs.fake_found && !inputs.fake_limitless }}   # a faked run never opens a real issue (code review, Nov 24)
        uses: actions/github-script@v8
        env:
          WATCH_REPORT: ${{ runner.temp }}/watch.json
        with:
          script: |
            const fs = require('fs'), R = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.env.WATCH_REPORT, 'utf8'));
            const run = `${context.serverUrl}/${context.repo.owner}/${context.repo.repo}/actions/runs/${context.runId}`;
            const all = (await github.paginate(github.rest.issues.listForRepo, {...context.repo, state: 'all', per_page: 100})).filter(i => !i.pull_request);
            // 1. each sighting once, ever
            for (const s of R.spotted || []) { if (all.some(i => i.title === s.title)) continue;
              await github.rest.issues.create({...context.repo, title: s.title, body: `${s.body}\n\nRun: ${run}`}); }
            // 2. the Limitless alarm: one open issue at most
            if (!R.next) return;   // the watcher stopped early: it never checked Limitless, so it must not close the alarm
            const open = all.filter(i => i.state === 'open' && i.title.startsWith('Limitless data paused since '));
            if (!R.paused) { for (const i of open) { await github.rest.issues.createComment({...context.repo, issue_number: i.number, body: `Data flows again. ${run}`});
                await github.rest.issues.update({...context.repo, issue_number: i.number, state: 'closed', state_reason: 'completed'}); } return; }
            const body = `${R.paused.body}\n\nLast checked: ${run}`;
            if (!open.length) { await github.rest.issues.create({...context.repo, title: R.paused.title, body}); return; }
            const i = open[0], moved = i.title !== R.paused.title;
            await github.rest.issues.update({...context.repo, issue_number: i.number, title: R.paused.title, body});
            if (moved) await github.rest.issues.createComment({...context.repo, issue_number: i.number, body: `Still paused; now: ${R.paused.title.slice(28)}. ${run}`});

===== FILE: .github/workflows/promote.yml (claude/design6)
name: Promote to live
# Protect the live site (Sim, Nov 9, "Process fixes" step 2). The live page's data refresh (a scheduled task, ~6:52 AM New York)
# reads the data files from the `live` branch, not from main. This workflow moves `live` to a main commit ONLY after the full
# test run ("Test NastyPlot page": page tests, threat/lead/grade checks and the never-get-worse accuracy gate) passed on exactly
# that commit. If the tests fail, `live` stays on the last version that passed: players keep yesterday's data, and GitHub
# emails the owner about the failed test run. (Nov 9: tests went red at 11:42 UTC and the refresh copied the new data at 11:52.)
on:
  workflow_run:
    workflows: ["Test NastyPlot page"]
    types: [completed]
    branches: [main]
permissions:
  contents: write
jobs:
  promote:
    if: ${{ github.event.workflow_run.conclusion == 'success' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - name: Move live to the tested commit (only forward)
        run: |
          SHA="${{ github.event.workflow_run.head_sha }}"
          git fetch origin live || true
          # never move live backwards: only if the tested commit is newer than (contains) what live points to
          if git rev-parse --verify -q origin/live >/dev/null && ! git merge-base --is-ancestor origin/live "$SHA"; then
            echo "live is already at a newer tested commit; nothing to do"; exit 0; fi
          git push origin "$SHA:refs/heads/live"
          echo "live -> $SHA"

===== FILE: .github/workflows/test.yml (claude/design6)

on:

# Changes land as a few commits in a row; only the newest one is tested. An older run still going is cancelled

  # Safari's engine (code review C-I8, Nov 17: Sim tests on iPhone and iPad; the suites ran in Chromium only). The My Team group
  # on WebKit. Reported, not blocking yet. SUG-05 (Nov 18): failed 4 of 5 here; the test read the 4 s toast after a settle

    if: github.event_name != 'workflow_run'

  # Code tests (Sim, Nov 9, "split the tests"): every check runs on a FROZEN copy of the data (the out/ folder of commit

  # v2-minors (the small fixes' checks, 18-21 min in one go) as three parallel parts; each runs its share once (PART in the file's header).
  v2-minors:
    name: new site small checks (v2-minors ${{ matrix.part }})
    # Sim, Nov 18 (Q6 A): the daily data run (workflow_run) skips this frozen-data new-site job, so a flake here can't hold back the
    # day's data; every code push still runs it and it still blocks there. Today's data on the new site is checked by v2-today (data-checks).
    if: github.event_name != 'workflow_run'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 35
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        part: [main1, main2, groups]
    steps:
      - name: Wait for the rest of a multi-commit change
        run: sleep 120
      - uses: actions/checkout@v5
      - name: Use the frozen test data
        env:
          TEST_DATA: 0c992f6977bc1cecc4e03eb269969a82b99681cf
        run: |
          git fetch --depth=1 origin $TEST_DATA
          git checkout $TEST_DATA -- out/
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v5
        with:
          node-version: '22'
      - run: npm ci && npx playwright install --with-deps chromium
      - run: node app/build.mjs --v2 --preview-data
      - run: PART=${{ matrix.part }} PAGE=app/dist/v2.html node app/test/v2-minors.mjs
  # Data checks (Sim, Nov 9): the checks that judge results, run on TODAY's data. Red here means today's data changed a

      # the new site on today's data (switch readiness Tests 3, Nov 18): built as it goes live (--v2 --public --split), its data store

// A daily data document can be stored in two parts (meta/<k> and meta/<k>_2) when it grows past the per-document size limit
