===== FILE: app/src/app/16-outside-performance-data.js (claude/design6)
// ---------- Outside performance data: raw source tables -> rating inputs ----------
// The daily refresh writes the raw tables to meta/perf exactly as each source prints them; the snapshot built into the
// page (PERF_RAW) has the same shape. Names are matched to the roster here, and Mega usage is split out of base names.
const PZ_FORM = {'Hisuian Form': 'Hisui', 'Alolan Form': 'Alola', 'Galarian Form': 'Galar', 'Female': 'F', 'Dusk Form': 'Dusk', 'Midnight Form': 'Midnight', 'Low Key Form': 'Low-Key',
  'Paldean Form (Aqua Breed)': 'Paldea-Aqua', 'Paldean Form (Blaze Breed)': 'Paldea-Blaze', 'Paldean Form (Combat Breed)': 'Paldea-Combat', 'Small Variety': 'Small', 'Large Variety': 'Large',
  'Jumbo Variety': 'Super', 'Yellow Plumage': 'Yellow', 'Family of Four': '', 'Fancy Pattern': '', 'Male': 'M'};
const PZ_ROTOM = {'Wash Rotom': 'Rotom-Wash', 'Heat Rotom': 'Rotom-Heat', 'Frost Rotom': 'Rotom-Frost', 'Fan Rotom': 'Rotom-Fan', 'Mow Rotom': 'Rotom-Mow'};
function srcName(raw) { let n = String(raw || '').trim(); if (!n) return null; if (byName.has(n)) return n;
  const mega = /^Mega /.test(n); n = n.replace(/^Mega /, '');
  const m = n.match(/^(.*?) \[(.*)\]$/); if (m) { const f = PZ_FORM[m[2]]; n = f ? m[1] + '-' + f : m[1]; }
  if (PZ_ROTOM[n]) n = PZ_ROTOM[n];
  if (n === 'Floette' && !mega) n = 'Floette-Eternal';
  if (mega) { const z = n.match(/^(.*) ([XYZ])$/); n = z ? `${z[1]}-Mega-${z[2]}` : n + '-Mega'; }
  // Megas of gendered species: "Mega Meowstic" alone means the female Mega (the one Limitless shows in use)
  if (mega && n === 'Meowstic-Mega') n = 'Meowstic-F-Mega';
  // A plain name only ever maps to a plain form ("Meowstic-F" used to pick up Meowstic-F-Mega here)
  for (const x of [n, n.replace(/'/g, '’'), n.replace(/’/g, "'"), ...(mega ? [n.replace(/-Mega$/, '') + '-Mega'] : [])]) if (byName.has(x) && (mega || !byName.get(x).m)) return x;
  // cosmetic forms (Sinistcha-Masterpiece, Maushold-Four, Vivillon-Pokeball) count as the base Pokémon
  const base = n.split('-')[0]; if (!mega && byName.has(base) && !byName.get(base).m) return base;
  return null; }
// op.gg names: "Alolan Ninetales", "Indeedee (Female)", "Heat Rotom". A name listed twice means two forms; the first is the Hisuian one.
const OP_REGION = {Alolan: 'Alola', Galarian: 'Galar', Hisuian: 'Hisui', Paldean: 'Paldea'};
function opggNames(list) { const seen = new Set(), out = [];
  for (const x0 of list || []) { let x = String(x0 || '').trim(), n = null; const m = x.match(/^(Alolan|Galarian|Hisuian|Paldean) (.*)$/); let reg = null; if (m) { reg = OP_REGION[m[1]]; x = m[2]; }
    const f = x.match(/^(.*?) \((.*)\)$/); let base = f ? f[1] : x, form = f ? f[2] : '';
    if (reg === 'Paldea' && /Breed/.test(form)) n = 'Tauros-Paldea-' + form.split(' ')[0];
    else if (reg) n = base + '-' + reg;
    else if (/^Female$/.test(form)) n = base + '-F';
    else if (/Dusk|Midnight/.test(form)) n = base + '-' + form.split(' ')[0];
    else if (/Low Key/.test(form)) n = base + '-Low-Key';
    else if (/Yellow Plumage/.test(form)) n = base + '-Yellow';
    else if (/Super Size/.test(form)) n = base + '-Super'; else if (/Small Size/.test(form)) n = base + '-Small'; else if (/Large Size/.test(form)) n = base + '-Large';
    else n = base;
    if (!reg && !form && ['Arcanine', 'Zoroark', 'Typhlosion', 'Samurott', 'Goodra', 'Decidueye', 'Avalugg'].includes(base) && !seen.has(base)) { seen.add(base); n = base + '-Hisui'; }
    out.push(srcName(n) || srcName(x0)); }
  return out; }
// code review B14: another site's list is shown only with an https:// link and a plain key; the name is trimmed
function outsideClean(o) { const lists = o.lists.filter(L => L && typeof L === 'object' && typeof L.url === 'string' && /^https:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+$/.test(L.url) && L.url.length <= 300 && typeof L.k === 'string' && /^[\w-]{1,40}$/.test(L.k))
  .map(L => Object.assign({}, L, {name: String(L.name || '').slice(0, 60)})); return lists.length ? Object.assign({}, o, {lists}) : null; }
function perfFromRaw(raw) {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return null;
  const num = v => (typeof v === 'number' && isFinite(v)) ? v : +v;
  // tournament tables list base names that include their Mega users; split the plain form out
  // split: the table already lists Megas on their own rows (our daily Limitless table), so nothing is subtracted
  const pz = (rows, megas, split) => { const D = {}; for (const r of [...(rows || []), ...(megas || [])]) { if (!Array.isArray(r)) continue; const n = srcName(r[0]); if (!n) continue;
      const u = num(r[1]), w = num(r[2]), mt = num(r[3]); if (![u, w, mt].every(isFinite)) continue;
      // two rows for one Pokémon (cosmetic forms like Sinistcha-Masterpiece) add up instead of overwriting
      const o = D[n]; D[n] = o ? [+(o[0] + u).toFixed(2), o[2] + mt > 0 ? +((o[1] * o[2] + w * mt) / (o[2] + mt)).toFixed(1) : w, o[2] + mt] : [u, w, mt]; }
    if (!split) for (const p of ROSTER) { if (p.m || !D[p.n] || (p.n !== p.b && p.n !== 'Floette-Eternal')) continue;
      const ms = ROSTER.filter(q => q.m && q.b === p.b && D[q.n]); if (!ms.length) continue;
      let [u, w, mt] = D[p.n], wins = w / 100 * mt; for (const q of ms) { u -= D[q.n][0]; mt -= D[q.n][2]; wins -= D[q.n][1] / 100 * D[q.n][2]; }
      D[p.n] = [Math.max(0, +u.toFixed(2)), mt > 0 ? +Math.max(0, Math.min(100, wins / mt * 100)).toFixed(1) : 0, Math.max(0, Math.round(mt))]; }
    return D; };
  const usageMap = rows => { const D = {}; for (const r of rows || []) { if (!Array.isArray(r)) continue; const n = srcName(r[0]); if (n && isFinite(num(r[1]))) D[n] = +((D[n] || 0) + num(r[1])).toFixed(2); } return D; };
  const smogon = usageMap(raw.smogon && raw.smogon.rows);
  // ladder and in-game rows use species names that cover the plain form and its Megas together ("Garchomp" includes
  // Mega Garchomp and Mega Garchomp Z). Share each row out by how strong players split the species (Smogon's form usage,
  // else tournament usage), so a popular Mega isn't scored as unused and its base form isn't credited with its games.
  const tourU = (() => { const D = {}; for (const r of [...((raw.tour && raw.tour.rows) || []), ...((raw.tour && raw.tour.megas) || [])]) { if (Array.isArray(r)) { const n = srcName(r[0]); if (n && isFinite(num(r[1]))) D[n] = (D[n] || 0) + num(r[1]); } } return D; })();
  // A species row covers the plain form and its Megas. Regional forms (Alolan Raichu, Galarian Slowbro) have no Mega of
  // their own, so they are never split with the Kanto form's Megas.
  const formsOf = n => { const q = byName.get(n); if (!q) return [[n, 1]]; if (!q.m && q.n !== q.b && q.n !== 'Floette-Eternal') return [[n, 1]];
    const fam = ROSTER.filter(f => f.b === q.b && (f.n === q.n || f.m));
    for (const U of [smogon, tourU]) { const tot = fam.reduce((a, f) => a + (U[f.n] || 0), 0); if (tot > 0) return fam.filter(f => (U[f.n] || 0) > 0).map(f => [f.n, (U[f.n] || 0) / tot]); }
    return [[n, 1]]; };
  const ladder = {}; for (const r of (raw.ladder && raw.ladder.rows) || []) { if (!Array.isArray(r)) continue; const n = srcName(r[0]); if (!n) continue; const v = [num(r[1]), num(r[2]), num(r[3])]; if (!v.every(isFinite)) continue;
    for (const [k, sh] of formsOf(n)) { const o = ladder[k]; ladder[k] = o ? [+(o[0] + v[0] * sh).toFixed(2), o[1], o[2]] : [+(v[0] * sh).toFixed(2), v[1], v[2]]; } }
  // in-game ranked doubles (op.gg): a full rank order, no percentages. Turn rank r into a usage figure by giving it the r-th
  // biggest usage on the 1760+ ladder (same shape of curve), then hand base names to the form strong players use.
  const ingame = {}, ig = opggNames(raw.ingame && raw.ingame.names), curve = Object.values(smogon).sort((a, b) => b - a);
  const igSeen = new Set(); ig.forEach((n, i) => { if (!n || igSeen.has(n)) return; igSeen.add(n); const v = curve.length ? (curve[i] != null ? curve[i] : curve[curve.length - 1] * Math.pow(.97, i - curve.length + 1)) : 0;
    for (const [k, sh] of formsOf(n)) ingame[k] = (ingame[k] || 0) + v * sh; });
  const out = {updated: raw.updated || '', doubles: pz(raw.tour && raw.tour.rows, raw.tour && raw.tour.megas, !!(raw.tour && raw.tour.split)), official: pz(raw.official && raw.official.rows, raw.official && raw.official.megas, !!(raw.official && raw.official.split)),
    adj: (() => { const D = {}; for (const r of (raw.adj && raw.adj.rows) || []) { if (!Array.isArray(r)) continue; const n = srcName(r[0]); const b = num(r[1]), g = num(r[2]); if (!n || !isFinite(b) || !isFinite(g)) continue;
      const t = num(r[3]) || 0, w = isFinite(num(r[4])) ? num(r[4]) : null, o = D[n];
      // two rows for one Pokémon ("Floette|M" and "Eternal Flower Floette|M"): weighted merge by games
      D[n] = !o ? [b, g, t, w] : [+((o[0] * o[1] + b * g) / (o[1] + g || 1)).toFixed(3), o[1] + g, o[2] + t, o[3] == null || w == null ? (o[3] ?? w) : +((o[3] * o[1] + w * g) / (o[1] + g || 1)).toFixed(1)]; } return D; })(),
    ladder, smogon, ingame, ingameRank: (() => { const R = {}; ig.forEach((n, i) => { if (n) for (const [k] of formsOf(n)) if (!R[k]) R[k] = i + 1; }); return R; })(), ingameTotal: ig.length,
    sources: {tour: raw.tour && raw.tour.label, official: raw.official && raw.official.label, ladder: raw.ladder && raw.ladder.label, smogon: raw.smogon && raw.smogon.label, adj: raw.adj && raw.adj.label, ingame: raw.ingame && raw.ingame.label},
    // the date of each source's data, for the sheet ("Oct 30", "Sep 2026"), and the in-game season
    frozenOfficial: !!(raw.official && raw.official.frozen),
    dates: Object.fromEntries(PERF_KEYS.map(k => [k, perfDate(raw, k)]).filter(x => x[1])), season: (raw.ingame && (raw.ingame.season || ((raw.ingame.label || '').match(/season ([A-Z]-?\d+)/) || [])[1])) || '',
    outside: raw.outside && Array.isArray(raw.outside.lists) && raw.outside.data && typeof raw.outside.data === 'object' ? outsideClean(raw.outside) : null};
  return Object.keys(out.doubles).length >= 20 ? out : null;
}
// Outdated numbers are wrong numbers: every part of the outside data carries the date of its data, and a part older than its
// source's cadence is left out (daily sources 3 days, op.gg / Showdowntier / tier lists 4, Smogon's monthly stats
// 40 days past the end of their month). The parts are tour, adj, ingame, official, smogon, ladder and outside.
// A part marked frozen (the official-events table, kept from RK9 when its reads stopped on Nov 1: no permitted source has
// every match) is dated by its newest event and shown for 28 days after it, then left out.
// "2026-10-30" -> "Oct 30", "2026-09" -> "Sep 2026" (the one date format used for data dates)
function perfDateText(d) { const M = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'], s = String(d || ''); let m;
  if ((m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/))) return `${M[+m[2] - 1]} ${+m[3]}`; if ((m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})$/))) return `${M[+m[2] - 1]} ${m[1]}`; return s; }
/* Nov 7: the backups built into the page (PERF_RAW ratings inputs, RESULTS teams/cores, SETS in-game snapshot) were made
   from Reg M-C. They are only used while M-C is the active regulation; once the data job switches to a newer one, a thin
   first day of new data must not be topped up with the old regulation's numbers (the page says data is rebuilding). */
const BUILTIN_REG = 'M-C';
function builtinOk() { try { const r = META && ((META.perf && META.perf.reg) || META.reg); return !(r && typeof r === 'object' && /^[A-Z]-[A-Z]$/.test(r.id) && r.id !== BUILTIN_REG); } catch (e) { return true; } }
const PERF_KEYS = ['tour', 'adj', 'ingame', 'official', 'smogon', 'ladder', 'outside'], PERF_MAXAGE = {tour: 3, adj: 3, ingame: 4, official: 4, smogon: 40, ladder: 4, outside: 4}, PERF_FROZEN_MAXAGE = 28;
function perfDate(raw, k) { const x = raw && raw[k]; if (!x) return ''; return String(x.updated || raw.updated || '').slice(0, 10); }
function perfAgeDays(d) { const s = String(d || ''); let t;
  if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(s)) { const [y, m] = s.split('-').map(Number); t = Date.UTC(y, m, 1); } else t = Date.parse(s.length === 10 ? s + 'T12:00:00Z' : s);
  return isNaN(t) ? Infinity : (Date.now() - t) / 864e5; }
/* Nov 7: frozen parts (the RK9 official-events table, read before Nov 1) are never used. RK9's terms forbid automated
   extraction, so since Nov 1 the data job leaves it out; the page's built-in snapshot still had it, and topped the ratings up
   with it until 28 days after its newest event. Now it's left out everywhere. */
function perfFreshParts(raw) { const o = {}; if (!raw) return o; for (const k of PERF_KEYS) if (raw[k] && !raw[k].frozen && perfAgeDays(perfDate(raw, k)) <= PERF_MAXAGE[k]) o[k] = raw[k]; return o; } /* frozen parts are left out above, so their age limit never applied (code review A2-M5) */
// The performance data in use: today's refresh (meta/perf), each part only while it is current, topped up with the
// snapshot built into the page (PERF_RAW) where that part of the snapshot is still current. Without the refresh at all
// (no database), the built-in snapshot is used as it is, and its dates are shown.
let perfCache = {key: null, val: null};
function activePerf() { const key = metaVersion + '|' + Math.floor(Date.now() / 864e5); /* the day too: a tab left open keeps no part past its age limit (code review A2-M5) */ if (perfCache.key === key) return perfCache.val;
  let v = null; try { const raw = META.perf; if (raw) { const fresh = Object.assign({}, typeof PERF_RAW === 'object' && builtinOk() ? perfFreshParts(PERF_RAW) : {}, perfFreshParts(raw));
      if (fresh.tour) { for (const k of PERF_KEYS) if (fresh[k] && !fresh[k].updated) fresh[k] = Object.assign({}, fresh[k], {updated: perfDate(raw[k] ? raw : PERF_RAW, k)}); v = perfFromRaw(Object.assign({updated: raw.updated}, fresh)); }
      /* Sim, Nov 15 (Q16): when the refresh is past its age limit but still newer than the built-in snapshot, keep the newest we have
         (its own parts and dates, shown plainly) instead of falling back to older ratings; frozen parts stay out (Nov 7 rule) */
      else if (raw.tour && !raw.tour.frozen && !(typeof PERF_RAW === 'object' && builtinOk() && perfDate(PERF_RAW, 'tour') > perfDate(raw, 'tour'))) {
        const own = {}; for (const k of PERF_KEYS) if (raw[k] && !raw[k].frozen) own[k] = Object.assign({}, raw[k], {updated: raw[k].updated || perfDate(raw, k)});
        v = perfFromRaw(Object.assign({updated: raw.updated}, own)); } } } catch (e) { v = null; }
  if (!v) { try { v = typeof PERF_RAW === 'object' && builtinOk() ? perfFromRaw(PERF_RAW) : null; } catch (e) { v = null; } }
  perfCache = {key, val: v}; return v; }

===== FILE: app/src/app/24-sheet-data.js (claude/design6)
// ---------- the Pokémon info sheet's working-out (R4, Nov 11; shared with the new build) ----------
// Today's sheet (lairSheet, 25-ratings-page.js) draws from these; the new build's sheet draws from the same ones.
// What the data says: one row per source, [label, value, note] (value and note may hold winSpan / pctFmt markup)
function lairEvidence(p) { const r = rating(p), src = r.src || {}, pf0 = activePerf(), ev = [];
    const pt = pilotFor(p); if (pt) ev.push(['Same players test', `${winSpan(pt.w)} wins with it · ${pctFmt(pt.wo)} without`, `the same ${pt.players.toLocaleString()} players: ${pt.g.toLocaleString()} games with it, ${pt.go.toLocaleString()} without. ${pt.d >= 3 ? 'A big gap: the Pokémon itself is winning games' : pt.d >= 1 ? 'Wins a little more than these players usually do' : pt.d > -1 ? 'About the same: these players win as often without it' : 'These players do better without it'}`]);
    // (the model-based "own share of team wins / win chance" row was removed Oct 28 with the other win predictions)
    { const bl = typeof bringLead === 'function' ? bringLead(p) : null; if (bl) ev.push(['Team preview (Showdown replays)', `brought ${pctFmt(bl.b)} · led ${pctFmt(bl.l)} of games`, `when it's on the team · ${bl.g.toLocaleString('en-US')} games${bl.forms ? ' · all forms' : ''}${bl.g < 300 ? ' · few games' : ''} · shown, not scored yet`]); }
    // every row says how recent its numbers are, in one format ("Oct 30", "Sep 2026")
    const dt = k => { const d = pf0 && pf0.dates && pf0.dates[k]; return d ? ' · ' + perfDateText(d) : ''; };
    if (src.tour) ev.push(['Limitless tournaments', `${pctFmt(src.tour[0])} of teams`, `${winSpan(src.tour[1])} wins · ${src.tour[2].toLocaleString()} games${dt('tour')}`]);
    if (src.official) ev.push(['Official VGC events', `${pctFmt(src.official[0])} of teams`, `${pctFmt(src.official[1])} of matches won · ${src.official[2].toLocaleString()} matches · whole field${pf0 && pf0.frozenOfficial ? ` · RK9, read before Nov 1 · frozen, newest event${dt('official').replace(' ·', '')}` : dt('official')}`]);
    const fam = ROSTER.some(q => q.m && q.b === p.b), spn = ((byName.get(p.b) || ROSTER.find(q => q.b === p.b && !q.m) || p).d || '').replace(/\s*\(.*\)$/, '').replace(/^Mega /, ''); // Floette has no plain entry (only Eternal and Mega)
    if (src.ingame != null) ev.push(['In-game Ranked Doubles', `#${src.ingame} most used`, `op.gg${pf0 && pf0.season ? ', season ' + esc(pf0.season) : ''}${fam ? ` · ${esc(spn)}, all forms (shared by form)` : ''}${dt('ingame')}`]);
    if (src.smogon != null) ev.push(['Showdown 1760+', `${pctFmt(src.smogon)} of teams`, `Smogon usage stats${dt('smogon')}`]);
    if (src.ladder) ev.push(['Showdown ladder', `${pctFmt(src.ladder[0])} of teams`, `${winSpan(src.ladder[1])} wins · avg player ${src.ladder[2]}${fam ? ` · ${esc(spn)} total, shared by form` : ''} · Showdowntier, 14 days${dt('ladder')}`]);
  return ev; }
// Why it rates N: the bars, [label, value 0-100 or null, note], in the order the sheet shows them. Which bars depends on
// what the rating is built from: tournament top cuts (r.parts and r.tc), a win model (r.win), or results + profile.
function lairBars(p) { const r = rating(p);
  if (r.parts && r.tc) return [['Top-cut presence', r.parts.top, `on ${pctFmt(r.tc.top)} of top-cut teams`], ['Picked by strong players', r.parts.rw, `on ${pctFmt(r.tc.rw)} of teams, strong players counted more`],
    ...(r.parts.lift != null ? [['Converts to top cut', r.parts.lift, `${Math.abs(r.tc.lift - 1) < .005 ? 'makes top cut as often as' : `makes top cut ${Math.round(Math.abs(r.tc.lift - 1) * 100)}% ${r.tc.lift > 1 ? 'more' : 'less'} often than`} its usage predicts`]] : []),
    ['Win rate', r.parts.wr, r.tc.g < 30 ? `only ${Math.round(r.tc.g)} games so far` : `${winFmt(r.tc.wr)} wins, recent games weighted${r.tc.g < 800 ? ' · few games' : ''}`]];
  if (!r.tc && r.parts === null) return [];
  if (r.win != null) return [['Winning', r.win, r.conf < .4 ? 'few games' : ''], ['Picked by strong players', r.popAll], ['Stats & typing', r.intrinsic]];
  return [['Real results', r.evidence, r.conf < .4 ? 'small sample' : ''], ['Power', r.power], ['Typing', r.typing], ['Tools', r.tools]]; }
// Typical set: the nature(s) and stat points most players run (Sim, Nov 6)
function lairSetBits(p) { let na = null, cs = null; try { const st = setFor(p); na = st.na && st.na.length ? st.na.slice(0, 2).map(x => x[0]) : null; } catch (e) {} try { cs = commonSpread(p); } catch (e) {}
  if (!na && cs && cs.nat) na = [cs.nat];
  // Sim, Nov 13 (Natures Q1 B): the nature shown first is the complete spread's, so it matches the points beside it (w); the next most run follows
  if (cs && cs.nat && na) na = [cs.nat, ...na.filter(x => x !== cs.nat)].slice(0, 2); const L = ['HP', 'Atk', 'Def', 'SpA', 'SpD', 'Spe'], K = ['hp', 'atk', 'def', 'spa', 'spd', 'spe'];
  const w = cs ? K.map((k, j) => [cs[k], L[j]]).filter(x => x[0] > 0).sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]).map(x => `${x[0]}\u00a0${x[1]}`).join(', ') : '';
  return {na, w}; }

// ---------- ratings: tiers, rank and range (moved from 25-ratings-page.js, Nov 11) ----------
const LTIERS = [{k: 'S', min: 80, show: 90, c: '#ffb400', d: 'Top of the format'}, {k: 'A', min: 70, show: 80, c: '#ff6a3d', d: 'Strong, proven picks'}, {k: 'B', min: 60, show: 70, c: '#b45cff', d: 'Solid with the right team'},
  {k: 'C', min: 50, show: 60, c: '#2f9bff', d: 'Niche or team-dependent'}, {k: 'D', min: 35, show: 50, c: '#22b58a', d: 'Needs a specific plan'}, {k: 'F', min: 0, show: 1, c: '#8a90a6', d: 'Rarely worth a slot'}];
const lTier = t => LTIERS.find(x => t >= x.min) || LTIERS[LTIERS.length - 1];
function lairRows() { const R = ratings();
  return ROSTER.map(p => ({p, r: R.get(p.n)})).filter(x => x.r).sort((a, b) => b.r.total - a.r.total || a.p.d.localeCompare(b.p.d)); }
/* Rank next to the score (Nov 2): position among the Pokémon WITH results (a placeholder rating has no rank); ties share
   a rank (1 + how many rate strictly higher). Ties are judged on the SHOWN rating (RS): two Pokémon both showing 97 are both #1
   (player review 3b, Nov 17: the raw score made 97/97 rank #1/#2 while 96/96 shared #3). Cached per ratings map. */
let lairRankCache = {R: null, m: null, of: 0};
function lairRank(p) { if (!p) return null; const R = ratings(); if (lairRankCache.R !== R) { const xs = lairRows().filter(x => x.r.tc), m = new Map();
    xs.forEach((x, i) => m.set(x.p.n, i && RS(xs[i - 1].r.total) === RS(x.r.total) ? m.get(xs[i - 1].p.n) : i + 1)); lairRankCache = {R, m, of: xs.length}; }
  const k = lairRankCache.m.get(p.n); return k ? {rank: k, of: lairRankCache.of} : null; }
/* what the N in "of N" counts (Sim, Nov 4): the long form says it in full, 'cap' is the short form for tight spots,
   short (1) is just "#20"; '' when it has no results yet */
const rankTxt = (p, short) => { const k = lairRank(p); return k ? (short === 'cap' ? `#${k.rank} of ${k.of} with results` : short ? `#${k.rank}` : `#${k.rank} of ${k.of} Pokémon with tournament results`) : ''; };
/* the range in words, on the shown scale: "likely 80–88" (never "±4") */
const rRange = r => { const a = RS(Math.max(1, r.total - (r.band || 0))), b = RS(Math.min(99, r.total + (r.band || 0))); return a === b ? `likely ${a}` : `likely ${a}–${b}`; };
/* a tier's shown band in words: "90+", ..., "under 50" */
const tierBand = T => T.k === 'F' ? `under ${LTIERS[LTIERS.length - 2].show}` : `${T.show}+`;
// the same players test (moved from 52-sleepers.js, Nov 11)
function pilotFor(p) { const P = META.model && META.model.pilot, v = P && P[p.n]; if (!Array.isArray(v) || v.length < 5) return null;
  const [g, w, go, wo, players] = v.map(Number); if (![g, w, go, wo, players].every(isFinite)) return null;
  return {g, w, go, wo, players, d: +(w - wo).toFixed(1), dShrunk: (w - wo) * g / (g + 500)}; }

// ---------- the info sheet's Info tab: pure helpers (moved from 67-sheet-tabs.js as SHT.*, which LST takes on; Nov 11) ----------
const SHT = {};
/* stat bar colours by value: cool for low, green for high, magenta for exceptional (never red: red is for opponents) */
SHT.stCol = v => v >= 150 ? '#E048C8' : v >= 120 ? '#3FB618' : v >= 100 ? '#12B5A4' : v >= 80 ? '#2F9BFF' : v >= 50 ? '#5B6BF5' : '#9B6CFF';
SHT.baseForm = p => { if (!p || !p.m) return null; const b = byName.get(p.b); return b && !b.m ? b : ROSTER.find(q => q.b === p.b && !q.m) || null; };
SHT.abDesc = a => (typeof ABIL === 'object' && ABIL[a] ? String(ABIL[a]).replace(/Pokemon/g, 'Pokémon') : '');
/* type effectiveness in words (the Type chart page uses the same groups) */
SHT.word = x => x === 0 ? 'no damage' : x === .25 ? '¼ damage' : x === .5 ? '½ damage' : x === 1 ? 'normal damage' : x === 1.25 ? 'a quarter more damage' : x === 2 ? '2× damage' : x === 4 ? '4× damage' : x + '× damage';
/* the matchups of p's typing, and what each ability it can have changes (ABILITY_MODS in engine/types.js, the same table
   the team checks and the Type chart use) */
SHT.matchups = p => { const g = {q: [], d: [], h: [], qr: [], z: []}, abs = [...new Set(p.a)], only = abs.length === 1 && ABILITY_MODS[abs[0]] ? abs[0] : null;
  /* with only one possible ability (every Mega, Rotom's Levitate ...) the lists already count it; otherwise they are for the typing alone */
  for (const atk of TYPES) { const x = mult(atk, p.t, only, !!only); (x === 0 ? g.z : x >= 4 ? g.q : x >= 2 ? g.d : x <= .25 ? g.qr : x <= .5 ? g.h : []).push(atk); }
  const ab = [...new Set(p.a)].filter(a => ABILITY_MODS[a]).map(a => ({a, ch: TYPES.filter(t => mult(t, p.t, a, true) !== mult(t, p.t, null, false)).map(t => ({t, raw: mult(t, p.t, null, false), x: mult(t, p.t, a, true)}))})).filter(o => o.ch.length);
  return {g, ab, only}; };
/* Speed at level 50 from the app's own Champions maths (39-damage-calc-speed.js): calcStat = floor((base + 20 + points) × nature),
   spdCalc for Scarf / Tailwind / weather abilities, likelySpeed for what players run, outspeedOf for "outspeeds X% of the top 30" */
SHT.speeds = p => { const rows = [], os = (v, tr) => { try { return outspeedOf(v, '', {tr: !!tr}); } catch (e) { return null; } };
  const maxN = calcStat(p, 'Spe', 32, 1.1);
  try { const s = likelySpeed(p, null), ab = abilityOf({p}), v = spdCalc(s.v, s.scarf, ab, '', {});
    rows.push({k: 'run', l: 'What players run', v, note: s.label.replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase()) + (s.scarf ? ', Choice Scarf' : ''), os: os(v), main: true}); } catch (e) {}
  rows.push({k: 'none', l: 'No Speed points', v: calcStat(p, 'Spe', 0, 1), note: 'neutral nature'});
  rows.push({k: 'max', l: 'Max Speed points', v: calcStat(p, 'Spe', 32, 1), note: '32 points, neutral nature'});
  rows.push({k: 'maxn', l: 'Max + Speed nature', v: maxN, note: '32 points, Speed-boosting nature (Timid, Jolly…)'});
  if (!p.m) rows.push({k: 'scarf', l: 'Choice Scarf', v: spdCalc(maxN, true, '', '', {}), note: 'max + Speed nature'});
  rows.push({k: 'tw', l: 'In Tailwind', v: spdCalc(maxN, false, '', '', {tw: true}), note: 'max + Speed nature, Speed doubled'});
  const WX = {sun: 'sun', rain: 'rain', sand: 'sand', snow: 'snow'};
  for (const a of [...new Set(p.a)]) { const w = typeof WEATHER_SPD_AB === 'object' && WEATHER_SPD_AB[a]; if (w) rows.push({k: 'wx', l: `${a} in ${WX[w] || w}`, v: spdCalc(maxN, false, a, w, {}), note: 'max + Speed nature, Speed doubled'}); }
  rows.push({k: 'tr', l: 'Trick Room build', v: calcStat(p, 'Spe', 0, .9), note: '0 points, Speed-lowering nature', tr: true});
  /* no repeated numbers (Sim, Nov 2): when what players run equals a standard row, that row is folded into it */
  const run = rows.find(r => r.k === 'run'), dup = run && rows.find(r => r !== run && !r.tr && r.k !== 'wx' && r.k !== 'tw' && r.v === run.v);
  if (dup) { run.note += ` · same as ${dup.l.replace(/^./, c => c.toLowerCase())}`; rows.splice(rows.indexOf(dup), 1); }
  for (const r of rows) if (r.os === undefined) r.os = os(r.v, r.tr);
  return rows; };

// ---------- the sheet's header numbers and Mega Stone line (moved from 61-concept-h-pages.js, Nov 11) ----------
// The header shows real totals, the same numbers as this Pokémon's row in the Ratings list: tournament share, wins and games
// when it has tournament games, else the rating's own top-cut figures (30+ games). thinR: few games, so "Early read".
function lairHead(p) { const r = rating(p), src = r.src || {};
  const tOK = src.tour && +src.tour[2] > 0, tcOK = !tOK && r.tc && r.tc.g >= 30, hU = tOK ? +src.tour[0] : tcOK ? r.tc.field : null, hW = tOK ? +src.tour[1] : tcOK ? r.tc.wr : null, hG = tOK ? +src.tour[2] : tcOK ? r.tc.g : null;
  const tot = tOK && RES && +RES.teams > 0 ? +RES.teams : null, thinR = (hG != null && thinFor(hG, 'rating')) || r.band >= 10;
  return {tOK, tcOK, hU, hW, hG, tot, thinR}; }
// Mega Stones: the species' Megas, the stone players pick most (for species with two, sweep Oct 31), how many hold a stone
function lairStones(p, st) { const megas = ROSTER.filter(q => q.m && q.b === p.b && q.n !== p.n), top = (st.itStones || [])[0], mg = (top && megas.find(q => stoneFor(q) === top[0])) || megas[0], stn = st.itStone || 0, rest = (st.it || []).reduce((a, [, v]) => a + (+v || 0), 0);
  return {megas, top, mg, stn, rest}; }
// a Mega's key in the Showdown replay data (brought-with): its base form (moved from 60-concept-h.js, Nov 11)
function brKeyOf(p) { return p.n === 'Floette-Mega' ? 'Floette-Eternal' : p.n.replace(/-Mega(-[XYZ])?$/, ''); }

// ---------- the regulation in force (moved from 43-data-health.js, Nov 11) ----------
const PAGE_REG = 'M-C';
function regInfo() { const r = (META.perf && META.perf.reg) || META.reg; /* META.reg: kept even when a thin first day of a new regulation fails cleanPerf (Nov 7) */ if (!r || typeof r !== 'object' || !/^[A-Z]-[A-Z]$/.test(r.id)) return null;
  const day = d => /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(d || '')) ? String(d) : '', now = r.now && typeof r.now === 'object' && /^[A-Z]-[A-Z]$/.test(r.now.id) && r.now.id !== r.id && day(r.now.since) ? r.now : null;
  return {id: r.id, since: day(r.since), now: now ? {id: now.id, since: day(now.since), events: +now.events || 0, players: +now.players || 0, need: now.need && +now.need.events > 0 ? +now.need.events : null} : null}; }
// the regulation's name, read from the data (regInfo), so nothing typed goes stale at a switch (Nov 25, for Reg M-D on Dec 2);
// before the data says, this page's own regulation (PAGE_REG). Then the rules note under team checks and Import (moved from 56-about.js, R4 Nov 11)
function regId() { const r = regInfo(); return r ? r.id : PAGE_REG; }
function regName() { return 'Reg ' + regId(); }
function regNote(cls) { return `<p class="regnote${cls ? ' ' + cls : ''}">Based on our reading of the ${esc(regName())} rules — check the official rules before an event.</p>`; }

// ---------- moved from 25-ratings-page.js (Nov 14), shared with the new site's Meta page ----------
// The ratings' own track record, measured live: inside each recent event's top cut, how often the
// team with the higher average rating finished higher. Cheap (ratings are cached), so it's recomputed with the data.
let ratingTrackCache = {key: null, res: null};
function ratingTrack() { const E = META.events; if (!E) return null; const key = E.updated + '|' + E.events.length + '|' + metaVersion; if (ratingTrackCache.key === key) return ratingTrackCache.res;
  let agree = 0, pairs = 0;
  for (const ev of E.events) { const sc = (ev.top || []).filter(t => t && t.mons && t.mons.length === 6 && t.mons.every(m => byName.get(m.n))).map(t => ({pl: t.place, s: t.mons.reduce((a, m) => a + rating(byName.get(m.n)).total, 0) / 6}));
    for (let a = 0; a < sc.length; a++) for (let b = a + 1; b < sc.length; b++) { const A = sc[a], B = sc[b]; if (A.pl === B.pl || A.s === B.s) continue; pairs++; if ((A.s > B.s) === (A.pl < B.pl)) agree++; } }
  const res = pairs >= 200 ? {acc: agree / pairs, pairs, events: E.events.length} : null; ratingTrackCache = {key, res}; return res; }

===== FILE: app/src/app/43-data-health.js (claude/design6)
// ---------- data health: say plainly when a source stops updating ----------
// Each data document carries its own date. A source past its usual age (the same limits as the daily job's health gate,
// health.mjs) gets a calm amber note on Home and the Meta pages (CSS shows #healthBar only there), naming it and the day it
// was last updated, so stale numbers are never shown as if they were fresh. A missing document is simply not listed.
// In-game rankings: 9999 days (paused Nov 18: op.gg's Terms ban automated reads; the list is frozen at Nov 17), the same as
// health.mjs. The Meta pages say "paused since Nov 17 (op.gg)" instead. If the read resumes, restore 3 here and in health.mjs.
const HEALTH = [['doubles_tour', 'Tournament sets', 2], ['perf', 'Tournament win rates', 2], ['teams', 'Top teams', 2], ['model', 'Win model', 2], ['events', 'Events', 3], ['doubles_ingame', 'In-game rankings', 9999], ['practice', 'Team preview practice', 4], ['track', 'Ratings track record', 2], ['doubles', 'Showdown ladder stats', 45]];
const healthDay = t => { try { return new Date(t).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric'}); } catch (e) { return ''; } };
function healthStale(now) { now = now || Date.now(); const out = []; const at = typeof DATA_AT === 'object' ? DATA_AT : {};
  for (const [k, label, days] of HEALTH) { const t = at[k]; if (!t) continue; const age = Math.floor((now - t) / 864e5); if (age > days) out.push({k, label, age, day: healthDay(t)}); }
  return out; }
function renderHealth() { try { renderReg(); } catch (e) {} const el = $('healthBar'); if (!el) return; const stale = healthStale();
  el.hidden = !stale.length;
  el.innerHTML = stale.length ? `<b>Some data is older than usual:</b> ${stale.map(x => `${esc(x.label)}, last updated ${esc(x.day)}`).join('; ')}.<span class="t2">Everything else is current.</span>` : ''; }
/* ---------- regulation banner (Nov 1) ----------
   meta/perf.reg (reg.mjs → perf.json): the regulation every number is built from, and a newer one being played. The data job
   never mixes two regulations; while a new one is short of data the page keeps the old numbers and says so calmly on Home
   and the Meta pages (CSS shows #regBar only there). PAGE_REG is the regulation this page's rules are built for. */
/* PAGE_REG and regInfo live in 24-sheet-data.js (Nov 11) */
const regDay = d => { try { return new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric'}); } catch (e) { return d; } };
function renderReg() { const el = $('regBar'); if (!el) return; const r = regInfo(); let h = '';
  if (r && r.now) h = `<b>New regulation (Reg ${esc(r.now.id)}) started ${esc(regDay(r.now.since))}</b> — data is rebuilding; numbers below are from Reg ${esc(r.id)} until there's enough.`
    + `<span class="t2">${r.now.events ? `${r.now.events.toLocaleString('en-US')} Reg ${esc(r.now.id)} event${r.now.events === 1 ? '' : 's'} so far${r.now.need ? `, switches at ${r.now.need}` : ''}` : `Seen on the Showdown ladder; no Reg ${esc(r.now.id)} tournaments yet`}. The two are never mixed.</span>`;
  else if (r && r.id !== PAGE_REG) h = `<b>Numbers are from Reg ${esc(r.id)}</b>${r.since ? ` (started ${esc(regDay(r.since))})` : ''}.<span class="t2">Reg ${esc(PAGE_REG)} results are no longer counted. Anything new in Reg ${esc(r.id)} may be missing until NastyPlot is updated for it.</span>`;
  el.hidden = !h; el.innerHTML = h; }

===== FILE: app/src/app/76-team-sheet.js (claude/design6)
/* ---------- Team Sheet page (Sim, Nov 9) ----------
   "A page where we can see our entire team, every move, held item, Ability and stat spread in one quick look."
   One card per Pokémon: item and Ability, the four moves (type colours), the nature (what it raises and lowers) and the six
   stats as Champions stat points (0-32) with the real level-50 number (HP = base + 75 + points; others (base + 20 + points)
   x nature, calcStat in 39-damage-calc-speed.js). Every card says where the spread comes from: the player's own, their nature
   on the most common points (a team sheet gives no points), or the most common spread. Desktop 3 x 2 (the whole team on one
   screen), iPad 2 columns, phone 1. Tap a card to edit that Pokémon on My Team. My Team's own sheet fold is unchanged. */
Object.assign(VIEW_TITLE, {sheet: 'Team sheet'}); Object.assign(VIEW_PARENT, {sheet: 'report'});
/* TS (with STATS and natBits) is created in 44-share-images.js (Nov 11); this file adds the Team Sheet page's helpers */
// the six stats: real value at level 50 (big), stat points (small), nature's + and −. Shared by this page and the
// "Open team sheet" fold on My Team (Sim, Nov 9: one team sheet everywhere, with the stat spread in it)
TS.stats = m => { return sheetData(m).stats.map(({S: S_, pts, val, mark}) => { const mk = mark ? ' ' + mark : '';
    return `<div class="ts-st${mk}"><span class="ts-sl">${S_}${mk === ' up' ? '<i aria-label="raised by nature">+</i>' : mk === ' dn' ? '<i aria-label="lowered by nature">−</i>' : ''}</span><b class="ts-sv">${val == null ? '—' : val}</b><span class="ts-sp">${pts == null ? '' : pts}</span></div>`; }).join(''); };
// o (the "Open team sheet" fold on My Team, Nov 9): {fold: true, note: '<hint html>'} = tap opens the editor right there
// (data-chedit), with the remove button and the one-line hint the old sheet rows had
TS.card = (m, o = {}) => {
  const p = m.p, {nat, src, up, dn} = sheetData(m), nb = {up, dn};
  const stats = TS.stats(m);
  const mv = [0, 1, 2, 3].map(j => { const x = m.moves[j]; return x ? `<li style="--mt:${TC[x.type] || 'var(--ar-ink-3)'}">${esc(x.name)}</li>` : '<li class="none">No move</li>'; }).join('');
  const nl = nat ? `${esc(nat)}${nb.up && nb.dn && nb.up !== nb.dn ? ` <span class="ts-nb">+${nb.up} −${nb.dn}</span>` : ''}` : 'Nature unknown';
  return `<article class="ts-card${o.fold ? ' ts-fold' : ''}" role="button" tabindex="0" ${o.fold ? `data-chedit="${m.i}"` : `data-tsedit="${m.i}"`} style="${tyVars(p)}" aria-label="${esc(m.d)}: edit its set${o.fold ? '' : ' on My Team'}">
    ${o.fold ? `<button type="button" class="chrm ts-rm" data-chrm="${m.i}" aria-label="Remove ${esc(m.d)} from the team">−</button>` : ''}<header class="ts-hd">${sprite(p) || ''}<div class="ts-id"><h3 class="ts-nm">${esc(m.d)}</h3>
      <p class="ts-ia"><span><em>Item</em> ${esc(m.item || 'No item')}</span><span><em>Ability</em> ${esc(m.ability || '—')}</span></p></div></header>
    <ul class="ts-mv">${mv}</ul>${o.note || ''}
    <div class="ts-spr"><p class="ts-nat"><b>${nl}</b><span class="ts-src">${src}</span></p><div class="ts-stats" role="group" aria-label="Stats at level 50, with stat points below">${stats}</div></div>
  </article>`; };
TS.addCard = i => `<button type="button" class="ts-card ts-add" data-chedit="${i}" aria-label="Add a Pokémon to slot ${i + 1}"><span class="ts-plus" aria-hidden="true">+</span><span>Add a Pokémon</span></button>`;

  o.innerHTML = head + `<div class="ts-acts"><button type="button" class="ar-btn ar-primary" data-tsshare="1"><span>Share as image</span></button><button type="button" class="ar-btn" data-tscopy="1">Copy as text</button><span class="ts-key">Big number: the stat at level 50 · small number: stat points (0–32) · <b class="up">+</b>/<b class="dn">−</b> nature</span></div>

CH.wrap('setView', v => { if (v === 'sheet') renderSheet(); });

  if (t.dataset.tsopen != null) { setView('sheet'); window.scrollTo(0, 0); return; }

  const i = +t.dataset.tsedit; setView('report'); requestAnimationFrame(() => { const b = document.querySelector(`[data-pane="report"] [data-chedit="${i}"]`); if (b) b.click(); }); });

/* one Undo time everywhere: 10 s (Sim's design review #2, Nov 18: 'one Undo time of at least 10 s, paused on hover/focus'; the pause is in V2.toast) */
V2.UNDO_MS = 10000;
V2.undoToast = (msg, restore) => { V2._undoFn = restore; return V2.toast(V2.esc(msg) + ' <button type="button" data-v2undo>Undo</button>', V2.UNDO_MS); };
document.addEventListener('click', e => { if (!(e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-v2undo]'))) return; const f = V2._undoFn; V2._undoFn = null; document.querySelectorAll('.np-toast,.np-sheet-undo').forEach(x => x.remove()); if (f) { try { f(); } catch (x) { console.error(x); } V2.toast('Undone'); } });
/* review Nov 18 (C1, C2): a place hold (Apply's in sugs.js, Remove's V2.rmHold in six.js) lets go on the player's wheel, touch, pointer
   or key, and also on any scroll it didn't make itself: a screen reader moving its cursor, find-in-page, a link to a spot, a script.
   It knows its own scrolls (mark() after each scrollBy, and each frame). A scroll that comes with a layout change (the held element
   moved in the page, or the page got shorter or longer: scroll anchoring, a clamp) is the layout's, so the hold keeps it. Leaving the
   page (hashchange, popstate) ends it too. tgt() is the element the hold keeps in place; the hold asks on() each frame. */
/* Nov 19 (CI run 1365, BR-C1b): a layout change landing in the same frame as an outside scroll (the suggestions' redraw as a script
   scrolls 1500px) made the whole scroll count as the layout's, and the hold pulled the page back. A layout's own scroll is never larger
   than the layout change itself (the held element's move or the page's change in height), so a bigger scroll is not the layout's. */
V2.holdGuard = tgt => { const ev = ['wheel', 'touchstart', 'pointerdown', 'keydown'], at = () => { const e = tgt(); return [e ? Math.round(e.getBoundingClientRect().top + scrollY) : null, document.documentElement.scrollHeight]; };
  let y = scrollY, sig = at(), done = false; const stop = () => { if (done) return; done = true; ev.forEach(n => removeEventListener(n, stop, true)); removeEventListener('scroll', seen); removeEventListener('hashchange', stop); removeEventListener('popstate', stop); };
  const lay = s => (s[0] == null) !== (sig[0] == null) ? Infinity : Math.max(Math.abs((s[0] || 0) - (sig[0] || 0)), Math.abs(s[1] - sig[1])); /* how far the layout moved since the hold's last look */
  const seen = () => { if (done) return; const s = at(), d = Math.abs(scrollY - y); if (d > 1 && d > lay(s) + 2 && !(performance.now() - (V2._smoothAt || -1e9) < 1500)) return stop(); y = scrollY; sig = s; }; /* a site smooth scroll from the last 1.5 s is the site's: the hold corrects it */
  ev.forEach(n => addEventListener(n, stop, {capture: true, passive: true})); addEventListener('scroll', seen, {passive: true}); addEventListener('hashchange', stop); addEventListener('popstate', stop);
  return {mark: () => { y = scrollY; sig = at(); }, stop, on: () => !done}; };
// smooth scrolling only when the device doesn't ask for reduced motion (code review C7: eight scrolls ignored it)
/* review Nov 18 (C1): when a smooth scroll starts is kept (V2._smoothAt): one the site started can still be gliding when a hold begins
   (a Card tap's, then Remove at once: it landed after the hold's first frame), and a hold must not take it for the player's */
V2.smooth = () => { try { if (matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return 'auto'; } catch (e) { return 'auto'; } V2._smoothAt = performance.now(); return 'smooth'; };
V2.toast = (html, ms = 4000) => { document.querySelectorAll('.np-toast').forEach(x => x.remove());
  const t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'np-toast'; t.setAttribute('role', 'status'); t.innerHTML = html; document.body.append(t);
  const tops = [...document.querySelectorAll('.np-dock,.np-bar')].map(e => e.getBoundingClientRect()).filter(r => r.height && r.top < innerHeight).map(r => r.top);
  if (tops.length) t.style.setProperty('--tb', (innerHeight - Math.min(...tops) + 12) + 'px'); // a custom property, so an open sheet can still move it (components.css)
  // code review B9b: a toast stays while it has keyboard focus or the pointer (its Undo was gone after 4 s even mid-reach)
  /* Sim Q2 (Nov 16, recommended A): while a sheet is open the toast sits under its backdrop (his Nov 11 rule), so an Undo moves into a bar at
     the top of the sheet (the Undo bar style Suggestions uses); the words stay in the toast; the bar goes with the next change or the sheet */
  { const sh = document.querySelector('.np-sheet'), ub = t.querySelector('#toastUndo,[data-v2undo],[data-bxundo]'); document.querySelectorAll('.np-sheet-undo').forEach(x => x.remove());
    if (sh && ub) { const bar = document.createElement('div'), m = document.createElement('span'); bar.className = 'sg2-undo np-sheet-undo'; bar.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      /* the toast keeps its own Undo (full test run, Nov 16: Clear Team closes its sheet right after, and an Undo moved into it went with it);
         the sheet's bar has a twin that presses the toast's, and the toast stays (under the backdrop) while the bar is up */
      const tw = document.createElement('button'); tw.type = 'button'; tw.className = 'np-btn2 k-btn'; tw.innerHTML = '<span>Undo</span>'; tw.addEventListener('click', () => { if (ub.isConnected) ub.click(); bar.remove(); });
      m.textContent = t.textContent.replace(/\s*Undo\s*$/, ''); bar.append(m, tw); V2._ubar = bar;
      const host = [...sh.children].find(c => !c.classList.contains('x') && c.tagName !== 'BUTTON') || sh; host.prepend(bar); } }
  const gone = () => { if (t.matches(':focus-within,:hover') || (V2._ubar && V2._ubar.isConnected && t.contains(t.querySelector('#toastUndo,[data-v2undo],[data-bxundo]')))) { V2._tt = setTimeout(gone, 1000); return; } t.remove(); }; clearTimeout(V2._tt); V2._tt = setTimeout(gone, ms); return t; };
// Form errors reach the field (Sim's design review, Nov 19, #6; the non-visual part, nothing on screen changes): V2.fieldErr marks the
// field(s) in error with aria-invalid="true" and links them (aria-describedby) to the line that already explains the error, and moves the
// focus to the first one; V2.fieldOk takes both off again once it's fixed. Sim's W3b A (Nov 19): a 2px red edge on the field and the line in red
// with a '!' (.np-errmsg, components.css). Test: app/test/v2-formerr.mjs
V2.fieldErr = (fields, note, focus = true) => { fields = [].concat(fields).filter(Boolean); if (!fields.length) return;
  if (note && !note.id) note.id = 'np-err-' + (V2._errN = (V2._errN || 0) + 1);
  fields.forEach(f => { f.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true'); if (!note) return; f.dataset.errNote = note.id;
    const ids = (f.getAttribute('aria-describedby') || '').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (!ids.includes(note.id)) f.setAttribute('aria-describedby', ids.concat(note.id).join(' ')); });
  if (note) note.classList.add('np-errmsg'); /* Sim's W3b A (Nov 19): the line shows as an error (components.css) */
  if (focus) fields[0].focus({preventScroll: false}); };
V2.fieldOk = fields => [].concat(fields).filter(Boolean).forEach(f => { f.removeAttribute('aria-invalid'); const id = f.dataset.errNote; if (!id) return; delete f.dataset.errNote;
  /* the line stops looking like an error once no field it explains is still in error */ const n = document.getElementById(id); if (n && !document.querySelector(`[data-err-note="${id}"]`)) n.classList.remove('np-errmsg');
  const ids = (f.getAttribute('aria-describedby') || '').split(/\s+/).filter(x => x && x !== id); if (ids.length) f.setAttribute('aria-describedby', ids.join(' ')); else f.removeAttribute('aria-describedby'); });
// Focus on slanted controls (audit P0, Nov 16): a control whose own shape (or a near parent's) is clipped gets .np-fin while focused, so its
// ring is drawn inside the shape (base.css); checked on focus, so any slanted control, now or later, is covered without a list of classes
document.addEventListener('focusin', e => { const t = e.target; if (!(t instanceof Element) || t.classList.contains('np-fin')) return;
  for (let el = t, k = 0; el && k < 3; el = el.parentElement, k++) { const cp = getComputedStyle(el).clipPath; if (cp && cp !== 'none' && !/^inset\(50%/.test(cp)) { t.classList.add('np-fin'); return; } } }, true);
// ---------- phones: keep an open sheet above the on-screen keyboard (Sim, Nov 11: the keyboard covered Save Team) ----------
// iOS draws the keyboard over fixed elements; visualViewport says how much of the screen is left, so --kb lifts and shortens
// the sheet (components.css), and the box being typed in is scrolled into view inside it.
(() => { const vv = window.visualViewport; if (!vv) return; let raf = 0;
  // Sim, Nov 12 (iPhone): scrolling an info sheet made it flash. Scrolling and bouncing resize the app's bars, so innerHeight vs
  // visualViewport flickered and the sheet's bottom/height (--kb) jumped with it. A keyboard can only be open while a text box has
  // focus, so --kb is 0 otherwise, and the properties are written only when they change.
  let last = '';
  const set = () => { raf = 0; const a = document.activeElement, typing = !!(a && /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(a.tagName));
    const kb = typing ? Math.max(0, Math.round(innerHeight - vv.height - vv.offsetTop)) : 0, R = document.documentElement.style;
    const vals = [kb, typing ? Math.max(0, Math.round(vv.offsetTop)) : 0, typing ? Math.round(vv.height) : 0], key = vals.join(',');
    if (key !== last) { last = key; R.setProperty('--kb', vals[0] + 'px'); if (typing) { R.setProperty('--vvt', vals[1] + 'px'); R.setProperty('--vvh', vals[2] + 'px'); } }
    if (kb && a.closest && a.closest('.np-sheet')) a.scrollIntoView({block: 'nearest'}); };
  const later = () => { if (!raf) raf = requestAnimationFrame(set); };
  vv.addEventListener('resize', later); vv.addEventListener('scroll', later); set(); })();
// Sim, Nov 11 (iPhone, Claude app): typing in a sheet sent it off screen; inside the app's frame the keyboard height can't be
// trusted. While a box in a sheet has focus, the sheet pins to the top of what is visible instead (kbo, components.css).
document.addEventListener('focusin', e => { const t = e.target; if (t && t.closest && t.closest('.np-sheet') && /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(t.tagName)) document.documentElement.classList.add('kbo'); });
document.addEventListener('focusout', () => setTimeout(() => { const a = document.activeElement; if (!(a && a.closest && a.closest('.np-sheet') && /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA)$/.test(a.tagName))) document.documentElement.classList.remove('kbo'); }, 60));
// ---------- sheets: one at a time; focus moves in, stays in (Tab wraps), and returns to what opened it ----------
// Back closes what's open on top (X-06, as today's site): opening a sheet adds one history step at the same address, so the phone
// or browser Back button closes it and the page keeps its place; closing it with ✕, the backdrop or Escape takes that step back
// out quietly. A sheet closed by a move to another page leaves its step behind; Back steps over it.
V2.hist = {skip: 0, pop: false, t: null};
V2.histOpen = () => { clearTimeout(V2.hist.t); try { if (!(history.state && history.state.v2sh)) history.pushState(Object.assign({}, history.state, {v2sh: 1}), ''); } catch (e) {} };
V2.histClose = () => { if (V2.hist.pop) return; clearTimeout(V2.hist.t);
  V2.hist.t = setTimeout(() => { try { if (!V2._sheet && history.state && history.state.v2sh) { V2.hist.skip++; history.back(); } } catch (e) {} }, 0); };
addEventListener('popstate', () => { if (V2.hist.skip) { V2.hist.skip--; return; }
  if (V2._sheet) { V2.hist.pop = true; try { V2.closeSheet(); } finally { V2.hist.pop = false; } return; }
  // a sheet's leftover step: skip it the way you're travelling (arriving from deeper = going back; same depth = going forward)
  try { if (history.state && history.state.v2sh) { V2.hist.skip++; if ((history.state.v2d || 0) >= (V2.nav ? V2.nav.d : 0)) history.forward(); else history.back(); } } catch (e) {} });

// nothing on close (EDIT-01b in WebKit on Windows, Nov 19-20). Remember the control a click landed on; a sheet opened within a moment
// of it returns focus there. Chrome focuses the clicked button anyway, so there it is the same element as before.

document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key !== 'Enter' && e.key !== ' ') V2._tap = null; }, true);

  if (e.key === 'Escape') { const t = e.target; if (t && t.matches && t.matches('[data-btfind]') && t.value) return; /* code review B1: a filled Their Six box clears first (its own handler); the next Escape closes */ e.preventDefault(); V2.closeSheet(); return; }
  // focus stays in the sheet (Sim's check, Nov 12: in the info sheet Tab left after 6 stops, because the inactive tabs, tabindex -1,

  if (e.key !== 'Tab') return; const shown = x => x.getClientRects().length && !x.closest('[hidden],[inert]') && !(x.closest('details:not([open])') && !(x.matches('summary') && x.parentElement.matches('details:not([open])')));
  const f = [...S.wrap.querySelectorAll('.np-sheet :is(a[href],button,input,select,textarea,summary,[tabindex])')].filter(x => x.tabIndex >= 0 && !x.disabled && shown(x)); /* the toast is not in this loop: while a sheet is open it sits under the backdrop (Sim, Nov 11), so its Undo can't be seen there (sweep, Nov 15) */
  if (!f.length) return; const i = f.indexOf(document.activeElement);

     Search; we move focus ourselves on every Tab, in the same order, so every browser stays inside */
  e.preventDefault(); f[(i + (e.shiftKey ? f.length - 1 : 1)) % f.length].focus(); });

// ---------- search: places and Pokémon, from the header button or the / key ----------
V2.find = q => { q = q.trim().toLowerCase(); if (!q) return {places: [], mons: []};
  // accents count as their plain letter ("é" = "e", as in Pokémon); a query that is all punctuation finds nothing (Sim's check E16,
  // Nov 12: "é" became an empty query, which matched every name, so the most-used Pokémon showed up)
  const norm = x => x.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''), nq = norm(q);
  if (!nq) return {places: V2.findOk().filter(([, n]) => n.toLowerCase().includes(q)), mons: []};
  const places = V2.findOk().filter(([k, n]) => n.toLowerCase().includes(q) || (V2.placeAlias[n] || []).some(a => a.includes(q)) || (q.length >= 4 && new RegExp('\\b' + q.replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g, '')).test((V2.placeLines[k] || '').toLowerCase()))).slice(0, 6); /* Nov 15: by a word of its line too ('damage', 'speed') */
  const mons = [...V2.byName.values()].map(p => { const d = norm(V2.disp(p.n)), n = norm(p.n); const at = Math.min(...[d.indexOf(nq), n.indexOf(nq)].filter(i => i >= 0).concat(99));
    return {p, at: at === 99 ? null : at}; }).filter(x => x.at !== null).sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at || (b.p.u || 0) - (a.p.u || 0) || V2.disp(a.p.n).length - V2.disp(b.p.n).length).slice(0, 8).map(x => x.p);
  return {places, mons}; };

/* Q10 (Sim, Nov 18, answer A): the Day/Night button has three states, Day, Night and Auto (Auto = nothing saved, the page follows the
   device live, the same as a first visit). The mode lives in memory (head.html sets window.__npThMode before first paint).
   Order: from Auto the first tap always changes the look (THEME-02v: a light device gives Night), the next tap pins the other look,
   the third goes back to Auto. So on a dark device it runs Day > Night > Auto; on a light device Night > Day > Auto. Only the step
   back into Auto can leave the look as it is, and the button's icon and label say "Auto" there */
V2.devTheme = () => { try { return matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'night' : 'day'; } catch (e) { return 'day'; } };
V2.themeMode = () => { const m = window.__npThMode; return m === 'day' || m === 'night' ? m : 'auto'; };
V2.themeNext = () => { const m = V2.themeMode(), dv = V2.devTheme(); return m === 'auto' ? (V2.theme() === 'night' ? 'day' : 'night') : m !== dv ? dv : 'auto'; };
V2.THEME_WORD = {day: 'Day mode', night: 'Night mode', auto: 'Auto: follows your device'};
// ---------- data note (parity, Nov 15): today's #healthBar and #regBar (43-data-health.js), one calm line on Home and the Meta
// pages: a source past its usual age is named with its day, and a new or changed regulation is said plainly. Empty when all is current.
V2.REG_END_MC = '2026-12-02T01:59:00Z';
/* Nov 24 (competitor scan: rivals already mention Reg M-D; we said nothing): until M-C ends, a countdown line, shown in the Home hero's
   text under the herd (as a note above the hero it pushed the start buttons off the first screen: Stampede hero 3b; on Meta, the tabs) */
V2.REG_START_MD = '2026-12-02T02:00:00Z';
/* Nov 25 (night plan 1b): M-D starts 02:00 UTC, but our numbers switch only at the next data run (a hand run about 02:05 UTC, else the 09:07 one).
   In that gap the data still says M-C with nothing about M-D, so the same line says so, for up to 2 days (then the data note's
   staleness line takes over). It hides as soon as the data knows M-D (r.now or r.id M-D). */
V2.mdCountdown = (now = Date.now(), short = false) => { try { const r = typeof regInfo === 'function' && regInfo(), endMs = Date.parse(V2.REG_END_MC), stMs = Date.parse(V2.REG_START_MD);
  if (r && !r.now && r.id === 'M-C' && now >= stMs && now < stMs + 2 * 864e5) return short ? 'Reg M-D has started.' : 'Reg M-D has started: our numbers switch to it at the next data update.';
  if (!r || r.now || r.id !== 'M-C' || !(now < endMs)) return '';
  /* calendar days in the viewer's own time zone, so the last day says "today" (code review, Nov 24: it said "tomorrow" 59 min before the end) */
  const end = new Date(endMs), day = x => Date.UTC(x.getFullYear(), x.getMonth(), x.getDate()), d = Math.round((day(end) - day(new Date(now))) / 864e5),
    when = end.toLocaleString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit'});
  const lt = d <= 0 ? 'today' : d === 1 ? 'tomorrow' : `in ${d} days`; /* short form for phones: the long one pushed the start buttons off the first screen at 390x844 (Stampede hero 3b, Nov 24) */
  return short ? `Reg M-D ${d <= 0 ? 'arrives today' : d === 1 ? 'arrives tomorrow' : `in ${d} days`}.` : `Reg M-D is coming: M-C ends ${when} (${lt}), with the Dec 2 balance patch.`; } catch (e) { return ''; } };
/* Day N note (Nov 25; runbook day-one states; HH's design call under authority): for a new regulation's first 4 weeks, the Meta
   line under the tabs says how young the data is, "Day 3 of Reg M-D · 4 events · 212 teams", in place of "Reg M-D doubles · 212
   tournament teams" (same line and length: 0px added at 320-2560, measured). Days count in UTC because a regulation starts 02:00 UTC (Dec 2), still Dec 1 evening in the Americas. */
V2.regDay = (now = Date.now()) => { try { const r = typeof regInfo === 'function' && regInfo(); if (!r || r.now || !r.since || !isFinite(now)) return '';
  const d = Math.floor((now - Date.parse(r.since + 'T00:00:00Z')) / 864e5) + 1; if (!(d >= 1 && d <= 28)) return ''; const today = new Date(now).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const ev = (typeof META === 'object' && META.events && Array.isArray(META.events.events)) ? META.events.events.filter(e => { const x = String((e && e.date) || ''); return x >= r.since && x <= today; }).length : 0;
  return `Day ${d} of Reg ${String(r.id).replace('-', '\u2011')} · ${ev} event${ev === 1 ? '' : 's'}`; } catch (e) { return ''; } };

/* Not rated yet (Sim, Nov 25, 6:52 AM): in a new regulation's early days (its first 28 days, counted as V2.regDay does) or while no Pokémon
   has tournament results at all, a Pokémon with no results of its own shows the words "Not rated yet": no number, no tier, no rank, and it
   is left out of ranked lists, podiums, tiers, top-N lists and spotlights until it has results. With no results, ratings() falls back to
   stats (Gyarados showed "97 S tier #1" on a practice day one). A Pokémon has results when its rating carries tc (v3Apply; lairRank uses
   the same test). On a regulation past day 28 with results (Reg M-C today) both are false for every Pokémon, so nothing changes. */
V2.NOT_RATED = 'Not rated yet';
V2._nr = {R: null, none: false};
V2.noRatings = () => { try { const R = ratings(); if (V2._nr.R !== R) { let none = true; for (const v of R.values()) if (v && v.tc) { none = false; break; } V2._nr = {R, none}; } return V2._nr.none; } catch (e) { return false; } };
V2.regYoung = (now = Date.now()) => { try { const r = typeof regInfo === 'function' && regInfo(); if (!r || r.now || !r.since || !isFinite(now)) return false;
  return Math.floor((now - Date.parse(r.since + 'T00:00:00Z')) / 864e5) + 1 <= 28; } catch (e) { return false; } };
V2.unrated = p => { try { if (!p) return false; const r = ratings().get(p.n); if (r && r.tc) return false; return V2.noRatings() || V2.regYoung(); } catch (e) { return false; } };

V2.dataNote = () => { const E = V2.esc, out = [];
  /* review Nov 18 (C5): a --split Preview whose preview.json couldn't be read (missing, 404, offline) says so, on every page (V2.shell),
     rather than showing the built-in numbers as if they were today's */
  if (window.__NP_PVFAIL) out.push(`<b>This Preview couldn't read today's data</b> (preview.json: ${E(window.__NP_PVFAIL)}). The numbers shown are the built-in copy and may be out of date. Reload to try again.`);
  try { const r = typeof regInfo === 'function' && regInfo();
    if (r && r.now) out.push(`<b>New regulation: Reg ${E(r.now.id)} started ${E(regDay(r.now.since))}.</b> Numbers are from Reg ${E(r.id)} until there's enough Reg ${E(r.now.id)} data; the two are never mixed. ${r.now.events ? `${r.now.events.toLocaleString('en-US')} Reg ${E(r.now.id)} event${r.now.events === 1 ?     else if (r && r.id !== PAGE_REG) out.push(`<b>Numbers are from Reg ${E(r.id)}.</b> Anything new in Reg ${E(r.id)} may be missing until NastyPlot is updated for it.`); } catch (e) {}
  try { const st = typeof healthStale === 'function' ? healthStale() : [];
    if (st.length && window.__NP_PREVIEW) out.push(`<b>This Preview uses a saved copy of the data</b> from ${E(st[0].day)}; the live site updates every morning.`);
    else if (st.length) { const by = {}; st.forEach(x => { (by[x.day] = by[x.day] || []).push(x.label.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + x.label.slice(1)); });
      const and = a => a.length > 1 ? a.slice(0, -1).join(', ') + ' and ' + a[a.length - 1] : a[0];
      out.push(`<b>Some data is older than usual:</b> ${Object.keys(by).map(d => `${and(by[d])}, last updated ${E(d)}`).join('; ').replace(/^./, c => c.toUpperCase())}. Everything else is current.`); } } catch (e) {}
  return out.length ? `<p class="np-dnote" role="status"><i aria-hidden="true"></i><span>${out.join(' ')}</span></p>` : ''; };

// itself; sheets scroll on their own. Sections that are jumped to keep their own scroll-margin, so no page-wide scroll-padding.

/* Nov 24 (competitor scan: rivals show how fresh their data is; freshness is our specialty): "updated 7 h ago" from the OLDEST of the
   core parts (results, teams, events, model), the same parts "Data as of" stands on, so it never claims fresher than the slowest part */
V2.freshAgo = (now = Date.now()) => { try { if (!Number.isFinite(now) || typeof DATA_AT !== 'object' || !DATA_AT || typeof DATA_CORE === 'undefined') return '';
  const ts = DATA_CORE.map(k => DATA_AT[k]).filter(Number.isFinite); if (!ts.length) return '';
  /* round first, then pick the unit, so 59.5 min reads "1 h" and 47.5 h reads "2 days" (code review, Nov 24) */
  const m = Math.max(0, (now - Math.min(...ts)) / 6e4), mins = Math.max(1, Math.round(m)), hrs = Math.round(m / 60);
  return mins < 60 ? `updated ${mins} min ago` : hrs < 48 ? `updated ${hrs} h ago` : `updated ${Math.round(m / 1440)} days ago`; } catch (e) { return ''; } };
V2.asOf = () => { try { const t = typeof dataDateText === 'function' ? dataDateText() : ''; if (t) return t;
  /* no live data (SHELL-29, Nov 16): the built-in snapshot's own date, as the Ratings line used to show it, so the footer is never dateless */
  const pf = typeof activePerf === 'function' ? activePerf() : null, u = pf && pf.updated ? Date.parse(String(pf.updated).slice(0, 10) + 'T12:00:00Z') : NaN; if (isNaN(u)) return '';
  const days = calDays(u, Date.now()); return 'Data as of ' + new Date(u).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {month: 'short', day: 'numeric'}) + (days > 2 ? ' (' + days + ' days old)' : ''); } catch (e) { return ''; } };

// Word list (Sim, Nov 9; Nov 12: "fix them based on your recommendation"): the idea is Matchups and its levels are Beats it /
// Handles it, never "ready". Shared logic (also today's site) still writes "Ready for ..."; the new site says it the word list's way.
// The "81%" in "Ready for 81% of the top 30" is a weighted score, not a share of threats, so the plain count is shown instead.
V2.wl = (t, hand) => String(t == null ? '' : t)
  .replace(/\bReady for the top threats\b/g, 'Handles the top threats') /* the check's name as My Team shows it (audit #8, Nov 15) */
  .replace(/Ready for \d+% of the top (\d+) threats(?:,| ·) handles (\d+) of \d+/g, 'Beats or handles $2 of the top $1 threats')
  .replace(/Ready for only (\d+)% of the top (\d+) threats/g, (m, p, n) => hand ? `Beats or handles only ${hand.x} of the top ${hand.n}` : `Matchups ${p}% against the top ${n} threats`) /* scan S24 (Nov 20): the miss line said "Ready for only" */
  .replace(/; not ready for /g, '; no answer to ') /* scan S24: the Matchups level's own words */
  .replace(/\band only 0 resist\b/g, 'and none resist') /* scan S1 (Nov 20) */
  .replace(/Ready for \d+% of the top (\d+) threats/g, (m, n) => hand ? `Beats or handles ${hand.x} of the top ${hand.n}` : m)
  .replace(/([Rr])eady for (\d+)\/(\d+) top threats/g, (m, r, x, n) => `${r === 'R' ? 'B' : 'b'}eats or handles ${x} of ${n} top threats`)
  .replace(/Ready for (\d+)\/(\d+) → (\d+)\/(\d+) top threats/g, 'Beats or handles $1 → $3 of $2 top threats')
  .replace(/Ready for top threats: (\d+)\/(\d+) → (\d+)\/(\d+)/g, 'Beats or handles $1 → $3 of the top $2 threats') /* the suggestion Why? line (FP-3, Nov 16) */
  .replace(/(\d+)% → (\d+)% ready for the top (\d+) threats/g, 'Matchups $1% → $2% against the top $3 threats')
  .replace(/(\d+)% ready for the top (\d+) threats/g, 'Matchups $1% against the top $2 threats')
  .replace(/(.+) is ready for more of the top (\d+) threats \((\d+)% vs (\d+)% ready\)/g, '$1 has the better matchups against the top $2 threats ($3% vs $4%, weighted by how often each threat makes top cut)')
  .replace(/\b(grassy|electric|psychic|misty) terrain\b/g, (m, t) => t[0].toUpperCase() + t.slice(1) + ' Terrain') // Nov 15 (re-audit #13): terrain names as the rest of the site writes them // Compare (i42, word list); Nov 15 (re-audit #8): say it's weighted, so it doesn't read against the Beats it / Handles it counts
  .replace(/Ready for the top threats/g, 'Handles the top threats')
  .replace(/\bReady ([+−-]\d+%)/g, 'Matchups $1') // Quick add: the change to the matchup score
  .replace(/how many your team is ready for: it Beats them or Handles them/g, 'how many your team Beats or Handles');
// copy without losing your place (Sim's check E15, Nov 12): the shared copyText falls back to a hidden text box when the clipboard is
// blocked, which takes focus and is then removed; focus goes back to the control you pressed
V2.copy = async t => { const a = document.activeElement, ok = await copyText(t); if (a && a !== document.body && a.isConnected && document.activeElement !== a) a.focus({preventScroll: true}); return ok; };
// redraw the current page after data arrives or a choice changes, keeping the reader's place and focus (no scroll jump).
// Fix plan A5 (Sim, Nov 11): a redraw used to rebuild the whole page, header included, so focus fell back to the page top
// (the next Tab went to "Skip to the page") and a button could be replaced under the pointer. Now the new page is built
// off-screen and only the parts that differ are changed in place (V2.morph); what didn't change, and the focused control,
// stay the same elements. If the focused control itself had to be replaced, focus goes to its new copy (V2.focusKey).
V2.focusKey = el => { if (!el || el === document.body || !el.closest || !el.closest('#app')) return null; if (el.id) return {sel: '#' + CSS.escape(el.id), i: 0};
  const ds = [...el.attributes].filter(a => a.name.startsWith('data-') || a.name === 'href' || a.name === 'aria-label'), sel = el.tagName.toLowerCase() + ds.map(a => `[${a.name}="${CSS.escape(a.value)}"]`).join('');
  return {sel, i: [...document.querySelectorAll('#app ' + sel)].indexOf(el)}; };
// make old (live) look like new (built off-screen): same nodes kept where they match, attributes and text updated in place
// An element whose action markers (data-* attributes) differ is a different control: it is replaced, never changed in place, so a
// tap that is still being handled can't reach a different action (found Nov 12: "Show 2 more" became "See all" in place and the
// same click then opened All Suggestions; behaviour test SUG-04).
V2.dataSig = e => [...e.attributes].filter(a => a.name.startsWith('data-')).map(a => a.name + '=' + a.value).sort().join('&');
V2.morph = (o, n) => { if (o.nodeType !== n.nodeType || o.nodeName !== n.nodeName || (o.nodeType === 1 && (o.id !== n.id || V2.dataSig(o) !== V2.dataSig(n)))) { o.replaceWith(n); return; }
  if (o.nodeType !== 1) { if (o.nodeValue !== n.nodeValue) o.nodeValue = n.nodeValue; return; }
  /* code review A1-I3 (Nov 17): a redraw used to close every fold the visitor had opened (the fresh HTML has no "open"). A fold keeps
     the visitor's choice while the page's own wish for it is unchanged; when the page changes its mind, the page wins. */
  const fold = o.nodeName === 'DETAILS', want = fold && n.hasAttribute('open'), keep = fold && o._pg === want ? o.open : null; if (fold) o._pg = want;
  if (o.isEqualNode(n)) { if ('value' in o && /^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(o.nodeName)) V2.morphField(o, n); return; }
  for (const a of [...o.attributes]) if (!n.hasAttribute(a.name)) o.removeAttribute(a.name);
  for (const a of [...n.attributes]) if (o.getAttribute(a.name) !== a.value) o.setAttribute(a.name, a.value);
  // a select merges its options inside morphField (and sets its value after): merging them a second time here dropped the options the
  // first pass had just moved in, so a longer list fell back to its first option (Nov 14: the calc's set boxes after a new Pokemon)
  if (/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(o.nodeName)) { V2.morphField(o, n); if (o.nodeName === 'SELECT') return; }
  if (keep !== null && o.open !== keep) o.open = keep;
  V2.morphKids(o, n); };
// the page's wish for a fold, noted when the visitor first taps it (until then the fold is as the page drew it). A tap, not the
// "toggle" event: browsers also fire "toggle" when a fold is first drawn open, which would note the wrong wish.
document.addEventListener('click', e => { const s = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('summary'), d = s && s.parentElement; if (d && d.nodeName === 'DETAILS' && d._pg === undefined) d._pg = d.open; }, true);
V2.morphKids = (o, n) => { const oc = [...o.childNodes], nc = [...n.childNodes], k = Math.min(oc.length, nc.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < k; i++) V2.morph(oc[i], nc[i]); for (let i = k; i < oc.length; i++) oc[i].remove(); for (let i = k; i < nc.length; i++) o.append(nc[i]); };
// a form field ends up as a full redraw would leave it (value and ticks from the new page), so code that clears a box still works
V2.morphField = (o, n) => { if (o.nodeName === 'SELECT') { V2.morphKids(o, n); o.value = [...n.options].find(x => x.hasAttribute('selected'))?.value ?? o.value; return; }
  if (o.type === 'checkbox' || o.type === 'radio') o.checked = n.hasAttribute('checked'); else if (o.value !== (n.getAttribute('value') ?? n.textContent ?? '')) o.value = n.getAttribute('value') ?? (o.nodeName === 'TEXTAREA' ? n.textContent : ''); };
/* `pre` (speed, pl-2c): the page's HTML already built by V2.shell() in an earlier step (V2.warmDraw), still current (V2.preOk); else built here */
V2.redraw = pre => { const app = document.getElementById('app'); if (!app || !app.firstChild) { V2.draw(); return; }
  const y = scrollY, act = document.activeElement, key = V2.focusKey(act), r = pre && pre.tag === V2.preOk && V2.preOk(pre) ? pre.r : V2.shell(), t = document.createElement('template'); t.innerHTML = r.html;
  V2.rdN = (V2.rdN || 0) + 1; /* counted after the check above, so a pre-built HTML is never stale against this very redraw */
  V2.morphKids(app, t.content); if (scrollY !== y) scrollTo(0, y); /* only when the merge moved it (code review B-m2) */
  if (key && !act.isConnected) { const e = [...document.querySelectorAll('#app ' + key.sel)][Math.max(0, key.i)]; if (e) e.focus({preventScroll: true}); }
  /* stress test (Nov 16): a focused heading kept by the merge loses its temporary tabindex (copied from the fresh HTML) and the browser drops focus to <body>; put both back */
  else if (act && act !== document.body && act.isConnected && document.activeElement !== act && !(V2._sheet)) { if (!act.matches('a[href],button,input,select,textarea,summary,[tabindex]')) { act.tabIndex = -1; act.addEventListener('blur', () => act.removeAttribute('tabindex'), {once: true}); } act.focus({preventScroll: true}); }
  /* a page's after() runs after EVERY redraw, not only on entry: it must be safe to repeat (measure and lay out; anything that opens,
     focuses or announces must guard itself to run once, as #sheet and the Pokémon page's name focus do; code review B9c) */
  if (r.P && r.P.after) try { r.P.after(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } if (V2.fitWords) try { V2.fitWords(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); } V2.setTitle(); };
// the whole page as HTML (header, the page, footer, phone bar); V2.draw puts it in fresh, V2.redraw merges it in place
// one page's error never blanks the whole site (audit SHELL-34; today's safe() wrapper does the same per section): the header, menus
// and footer still draw, the page says what happened, and the error goes to the console for the checks to catch
V2.safeDraw = P => { try { return P.draw(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); /* Wave 2 part 5 (Sim chose A, Nov 22): the Page-not-found shape, with one way out (RELOAD. reloads the page); no underdog on an error */
    return `<div class="ra gsp nf-pg nf2" data-v2err><div class="nf2-two"><div class="k-head"><p class="k-kicker np-eyebrow">PAGE · DIDN\u2019T LOAD</p><h1 class="k-h1 u">This page <em>didn\u2019t load.</em></h1><p class="k-lead">Something went wrong drawing it. Reload to try again; the rest of the site still works.</p>${V2.kGo('RELOAD.', {'data-v2reload': ''})}</div></div></div>`; } };
document.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target.closest && e.target.closest('[data-v2reload]')) location.reload(); });
/* pre-launch check 1 (Nov 17): the page shipped with built-in numbers drew them first and swapped ~1.2 s later (Home 10,209 -> 13,826
   teams, the Game Plan's lead and bench flipped, Strength Score 92 -> 93). Numbers are drawn only once every daily document has answered
   (META_SEEN, 03-persistence.js), or there is no data store (a plain file, the tests, signed out), or after 8 s (a document that never
   answers: the built-in numbers rather than nothing). Until then the main area shows the loading panel (V2.loadingHtml). */
// speed fix 2 (Nov 17, pre-launch 2c): when today's numbers arrive, the ratings for every Pokémon and your team's report are worked out
// as their own steps (both are kept and reused by the drawing), then the page is drawn: three shorter tasks instead of one ~1 s freeze.
// The loading panel stays up until the draw, so nothing old or half-done shows.
/* pl-2c (Nov 19): the draw itself was still one task (Home ~0.5 s, My Team ~0.33 s at 4x CPU): working out the page's HTML, then putting it on the page.
   Now two steps: V2.shell() builds the HTML, and the next step puts that same HTML in (V2.redraw(pre)). The HTML is only reused while
   nothing it depends on has changed in between (V2.preOk: the address, the theme, the data version, the team, no other draw); otherwise
   the redraw builds it afresh, exactly as before. The page as finally drawn is the same; test app/test/v2-longtask.mjs. */
V2.preStamp = () => { let t = ''; try { t = JSON.stringify(S.slots) + '|' + (S.leads || []).join(); } catch (e) {}
  return [location.hash, document.documentElement.dataset.theme, typeof metaVersion !== 'undefined' ? metaVersion : '', V2.rdN || 0, V2.drawN || 0, t].join('\n'); };
V2.preOk = pre => pre.stamp === V2.preStamp();
V2.warmDraw = () => { let pre = null; const P = V2.pages[V2.route()], warm = (P && P.warm) || []; /* a page's own cached parts, each its own step (Home: its tools plate) */
  const steps = [() => ratings(), () => { const ms = members(); if (ms.length) prepOf(ms); }, ...warm,
    () => { const stamp = V2.preStamp(); V2.prePend = true; pre = {tag: V2.preOk, stamp, r: V2.shell()}; }]; let i = 0;
  /* V2.prePend: while built HTML waits for the next step, code the draw set up to look at the page "just after the draw" waits too
     (V2.afterPre; sugs.js ckWinSoon): without it, the winners pass found no Suggestions on the page and started at once (+0.5 s of blocking) */
  const sched = fn => window.__NP_PUBLIC && typeof requestIdleCallback === 'function' ? requestIdleCallback(fn, {timeout: 120}) : setTimeout(fn, 0);
  const next = () => { if (i < steps.length) { try { steps[i++](); } catch (e) { pre = null; } sched(next); } else try { V2.redraw(pre); } finally { V2.prePend = false; } }; sched(next); };

// ---------- Back button (Sim, Nov 12: "go to where we previously were"; pressing again goes further back) ----------
// Every page change is already one step in the browser's history (its address). Each step is marked with how deep in the site it is
// (v2d), the page it came from (v2p) and where it was scrolled (v2y). Back = one real history step, so it matches the phone swipe and
// the browser's Back exactly: same page, same scroll; a sheet that's open closes first (the sheets' own step, above). It shows only
// when there's a step on this site to go back to (depth > 0), so it can never take you out of NastyPlot.
V2.nav = {d: 0, p: '', ready: false, t: null, max: 0, hs: []}; // max: the deepest step you can go forward to; hs: each step's address (for Forward's label)
try { history.scrollRestoration = 'manual'; } catch (e) {}
V2.navMark = o => { try { history.replaceState(Object.assign({}, history.state, o), ''); } catch (e) {} };
V2.navInit = () => { if (V2.nav.ready) return; V2.nav.ready = true; const st = history.state || {};
  if (st.v2d == null) V2.navMark({v2d: 0, v2p: ''}); else { V2.nav.d = st.v2d; V2.nav.p = st.v2p || ''; } V2.nav.max = V2.nav.d; V2.nav.hs[V2.nav.d] = location.hash || '#home'; };
// called first thing on every address change: an entry we've marked before is a step back (or forward): return its scroll;
// an unmarked one is a new step: mark it one deeper and start at the top
V2.navStep = was => { V2.navInit(); const st = history.state || {};
  if (st.v2d != null) { V2.nav.d = st.v2d; V2.nav.p = st.v2p || ''; V2.nav.hs[V2.nav.d] = location.hash || '#home'; if (V2.nav.d > V2.nav.max) V2.nav.max = V2.nav.d; return st.v2y || 0; }
  // a new step after going back drops the steps you could go forward to (as in any browser)
  // the Back arrow's safety net (below) is a step back, not a new one
  // (a step back or forward keeps the steps you can go forward to, as the browser would)
  if (V2.nav.fb) { const fb = V2.nav.fb; V2.nav.fb = null; V2.nav.d = fb.d; V2.nav.p = fb.p; V2.nav.hs[V2.nav.d] = location.hash || '#home'; if (V2.nav.d > V2.nav.max) V2.nav.max = V2.nav.d; V2.navMark({v2d: fb.d, v2p: fb.p, v2y: 0}); return 0; }
  V2.nav.d++; V2.nav.p = was || '#home'; V2.nav.max = V2.nav.d; V2.nav.hs.length = V2.nav.d; V2.nav.hs[V2.nav.d] = location.hash || '#home'; V2.navMark({v2d: V2.nav.d, v2p: V2.nav.p, v2y: 0}); return 0; };
// remember where this step is scrolled: right before any tap that may change the page, and when the page is hidden or left.
// Sim, Nov 14 (Claude app on iPhone, every long page): the scroll bar flashed after scrolling a moment. Writing to the history while
// scrolling (each 200 ms pause) was the likely cause, so scrolling itself no longer touches the history; Back still returns to the spot.

V2.ARROW_LISTS = [['[data-v2q]', () => '[data-v2hits] .np-hit:not(.np-hitx)'], ['[data-bxq]', () => '[data-bxhits] .np-hit:not([aria-disabled])'],
