/* Real links on the public site (SEO audit Nov 25 #9: 0 real "/..." links on any page, so search engines could only find pages
   through the sitemap). On nastyplot.org every in-site link that has its own address gets it as href (/meta, /pokemon/garchomp,
   /privacy...), with the old #hash kept in data-h. A plain click still goes by the hash, exactly as before (same page, no reload);
   a new tab, a copied link or a search engine gets the real address. The Claude version is untouched. */
V2.pubPath = h => { const m = /^#([a-z-]+)(?:=(.*))?$/.exec(String(h || '')); if (!m) return null; const [, r, sub] = m;
  if (r === 'pokemon' && sub && typeof V2.pubSlug === 'function') { let n; try { n = decodeURIComponent(sub); } catch (e) { return null; } const s = V2.pubSlug(n); return s ? '/pokemon/' + s : null; }
  if (r === 'about' && sub === 'priv') return '/privacy';
  return sub == null && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(V2.PUB_PATHS, r) ? V2.PUB_PATHS[r] : null; };
V2.pubLinks = html => String(html).replace(/href="(#[^"]*)"/g, (m, h) => { const p = V2.pubPath(h.replace(/&amp;/g, '&')); return p ? `href="${p}" data-h="${h}"` : m; });
if (window.__NP_PUBLIC) {
  const sh = V2.shell; V2.shell = (...a) => { const o = sh(...a); if (o && typeof o.html === 'string') o.html = V2.pubLinks(o.html); return o; };
  document.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.button || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.shiftKey || e.altKey) return;
    const a = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[data-h]'); if (!a || a.target) return;
    e.preventDefault(); const h = a.getAttribute('data-h'); if (location.hash !== h) location.hash = h; }, true); }

===== FILE: experiments/md-day-one-test.mjs (claude/design6)
// Node 22 (no network) test suite for Reg M-D day-one model, history split, health floors, and regstate (Job 8).
// Usage: node experiments/md-day-one-test.mjs
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import {execFileSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {pathToFileURL} from 'node:url';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const ROOT = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
const TMP = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'np-md-day1-'));

// Copy patched scripts into TMP and add minimal stubs for unseen helper imports (pilot.mjs, team-basics.mjs)
for (const f of ['formats.mjs', 'model.mjs', 'model-guard.mjs', 'six.mjs', 'ctr.mjs', 'leads.mjs', 'scoreboard.mjs', 'track.mjs', 'health.mjs', 'regstate.mjs']) {
  fs.copyFileSync(path.join(ROOT, f), path.join(TMP, f));
}
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(TMP, 'pilot.mjs'), 'export const pilotTest = () => ({});\n');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(TMP, 'team-basics.mjs'), 'export const deckBasics = () => ({noMega: 0, protectLow: 0});\n');

const wr = (dir, rel, obj) => {
  const p = path.join(dir, rel);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(p), {recursive: true});
  fs.writeFileSync(p, typeof obj === 'string' ? obj : JSON.stringify(obj));
};
const rd = (dir, rel) => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, rel), 'utf8'));
const run = (script, cwd, args = [], env = {}) => {
  try {
    const stdout = execFileSync(process.execPath, [path.join(TMP, script), ...args], {
      cwd, env: {...process.env, GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY: '', HEALTH_REPORT: '', ...env}, encoding /* the same output here and on GitHub (Nov 24: health.mjs prints its table only when there's no job summary) */: 'utf8', stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']
    });
    return {code: 0, stdout, stderr: ''};
  } catch (e) {
    return {code: e.status ?? 1, stdout: String(e.stdout || ''), stderr: String(e.stderr || '')};
  }
};

// Build a valid full-size (>=250 mons) model object for tests
const makeFullModel = (reg, updated, seed = 0) => {
  const mons = {}, monSd = {};
  for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) {
    const n = `Mon${String(i).padStart(3, '0')}`;
    mons[n] = +(((i % 50) - 25) * 0.02 + seed * 0.001).toFixed(4);
    monSd[n] = 0.05;
  }
  return {
    reg, updated, ships: true, shrink: 10, setShrink: null, games: 5000, events: 40, v: 3,
    meanX: 0, calib: 0.9, dist: {since: updated.slice(0, 10), n: 100, q: Array.from({length: 101}, (_, i) => +(i * 0.02 - 1).toFixed(3))},
    setRange: {}, supK: 6, tested: [], setFreq: {}, team: {noMega: -0.5, protectLow: -0.4},
    mons, pairs: {}, monSd, pairSd0: 0.06, pairSd: {}, sets: {}, pilot: {},
    eval: {model_teams_only: {acc: 0.56, logloss: 0.68}, calibrated_logloss: {species_only: 0.68, with_sets: 0.675}, raw_win_rates: {acc: 0.54, logloss: 0.689}, events_beating_coin_flip: {beat_coin_flip: 8, events: 10}}
  };
};

// Build healthy mature out/ + data/ for a given reg and date
const seedHealthy = (dir, reg, since, date, padBytes = true) => {
  const iso = date + 'T10:00:00Z';
  const big = padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(60000) : '';
  wr(dir, 'data/index.json', {tournaments: {t1: {d: date}}});
  wr(dir, 'out/split/index.json', {made: iso, sizes: {'meta/model': 50000}, split: {}});
  wr(dir, 'out/reg.json', {id: reg, name: 'Reg ' + reg, since, sd: reg === 'M-C' ? 'gen9championsvgc2026regmc' : 'gen9championsvgc2026regmd', checked: date});
  wr(dir, 'out/limitless.json', {reg, updated: iso, results: {Pikachu: 10}, adj: {Pikachu: 0.5}, teams: 500, pad: big});
  wr(dir, 'out/meta_tour.json', {reg, updated: iso, top: Array.from({length: 15}, (_, i) => ({n: `Mon${i}`, u: 10})), pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(6000) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/teams.json', {reg, updated: iso, teams: Array.from({length: 6}, () => ['Mon0']), pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(2500) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/history.json', {reg, days: [{d: since, tour: {Mon0: 1}}, {d: date, tour: {Mon0: 1}}], pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(1200) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/model.json', makeFullModel(reg, iso));
  wr(dir, 'out/meta_ingame.json', {updated: '2026-11-18T10:00:00Z', top: Array.from({length: 12}, (_, i) => ({n: `Mon${i}`, r: i + 1})), pad: 'x'.repeat(1200)});
  wr(dir, 'out/meta_ladder.json', {reg, month: date.slice(0, 7), updated: iso, top: Array.from({length: 12}, (_, i) => ({n: `Mon${i}`, u: 5})), pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(6000) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/events.json', {reg, updated: iso, dict: ['Mon0', 'Mon1', 'Mon2', 'Mon3', 'Mon4', 'Mon5'], events: Array.from({length: 6}, (_, i) => ({id: `ev${reg}${i}`, date, name: `Ev ${i}`, players: 64, top: [[0, 1, 0, [[0], [1], [2], [3], [4], [5]]], [0, 2, 0, [[0], [1], [2], [3], [4], [5]]], [0, 3, 0, [[0], [1], [2], [3], [4], [5]]], [0, 4, 0, [[0], [1], [2], [3], [4], [5]]]]})), pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(11000) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/official.json', {reg, updated: iso, events: [], pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(1200) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/perf.json', {reg: {id: reg, name: 'Reg ' + reg, since}, updated: date, tour: {updated: date, rows: []}, adj: {updated: date, rows: []}, ingame: {updated: '2026-11-18', names: []}, smogon: {updated: date.slice(0, 7), rows: []}, ladder: {updated: date, rows: []}, outside: {lists: [{name: 'Showdowntier', date}]}, pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(6000) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/practice.json', {reg, updated: iso, rounds: Array.from({length: 25}, () => [0]), pad: padBytes ? 'x'.repeat(5500) : ''});
  wr(dir, 'out/weekly.json', {reg, v: 1, updated: iso, pad: 'x'.repeat(300)});
  wr(dir, 'out/calendar.json', {updated: iso, ev: [{s: date, e: '2027-01-10'}], pad: 'x'.repeat(250)});
  wr(dir, 'out/track.json', {reg, v: 1, updated: iso, since, snaps: 1, last: date, top: 8, ev: [], mons: [], mb: [[0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0], [0, 0]], fw: [], mv: null, pad: 'x'.repeat(250)});
  wr(dir, 'out/sources.json', {reg, updated: iso, pad: 'x'.repeat(300)});
  wr(dir, 'out/crosscheck.json', {reg, updated: iso, checks: [], pad: 'x'.repeat(300)});
  wr(dir, 'out/ratings_log.json', {reg, updated: iso, byReg: {[reg]: [{d: date, r: {Mon0: 85, Mon1: 70, Mon2: 50, Mon3: 30}}]}, pad: 'x'.repeat(300)});
  wr(dir, 'out/scoreboard.json', {reg, updated: iso, byReg: {[reg]: [{date, reg, model: {heldOutAcc: 0.56, calibratedLogloss: 0.675}}]}, history: [{date, reg}], pad: 'x'.repeat(300)});
  wr(dir, 'out/matches.json', {reg, updated: iso, events: [['e1', date, 64]], players: 64, teams: ['Mon000|Mon001|Mon002|Mon003|Mon004|Mon005', 'Mon006|Mon007|Mon008|Mon009|Mon010|Mon011'], m: [[0, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1]]});
};

let passed = 0;
const test = async (name, fn) => { await fn(); passed++; console.log(`PASS: ${name}`); };

// --- Scenario 1: Day before switch (2026-12-01, M-C active) ---

seedHealthy(d1, 'M-C', '2026-09-01', '2026-12-01');
wr(d1, 'out/prev_mc_model.json', makeFullModel('M-C', '2026-11-30T10:00:00Z'));

await test('1. regstate on day before reports M-C day 92', () => {
  const rs = run('regstate.mjs', d1);
  // Note: regstate uses today's clock unless called via buildRegState; test CLI writes out/regstate.json and check pure date too
  assert.equal(rs.code, 0);
  const out = rd(d1, 'out/regstate.json');
  assert.equal(out.reg, 'M-C');
  assert.equal(out.since, '2026-09-01');
  assert.equal(out.events, 1);
  assert.equal(out.games, 1);
});

await test('2. health on day before passes with 0 problems', () => {
  const r = run('health.mjs', d1, ['--root=' + d1, '--now=2026-12-01']);
  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /Daily data healthy/);
});

await test('3. model-guard on day before accepts valid M-C model vs prev M-C', () => {

  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.stdout);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /model guard: ok/);
});

wr(d2, 'out/reg.json', {id: 'M-D', name: 'Reg M-D', since: '2026-12-02', sd: 'gen9championsvgc2026regmd', switched: {from: 'M-C', to: 'M-D', on: '2026-12-02'}, checked: '2026-12-02'});

await test('11. health.mjs on switch day marks empty-but-stamped M-D files as thin and exits 0', () => {
  const d2h = path.join(TMP, 's2-health');

  wr(d2h, 'out/history.json', {reg: 'M-D', days: []});
  wr(d2h, 'out/model.json', {reg: 'M-D', updated: iso, ships: false, games: 0, events: 0, mons: {}});
  wr(d2h, 'out/meta_ladder.json', {reg: 'M-D', month: '', updated: iso, top: []});
  wr(d2h, 'out/events.json', {reg: 'M-D', updated: iso, events: []});
  wr(d2h, 'out/official.json', {reg: 'M-D', updated: iso, events: []});
  wr(d2h, 'out/perf.json', {reg: {id: 'M-D', name: 'Reg M-D', since: '2026-12-02'}, updated: '2026-12-02'});

  wr(d2h, 'out/scoreboard.json', {reg: 'M-D', updated: iso, byReg: {'M-D': []}});
  const r = run('health.mjs', d2h, ['--root=' + d2h, '--now=2026-12-02']);
  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /🟡 thin/);

const d3 = path.join(TMP, 's3-day3-5events');

  events: [['e1', '2026-12-02', 32], ['e2', '2026-12-02', 40], ['e3', '2026-12-03', 48], ['e4', '2026-12-03', 36], ['e5', '2026-12-04', 64]],
  players: 220,
  teams: ['Mon000|Mon001|Mon002|Mon003|Mon004|Mon005', 'Mon006|Mon007|Mon008|Mon009|Mon010|Mon011'],
  m: Array.from({length: 40}, (_, i) => [i % 2, (i + 1) % 2, 1, i % 5, 0, 1]) // constant win for team 0 -> model >= raw or tied -> !ships
});

await test('15. regstate buildRegState on day 3 with 5 events reports day=3, events=5, players=220, games=40', async () => {
  const {buildRegState} = await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(TMP, 'regstate.mjs')).href); /* a file URL works on Windows too (Dos's PC run, Nov 25) */
  const rs = buildRegState({regObj: {id: 'M-D', since: '2026-12-02'}, rawReg: null, matches: rd(d3, 'out/matches.json'), today: '2026-12-04'});
  assert.equal(rs.reg, 'M-D');
  assert.equal(rs.day, 3);
  assert.equal(rs.events, 5);
  assert.equal(rs.players, 220);
  assert.equal(rs.games, 40);
  assert.equal(rs.since, '2026-12-02');

await test('16. model.mjs on day 3 with 5 events writes M-D stamped not-ready model when !ships (does not restore M-C)', () => {
  wr(d3, 'out/model.json', makeFullModel('M-C', '2026-12-01T10:00:00Z'));

  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
  const m = rd(d3, 'out/model.json');

  assert.equal(m.ships, false);
  assert.equal(m.games, 40);

await test('17. model-guard.mjs rejects a broken shipped M-D model (<250 mons) without restoring M-C prev', () => {
  const broken = makeFullModel('M-D', '2026-12-04T10:00:00Z');
  broken.mons = {Mon000: 0.1};
  wr(d3, 'out/model.json', broken);
  wr(d3, 'out/prev_mc.json', makeFullModel('M-C', '2026-12-01T10:00:00Z'));
  const r = run('model-guard.mjs', d3, [], {GUARD_PREV: path.join(d3, 'out/prev_mc.json')});
  assert.equal(r.code, 1);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /only 1 Pokémon/);
  assert.equal(rd(d3, 'out/model.json').reg, 'M-D'); // did NOT overwrite with M-C
});

await test('18. scoreboard.mjs on day 3 does not warn on M-D against M-C 14-day median', () => {
  wr(d3, 'out/scoreboard.json', {
    reg: 'M-C', updated: '2026-12-01T10:00:00Z',
    byReg: {'M-C': Array.from({length: 14}, (_, i) => ({date: `2026-11-${17 + i}`, model: {heldOutAcc: 0.65, calibratedLogloss: 0.62}}))},
    history: []
  });
  const m = makeFullModel('M-D', '2026-12-04T10:00:00Z');
  m.eval.model_teams_only.acc = 0.52; m.eval.calibrated_logloss.with_sets = 0.69;
  wr(d3, 'out/model.json', m);
  const r = run('scoreboard.mjs', d3);
  assert.equal(r.code, 0);
  const sb = rd(d3, 'out/scoreboard.json');
  assert.deepEqual(sb.today.warnings, []);
  assert.equal(sb.byReg['M-C'].length, 14);
});

// --- Scenario 4: Day 20 with enough games (2026-12-21, M-D active) ---
const d4 = path.join(TMP, 's4-day20');
seedHealthy(d4, 'M-D', '2026-12-02', '2026-12-21', true);
wr(d4, 'out/regstate.json', {reg: 'M-D', day: 20, events: 35, players: 2400, games: 6000, since: '2026-12-02', updated: '2026-12-21T10:00:00Z'});

await test('19. health.mjs on day 20 enforces steady-state floors (empty file fails as broken)', () => {
  const d4b = path.join(TMP, 's4-day20-broken');
  fs.cpSync(d4, d4b, {recursive: true});
  wr(d4b, 'out/events.json', {reg: 'M-D', updated: '2026-12-21T10:00:00Z', events: []});
  const r = run('health.mjs', d4b, ['--root=' + d4b, '--now=2026-12-21']);
  assert.equal(r.code, 1);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /fewer than 5 events/);
});

await test('20. track.mjs on day 20 grades M-D events only against M-D snapshots (ignores M-C snapshots)', () => {
  wr(d4, 'out/ratings_log.json', {
    reg: 'M-D', updated: '2026-12-21T10:00:00Z',
    byReg: {
      'M-C': [{d: '2026-11-20', r: {Mon0: 95}}],
      'M-D': [{d: '2026-12-10', r: {Mon0: 82, Mon1: 75, Mon2: 65, Mon3: 55, Mon4: 45, Mon5: 35}}]
    }
  });
  const r = run('track.mjs', d4);
  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
  const tr = rd(d4, 'out/track.json');
  assert.equal(tr.reg, 'M-D');
  assert.equal(tr.since, '2026-12-10');
  assert.equal(tr.snaps, 1);
  assert.ok(tr.ev.length > 0 && tr.ev.every(e => e[5] === '2026-12-10'));
});

await test('21. scoreboard.mjs on day 20 grades 14+ day-old M-D snapshot on M-D games and ignores M-C snapshots', () => {
  const mdSnap = new Date(Date.now() - 20 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const mcSnap = new Date(Date.now() - 40 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const evDay = new Date(Date.now() - 1 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  wr(d4, 'out/ratings_log.json', {
    reg: 'M-D', updated: new Date().toISOString(),
    byReg: {
      'M-C': [{d: mcSnap, r: {Mon000: 99}}],
      'M-D': [{d: mdSnap, r: {Mon000: 90, Mon001: 80, Mon002: 70, Mon003: 60, Mon004: 50, Mon005: 40}}]
    }
  });
  wr(d4, 'out/matches.json', {
    reg: 'M-D', updated: new Date().toISOString(), events: [['e1', evDay, 64]], players: 64,
    teams: ['Mon000|Mon001|Mon002|Mon003|Mon004|Mon005', 'Mon006|Mon007|Mon008|Mon009|Mon010|Mon011'],
    m: Array.from({length: 310}, () => [0, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1])
  });
  const r = run('scoreboard.mjs', d4);
  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.stdout + r.stderr);
  const sb = rd(d4, 'out/scoreboard.json');
  assert.equal(sb.today.ratingsForward.snapshot, mdSnap);
  assert.equal(sb.today.ratingsForward.acc, 1);
});

await test('22. model-guard.mjs on day 20 catches rank reordering against prev M-D model and restores M-D prev', () => {
  const prevMD = makeFullModel('M-D', '2026-12-20T10:00:00Z', 0);
  const badMD = makeFullModel('M-D', '2026-12-21T10:00:00Z', 0);
  for (const k of Object.keys(badMD.mons)) badMD.mons[k] = -badMD.mons[k]; // invert all strengths -> rho ~ -1
  const teams = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < 205; i++) teams.push([0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(o => `Mon${String((i + o) % 260).padStart(3, '0')}`).join('|'));
  wr(d4, 'out/matches.json', {reg: 'M-D', updated: '2026-12-21T10:00:00Z', events: [['e1', '2026-12-21', 64]], players: 64, teams, m: teams.map((_, i) => [i, (i + 1) % teams.length, 1, 0, 0, 1])});
  wr(d4, 'out/model.json', badMD);
  wr(d4, 'out/prev_md.json', prevMD);
  const r = run('model-guard.mjs', d4, [], {GUARD_PREV: path.join(d4, 'out/prev_md.json')});
  assert.equal(r.code, 1);
  assert.match(r.stdout, /rank correlation/);
  assert.equal(rd(d4, 'out/model.json').updated, '2026-12-20T10:00:00Z');
});

fs.rmSync(TMP, {recursive: true, force: true});
console.log(`\nALL ${passed} TESTS PASSED`);

===== FILE: experiments/reg-guard-test.mjs (claude/design6)
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {stampReg} from '../reg-stamp.mjs';
import {guardOut, emptyFor} from '../reg-guard.mjs';

let failed = 0;
function check(name, actual, expected) {
  if (actual === expected) console.log(`pass ${name}`);
  else { failed++; console.log(`FAIL ${name}: expected ${JSON.stringify(expected)}, got ${JSON.stringify(actual)}`); }
}

const tmp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(process.env.TMPDIR || '/tmp', 'reg-guard-test-'));
const runCase = (regDoc, files) => {
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(tmp, 'c-'));
  const dataDir = path.join(dir, 'data');
  fs.mkdirSync(dataDir);
  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dataDir, 'index.json'), JSON.stringify({tournaments: {t1: {d: '2026-11-20', f: 'M-C'}}}));
  const outDir = path.join(dir, 'out');
  fs.mkdirSync(outDir);
  if (regDoc) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, 'reg.json'), JSON.stringify(regDoc));
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(files)) {
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outDir, k), typeof v === 'string' ? v : JSON.stringify(v));
  }
  const res = guardOut({outDir, regFile: path.join(outDir, 'reg.json'), log: () => {}});
  const read = f => JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(outDir, f), 'utf8'));
  const rawData = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dataDir, 'index.json'), 'utf8'));
  return {res, read, rawData};
};

try {
  // 1. stampReg adds reg without changing existing fields
  const orig = {updated: '2026-12-02', teams: [1, 2]};
  const stamped = stampReg(orig, 'M-D');
  check('stampReg-adds-reg', `${stamped.reg}/${stamped.teams.length}/${orig.reg}`, 'M-D/2/undefined');

  // 2. M-D active: M-D stamped file stays ok
  const mdReg = {id: 'M-D', since: '2026-12-02', sd: 'gen9championsvgc2026regmd', switched: {from: 'M-C', to: 'M-D', on: '2026-12-02'}};
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'limitless.json': {reg: 'M-D', teams: 42}});
    check('md-stamped-stays-ok', res.lines.includes('ok limitless.json') && read('limitless.json').teams === 42, true);
  }

  // 3. M-D active: M-C stamped model.json is emptied
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'model.json': {reg: 'M-C', ships: true, mons: {Incineroar: 0.5}}});
    check('mc-model-emptied', res.lines.includes('emptied model.json (was M-C)') && read('model.json').ships === false && read('model.json').reg === 'M-D', true);
  }

  // 4. M-D active: M-C stamped official.json is emptied
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'official.json': {reg: 'M-C', events: [{name: 'LAIC'}]}});
    check('mc-official-emptied', res.lines.includes('emptied official.json (was M-C)') && read('official.json').events.length === 0, true);
  }

  // 5. M-D active (switched): unstamped sources.json is emptied
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'sources.json': {updated: '2026-12-01', sdt: {rows: [1]}}});
    check('unstamped-after-switch-emptied', res.lines.includes('emptied sources.json (was unstamped)') && read('sources.json').sdt === undefined && read('sources.json').reg === 'M-D', true);
  }

  // 6. M-C active (before switch): unstamped legacy file stays ok
  const mcReg = {id: 'M-C', since: '2026-09-01', sd: 'gen9championsvgc2026regmc', switched: null};
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mcReg, {'sources.json': {updated: '2026-11-24', sdt: {rows: [1]}}});
    check('unstamped-before-switch-ok', res.lines.includes('ok sources.json') && read('sources.json').sdt.rows.length === 1, true);
  }

  // 7. M-C active: M-D stamped file (wrong reg) is emptied
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mcReg, {'teams.json': {reg: 'M-D', teams: [{event: 'Future'}]}});
    check('wrong-reg-before-switch-emptied', res.lines.includes('emptied teams.json (was M-D)') && read('teams.json').teams.length === 0 && read('teams.json').reg === 'M-C', true);
  }

  // 8. perf.json with object reg {id: 'M-D'} stays ok (does not break object reg)
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'perf.json': {updated: '2026-12-02', reg: {id: 'M-D', name: 'Reg M-D'}, tour: {rows: [1]}}});
    check('perf-object-reg-ok', res.lines.includes('ok perf.json') && read('perf.json').tour.rows.length === 1, true);
  }

  // 9. perf.json with object reg {id: 'M-C'} is emptied when M-D active
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'perf.json': {updated: '2026-12-01', reg: {id: 'M-C', name: 'Reg M-C'}, tour: {rows: [1]}}});
    check('perf-object-reg-mismatch-emptied', res.lines.includes('emptied perf.json (was M-C)') && read('perf.json').tour === undefined && read('perf.json').reg.id === 'M-D', true);
  }

  // 10. Exempt files (reg.json, calendar.json, skips.json) stay untouched
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'calendar.json': {ev: [{n: 'Regional'}]}, 'skips.json': {runs: [{day: '2026-12-02'}]}});
    check('exempt-files-untouched', res.lines.includes('ok reg.json') && res.lines.includes('ok calendar.json') && res.lines.includes('ok skips.json') && read('calendar.json').ev.length === 1, true);
  }

  // 11. Unknown .json file prints "unknown <file>" and is not deleted or modified
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'mystery.json': {hello: 1}});
    check('unknown-file-reported', res.lines.includes('unknown mystery.json') && read('mystery.json').hello === 1, true);
  }

  // 12. Corrupt JSON file after switch is replaced with empty state
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'weekly.json': '{not valid json'});
    check('corrupt-json-emptied', res.lines.includes('emptied weekly.json (was unstamped)') && read('weekly.json').enough === false && read('weekly.json').v === 1, true);
  }

  // 13. Raw data/ folder is never touched
  {
    const {rawData} = runCase(mdReg, {'model.json': {reg: 'M-C', ships: true}});
    check('data-folder-untouched', rawData.tournaments.t1.f, 'M-C');
  }

  // 14. replays.json empty state carries activeReg().sd as format
  {
    const e = emptyFor('replays.json', 'M-D', 'gen9championsvgc2026regmd');
    check('replays-empty-carries-sd', `${e.reg}/${e.format}/${e.games.length}`, 'M-D/gen9championsvgc2026regmd/0');
  }
  // 15. Scout's review (Nov 24): files holding every regulation's history are never emptied after a switch
  {
    const {res, read} = runCase(mdReg, {'scoreboard.json': {reg: 'M-C', byReg: {'M-C': [1, 2, 3]}}, 'ratings_log.json': {reg: 'M-C', days: [{d: '2026-11-30'}]}});
    check('history-files-kept-after-switch', res.lines.includes('ok scoreboard.json') && res.lines.includes('ok ratings_log.json') && read('scoreboard.json').byReg['M-C'].length === 3 && read('ratings_log.json').days.length === 1, true);
  }
  // 16. an emptied file is archived first (out/archive/<regulation>/), so nothing is ever lost
  {
    const {res} = runCase(mdReg, {'model.json': {reg: 'M-C', ships: true, mons: {Incineroar: 0.5}}});
    const dir = path.dirname(res.lines.length ? tmp : tmp);
    const found = fs.readdirSync(tmp).some(c => { const a = path.join(tmp, c, 'out', 'archive', 'M-C', 'model.json'); return fs.existsSync(a) && (JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(a, 'utf8')).mons || {}).Incineroar === 0.5; });
    check('emptied-file-archived-first', found, true);
  }
} finally {
  fs.rmSync(tmp, {recursive: true, force: true});
}

console.log(failed ? `${failed} FAILED` : 'ALL PASSED');
process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);

===== FILE: experiments/reg-test.mjs (claude/design6)
// Regulation change handling (Nov 1): feeds made-up data with a new regulation and checks
//   1) detection (formats.mjs detectReg): a new regulation in the newest week is seen, the numbers stay on the old one until
//      it has enough events, then switch; Showdown-only evidence is seen too; a quiet week changes nothing
//   2) no mixing: the event filters (sameReg, inRegWindow) keep exactly one regulation's events
//   3) the page: the calm banner on Home and the Meta pages (not elsewhere), the right words, nothing when there's no new one
// Usage: node experiments/reg-test.mjs [shotDir]
import fs from 'node:fs'; import {createRequire} from 'node:module';
import {detectReg, sameReg, inRegWindow, activeReg, regOf, REG_SWITCH, SITE_REG} from '../formats.mjs';
const fails = []; const ok = (c, m) => { console.log((c ? '  ok   ' : '  FAIL ') + m); if (!c) fails.push(m); };
const day = (base, k) => new Date(Date.parse(base + 'T12:00:00Z') + k * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
// 40 days of Reg M-C events, then a new Reg M-D starting on Nov 2 with a few events
const mc = Array.from({length: 120}, (_, i) => ({format: 'M-C', date: day('2026-09-25', Math.floor(i / 3)), players: 40}));
const md = n => Array.from({length: n}, (_, i) => ({format: 'M-D', date: day('2026-11-02', Math.floor(i / 4)), players: 70}));
const sv = [{format: 'SVI', date: '2026-11-05', players: 300}]; // another game's format: ignored
console.log('detection');
let r = detectReg([...mc, ...sv], {}, null, '2026-11-03');
ok(r.id === 'M-C' && !r.now, 'only Reg M-C events: stays on Reg M-C, no banner');
r = detectReg([...mc.filter(t => t.date < '2026-10-28'), ...md(6), ...sv], {}, null, '2026-11-04');
ok(r.id === 'M-C' && r.now && r.now.id === 'M-D' && r.now.since === '2026-11-02', `newest week mostly Reg M-D (6 events): detected, started Nov 2, numbers stay on Reg M-C`);
ok(r.seen['M-C'] && r.seen['M-D'] && !r.seen.SVI, 'counts per regulation kept apart (other games ignored)');
const mixedWeek = detectReg([...mc, ...md(2)], {}, null, '2026-11-03');
ok(mixedWeek.id === 'M-C' && !mixedWeek.now, 'two new-format events in a week still mostly Reg M-C: no banner');
const big = md(REG_SWITCH.events + 4);
r = detectReg([...mc.filter(t => t.date < '2026-10-28'), ...big], {}, r, '2026-11-09');
ok(r.id === 'M-D' && !r.now && r.since === '2026-11-02' && r.switched && r.switched.from === 'M-C', `enough Reg M-D data (${big.length} events, ${big.length * 70} players): switches, since Nov 2`);
ok(/regmd$/.test(r.sd), 'Showdown format follows the switch (' + r.sd + ')');
const back = detectReg([...mc], {}, r, '2026-11-10');
ok(back.id === 'M-D', 'once switched it never falls back to the old regulation');
r = detectReg(mc, {gen9championsvgc2026regmd: {n: 51, first: '2026-11-02'}}, null, '2026-11-03');
ok(r.id === 'M-C' && r.now && r.now.id === 'M-D' && r.now.from === 'showdown', 'Showdown replays of the next format alone: banner, numbers unchanged');
console.log('no mixing');
const index = {a: {d: '2026-10-20', name: 'Weekly'}, b: {d: '2026-10-21', name: 'Weekly', f: 'M-C'}, c: {d: '2026-11-03', name: 'Weekly', f: 'M-D'}};
ok(regOf(index.a) === SITE_REG.id, 'events saved before Nov 1 (no format stored) count as Reg M-C');
ok(Object.keys(index).filter(id => sameReg(index[id], 'M-C')).join() === 'a,b', 'Reg M-C numbers use only Reg M-C events');
ok(Object.keys(index).filter(id => sameReg(index[id], 'M-D')).join() === 'c', 'Reg M-D numbers use only Reg M-D events');
// Nov 17 date guard: Limitless still tags events after M-C's official end (Dec 1, 5:59 PM PST) as M-C
const late = {x: {d: '2026-12-01T20:00:00.000Z', f: 'M-C'}, y: {d: '2026-12-03T17:00:00.000Z', f: 'M-C'}, z: {d: '2026-12-05T17:00:00.000Z', f: 'M-D'}};
ok(Object.keys(late).filter(id => sameReg(late[id], 'M-C')).join() === 'x', 'an event tagged M-C but dated after M-C ends never counts for M-C');
ok(Object.keys(late).filter(id => sameReg(late[id], 'M-D')).join() === 'y,z', '... it counts for M-D, with the events tagged M-D');
const tagged = Array.from({length: 30}, (_, i) => ({format: 'M-C', date: day('2026-12-02', Math.floor(i / 4)) + 'T17:00:00.000Z', players: 60}));
const det = detectReg([...mc.filter(t => t.date >= '2026-11-20'), ...tagged], {}, null, '2026-12-10');
ok(det.id === 'M-D' || (det.now && det.now.id === 'M-D'), 'detection sees M-D even while Limitless still tags its events M-C (' + (det.id + (det.now ? ' now ' + det.now.id : '')) + ')');
ok(activeReg('/nonexistent/reg.json').end === '2026-12-02' && !inRegWindow('2026-12-03', activeReg('/nonexistent/reg.json')), 'official events after M-C ends are outside M-C\'s window');
const win = {since: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-11-02'};
ok(inRegWindow('2026-10-24', win) && !inRegWindow('2026-11-07', win) && !inRegWindow('2026-08-20', win), 'official events: only inside the regulation\'s dates');
const tmp = new URL('./reg-test-tmp.json', import.meta.url).pathname;
fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(detectReg([...mc.filter(t => t.date < '2026-10-28'), ...md(6)], {}, null, '2026-11-04')));
const A = activeReg(tmp); fs.unlinkSync(tmp);
ok(A.id === 'M-C' && A.end === '2026-11-02' && A.sd === SITE_REG.sd, 'active regulation while the new one builds: Reg M-C, window ends Nov 2');
// the page
console.log('page');
const {chromium} = createRequire(import.meta.url)('playwright');
const APP = new URL('../app/', import.meta.url).pathname, OUT = APP + '../out/', rd = f => { try { return JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(OUT + f, 'utf8')); } catch (e) { return null; } };
const L = rd('limitless.json'), P = rd('perf.json') || {};
const page = APP + 'reg-test.html'; fs.writeFileSync(page, '<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">' + fs.readFileSync(APP + 'dist/page.html', 'utf8').replace('render();setView(view,false);', 'window.__ev=c=>eval(c);render();setView(view,false);'));
const shots = process.argv[2]; if (shots) fs.mkdirSync(shots, {recursive: true});
const b = await chromium.launch();
async function load(reg, theme) { const D = {'meta/results': L.results, 'meta/perf': {tour: P.tour, ingame: P.ingame, adj: L.adj, updated: L.updated.slice(0, 10), reg}, 'meta/doubles_tour': rd('meta_tour.json'), 'meta/teams': rd('teams.json'), 'meta/history': rd('history.json'), 'meta/events': rd('events.json')};

  pg.on('pageerror', e => fails.push('page error: ' + e.message));
  await pg.addInitScript(D => { window.claude = {use: async n => n === 'db' ? {doc: p => ({onSnapshot: cb => { setTimeout(() => cb({exists: !!D[p], data: () => D[p]}), 5); return () => {}; }, get: async () => ({exists: false}), set: async () => {}})} : null};
    try { localStorage.setItem('cc2.welcomed', '"1"'); localStorage.setItem('cc2.seenNew', '"x"'); } catch (e) {} }, D);
  await pg.goto('file://' + page); await pg.waitForTimeout(3500); if (theme) await pg.evaluate(t => window.__ev(`setTheme('${t}')`), theme); return pg; }
const bar = async (pg, v) => { await pg.evaluate(v => window.__ev(`setView('${v}');scrollTo(0,0)`), v); await pg.waitForTimeout(250);
  return pg.evaluate(() => { const e = document.getElementById('regBar'); return e && !e.hidden && getComputedStyle(e).display !== 'none' ? e.textContent : ''; }); };
const newReg = detectReg([...mc.filter(t => t.date < '2026-10-28'), ...md(6)], {}, null, '2026-11-04');
let pg = await load({...newReg, seen: newReg.seen});
const home = await bar(pg, 'home');
ok(/New regulation \(Reg M-D\) started Nov 2, 2026/.test(home) && /numbers below are from Reg M-C until there's enough/.test(home), 'Home banner: ' + home.slice(0, 120));
ok(/6 Reg M-D events so far(?: ·|,) switches at 20/.test(home) && /never mixed/.test(home), 'banner says how far along it is and that the two are never mixed');
ok(/Reg M-D/.test(await bar(pg, 'ratings')) && /Reg M-D/.test(await bar(pg, 'meta')), 'banner on the Meta pages (Pokémon ratings, Usage)');
ok(!(await bar(pg, 'calc')) && !(await bar(pg, 'report')), 'no banner on My team or the calc');
const head = await pg.evaluate(() => { window.__ev("setView('ratings')"); const k = document.querySelector('#lair .chpghead .chk'); return k ? k.textContent : ''; });
ok(/Reg M.C doubles/.test(head), 'ratings still say Reg M-C while the new one builds');
if (shots) { for (const t of ['light', 'dark']) { await pg.evaluate(t => window.__ev(`setTheme('${t}');setView('home');scrollTo(0,0)`), t); await pg.waitForTimeout(400); await pg.screenshot({path: `${shots}/reg-home-${t}.png`});
  await pg.evaluate(() => window.__ev("setView('meta');scrollTo(0,0)")); await pg.waitForTimeout(400); await pg.screenshot({path: `${shots}/reg-meta-${t}.png`}); } }
await pg.context().close();
pg = await load(P.reg || {id: 'M-C', since: '2026-09-01', now: null});
ok(!(await bar(pg, 'home')) && !(await bar(pg, 'ratings')), 'no banner when every event is Reg M-C (today\'s real data)');
await pg.context().close();
pg = await load({id: 'M-D', since: '2026-11-02', now: null, switched: {from: 'M-C', to: 'M-D', on: '2026-11-09'}});
const sw = await bar(pg, 'home');
ok(/Numbers are from Reg M-D/.test(sw) && /Reg M-C results are no longer counted/.test(sw), 'after a switch: says the numbers are Reg M-D only');

pg = await load({id: 'bad', now: {id: 'zz'}});
ok(!(await bar(pg, 'home')), 'malformed regulation data: no banner, no error');
await pg.context().close();
await b.close(); fs.unlinkSync(page);
console.log(fails.length ? `\n${fails.length} failed` : '\nall passed'); process.exit(fails.length ? 1 : 0);

===== FILE: experiments/reg-switch-test.mjs (claude/design6)
import fs from 'node:fs';
import {
  REG_END, REG_SWITCH, SITE_REG,
  regAt, regOf, sameReg, inRegWindow, activeReg, detectReg,
} from '../formats.mjs';

let failed = 0;
function check(name, actual, expected) {
  const ok = actual === expected;
  if (ok) console.log(`pass ${name}`);
  else { failed++; console.log(`FAIL ${name}: expected ${JSON.stringify(expected)}, got ${JSON.stringify(actual)}`); }
}

const mkEvents = (format, n, playersEach, date = '2026-12-03T12:00:00Z') =>
  Array.from({length: n}, () => ({format, date, players: playersEach}));

// A0: the official end (Pokémon HOME news 878, checked Nov 25) and the page's own copy of it agree
check('mc-end-official', REG_END['M-C'], '2026-12-02T01:59:00Z');
{ const sh = fs.readFileSync(new URL('../app/src/v2/shell.js', import.meta.url), 'utf8').match(/^V2\.REG_END_MC = '([^']+)';/m);
  check('page-copy-matches', sh && sh[1], REG_END['M-C']); }

// A1: Event -> regulation date & tag edges
check('pacific-eve-before-end', regAt('M-C', '2026-12-01T17:58:00-08:00'), 'M-C');
check('pacific-eve-exact-end', regAt('M-C', '2026-12-01T17:59:00-08:00'), 'M-D');
check('utc-midnight-before-end', regAt('M-C', '2026-12-02T00:00:00Z'), 'M-C');
check('utc-015859-before-end', regAt('M-C', '2026-12-02T01:58:59Z'), 'M-C');
check('utc-015900-exact-end', regAt('M-C', '2026-12-02T01:59:00Z'), 'M-D');
check('tagged-mc-dated-dec3', regAt('M-C', '2026-12-03T12:00:00Z'), 'M-D');
check('no-tag-nov30', regOf({d: '2026-11-30'}), 'M-C');
check('no-tag-dec3', regOf({d: '2026-12-03T12:00:00Z'}), 'M-D');
check('no-date-keeps-tag', regOf({f: 'M-C'}), 'M-C');
check('sameReg-tagged-mc-dec3-is-md', sameReg({f: 'M-C', d: '2026-12-03T12:00:00Z'}, 'M-D'), true);

// A2: inRegWindow & activeReg().end
check('inRegWindow-dec1-ok', inRegWindow('2026-12-01', {since: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-12-02'}), true);
check('inRegWindow-dec2-excluded', inRegWindow('2026-12-02', {since: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-12-02'}), false);
check('inRegWindow-empty-date', inRegWindow('', {since: '2026-09-01', end: '2026-12-02'}), false);

const tmpFile = `${process.env.TMPDIR || '/tmp'}/reg-switch-test-${process.pid}.json`;
try {
  fs.writeFileSync(tmpFile, JSON.stringify({id: 'M-C', since: '2026-09-01', now: {id: 'M-D', since: '2026-12-03'}}));
  check('activeReg-end-capped-by-REG_END', activeReg(tmpFile).end, REG_END['M-C'].slice(0, 10));
} finally {
  try { fs.unlinkSync(tmpFile); } catch {}
}

// A3: detectReg & REG_SWITCH thresholds
const prevMC = {id: 'M-C', since: SITE_REG.since, sd: SITE_REG.sd};

const belowEv = detectReg(mkEvents('M-D', REG_SWITCH.events - 1, 100), {}, prevMC, '2026-12-05');
check('switch-below-events', `${belowEv.id}/${belowEv.now && belowEv.now.id}`, 'M-C/M-D');

const belowPl = detectReg(
  [...mkEvents('M-D', REG_SWITCH.events - 1, 60), {format: 'M-D', date: '2026-12-03T12:00:00Z', players: 59}],
  {}, prevMC, '2026-12-05'
);
check('switch-below-players', `${belowPl.id}/${belowPl.now && belowPl.now.id}`, 'M-C/M-D');

const oneBig = detectReg(mkEvents('M-D', 1, 2000), {}, prevMC, '2026-12-03');
check('switch-one-big-event', `${oneBig.id}/${oneBig.now && oneBig.now.id}`, 'M-C/M-D');

const atThresh = detectReg(mkEvents('M-D', REG_SWITCH.events, 60), {}, prevMC, '2026-12-10');
check('switch-at-threshold', `${atThresh.id}/${atThresh.now}/${atThresh.switched && atThresh.switched.to}`, 'M-D/null/M-D');

// REG_AUTO_SWITCH=0 holds switch
const oldEnv = process.env.REG_AUTO_SWITCH;
process.env.REG_AUTO_SWITCH = '0';
const {detectReg: detectRegNoAuto} = await import('../formats.mjs?noauto=1');
if (oldEnv === undefined) delete process.env.REG_AUTO_SWITCH; else process.env.REG_AUTO_SWITCH = oldEnv;
const held = detectRegNoAuto(mkEvents('M-D', REG_SWITCH.events, 60), {}, prevMC, '2026-12-10');
check('switch-held-auto-0', `${held.id}/${held.now && held.now.id}/${held.switched}`, 'M-C/M-D/null');

// Showdown-only ladder data sets banner but never switches
const sdOnly = detectReg([], {gen9championsvgc2026regmd: {n: 51, first: '2026-12-02'}}, prevMC, '2026-12-02');
check('showdown-only-no-switch', `${sdOnly.id}/${sdOnly.now && sdOnly.now.id}/${sdOnly.now && sdOnly.now.from}`, 'M-C/M-D/showdown');

// Never flips back from M-D to M-C
const prevMD = {id: 'M-D', since: '2026-12-02', sd: 'gen9championsvgc2026regmd', switched: {from: 'M-C', to: 'M-D', on: '2026-12-10'}};
const noBack = detectReg(
  [...mkEvents('M-C', 30, 100, '2026-11-30T12:00:00Z'), ...mkEvents('M-D', 5, 80, '2026-12-12T12:00:00Z')],
  {}, prevMD, '2026-12-12'
);
check('never-flips-back-to-mc', `${noBack.id}/${noBack.now}`, 'M-D/null');

console.log(failed ? `${failed} FAILED` : 'ALL PASSED');
process.exit(failed ? 1 : 0);

===== FILE: experiments/unrated-test.mjs (claude/design6)
// Not rated yet (Sim, Nov 25, 6:52 AM): on a new regulation's early days (Reg M-D from Dec 2), a Pokémon with no tournament results for
// that regulation shows "Not rated yet": no number, no tier, no rank, until it has results. Before this, with no results ratings() fell back
// to stats and Gyarados showed "97 S tier #1 right now".
// Two parts:
//  1. day one: the page is put in Reg M-D's first day with no results (META.reg / META.perf.reg = {id: 'M-D', since: '2026-12-02'}; the
//     built-in M-C snapshot is then refused by builtinOk(), and RES is emptied), then #meta, #home, #pokemon=Gyarados, #box and #sets are
//     visited at 390 and 1440. Fails if the visible text (or a button's spoken label) gives any single Pokémon a rating, a tier or a rank.
//  2. today (the page's own Reg M-C data): V2.unrated is false for every Pokémon, V2.noRatings is false, and the Meta page still shows tiers
//     and "#1 right now" (so the day-one checks above could fail).
//   PAGE=app/dist/v2.html node experiments/unrated-test.mjs   (a --v2 build; exit 1 on any failure)
import {chromium} from 'playwright'; import path from 'node:path';
const PAGE = path.resolve(process.env.PAGE || 'app/dist/v2.html');
let fails = 0; const ok = (c, m) => { console.log((c ? '  pass  ' : '  FAIL  ') + m); if (!c) fails++; };
const b = await chromium.launch();
const DAY1 = `(() => { const R = {id: 'M-D', since: '2026-12-02'}; META.reg = R; META.perf = {reg: R}; META.results = null; RES = null; metaVersion++;
  S.box = ['Gyarados'].concat((S.box || []).filter(n => n !== 'Gyarados')); return 'ok'; })()`;
// what a rating looks like in words: "97", "S tier", "#1 right now", "#3 of 40", "Rated 97", "Why it rates 97"
const BAD = [[/\b[SABCDF]\s*tier\b/, 'a tier'], [/#\d+ right now/i, '"#N right now"'], [/right now\s*#?1\b/i, 'a #1'], [/#\d+ of \d+/, 'a rank "#N of M"'],
  [/\brated\s+(number\s+)?\d+(?!\s*[–-])/i, '"rated N"'], [/\brates\s+\d/i, '"rates N"'], [/\bThe top 10\b/i, 'the top 10'], [/\bThe four highest rated\b/i, 'the four highest rated']];
const scan = async q => q.evaluate(() => { const vis = e => { const r = e.getBoundingClientRect(), s = getComputedStyle(e); return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && s.visibility !== 'hidden' && s.display !== 'none'; };
  const main = document.querySelector('#main') || document.body;
  return {text: main.innerText, labels: [...main.querySelectorAll('[aria-label]')].filter(vis).map(e => e.getAttribute('aria-label')).join('\n')}; });
const open = async (w, hash, fake) => { const ctx = await b.newContext({viewport: {width: w, height: w < 700 ? 844 : 900}}); const q = await ctx.newPage();
  const errs = []; q.on('pageerror', e => errs.push(String(e.message || e)));
  await q.addInitScript(() => { window.__PL_TEST = 1; }); await q.goto('file://' + PAGE + '#about', {timeout: 90000}); await q.waitForTimeout(2500);
  if (fake) await q.evaluate(c => window.__ev(c), DAY1);
  await q.evaluate(h => { location.hash = h; }, hash); await q.waitForTimeout(1500); return {q, ctx, errs}; };

console.log('Day one of Reg M-D, no results:');
for (const w of [390, 1440]) for (const h of ['#meta', '#home', '#pokemon=Gyarados', '#box', '#sets']) {
  const {q, ctx, errs} = await open(w, h, true);
  const st = await q.evaluate(() => window.__ev(`JSON.stringify({none: typeof V2.noRatings === 'function' ? V2.noRatings() : 'missing', gy: (rating(byName.get('Gyarados')) || {}).total, reg: (regInfo() || {}).id})`));
  const {text, labels} = await scan(q), hits = [];
  for (const [re, what] of BAD) { const m = re.exec(text); if (m) hits.push(`${what} in the text: "…${text.slice(Math.max(0, m.index - 40), m.index + 30).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}…"`);
    const n = re.exec(labels); if (n) hits.push(`${what} in a spoken label: "…${labels.slice(Math.max(0, n.index - 50), n.index + 30).replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}…"`); }
  ok(!hits.length && !errs.length, `${h} at ${w}: no rating, tier or rank for an unrated Pokémon ${st}${hits.length ? '\n          ' + hits.slice(0, 4).join('\n          ') : ''}${errs.length ? '\n          page error: ' + errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') : ''}`);
  if (h === '#pokemon=Gyarados') { ok(/Not rated yet/.test(text) && !/\b97\b(?!%)/.test(text), `${h} at ${w}: Gyarados says "Not rated yet", never 97`);
    ok(!/against the \d+ Pokémon with tournament results/.test(text) && !/#\d+ of \d+ with/.test(text), `${h} at ${w}: no claim of tournament results that don't exist`); }
  await ctx.close(); }

console.log('Today (the page\'s own Reg M-C data): unchanged');
{ const {q, ctx, errs} = await open(1440, '#meta', false);
  const r = JSON.parse(await q.evaluate(() => window.__ev(`JSON.stringify({has: typeof V2.unrated === 'function', none: typeof V2.noRatings === 'function' ? V2.noRatings() : null,
    young: typeof V2.regYoung === 'function' ? V2.regYoung() : null, unr: typeof V2.unrated === 'function' ? ROSTER.filter(p => V2.unrated(p)).map(p => p.n) : null, n: ROSTER.length,
    reg: regInfo(), rated: lairRows().length, same: typeof V2.ratedRows === 'function' ? V2.ratedRows().map(x => x.p.n).join() === lairRows().map(x => x.p.n).join() : null})`)));
  ok(r.has && r.none === false && r.young === false && Array.isArray(r.unr) && r.unr.length === 0 && r.same === true,
    `every one of ${r.n} Pokémon is rated as before (${JSON.stringify({reg: r.reg, noRatings: r.none, regYoung: r.young, unrated: r.unr && r.unr.slice(0, 5), sameRows: r.same})})`);
  const {text} = await scan(q);
  ok(/#1 right now/.test(text) && /\b[SA] tier\b/.test(text), 'the Meta page still shows "#1 right now" and tiers (so the day-one checks can fail)');
  /* the live site's own regulation line (out/reg.json: M-C since Sep 1, day 86 today): still past the first 28 days, so nothing changes */
  const r2 = JSON.parse(await q.evaluate(() => window.__ev(`(() => { META.reg = {id: 'M-C', since: '2026-09-01'}; metaVersion++;
    return JSON.stringify({reg: regInfo(), young: typeof V2.regYoung === 'function' ? V2.regYoung() : null, unr: typeof V2.unrated === 'function' ? ROSTER.filter(p => V2.unrated(p)).length : null}); })()`)));
  ok(r2.young === false && r2.unr === 0, `with the live regulation line (Reg M-C since Sep 1) nobody is unrated (${JSON.stringify(r2)})`);
  ok(!errs.length, 'no page errors' + (errs.length ? ': ' + errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ') : ''));
  await ctx.close(); }
await b.close();
console.log(fails ? `unrated test: ${fails} FAILED` : 'unrated test: all passed');
process.exit(fails ? 1 : 0);

===== FILE: docs/rebuild/md-start-night-plan.md (claude/design6)
# Reg M-D start night: Tue Dec 1, 2026 (US evening) = Wed Dec 2, 02:00 UTC — the exact plan (HH, Nov 25, 3:40 PM)
Times: M-C ends 01:59 UTC Dec 2 (Tue Dec 1 5:59 PM PT / 8:59 PM ET); M-D starts 02:00 UTC (6:00 PM PT / 9:00 PM ET).
Source of truth: Pokémon HOME news 878 (in-game). pokemon.com's "Wed Dec 2 6 PM PST" is a slip (checked Nov 25).

## Before (by Mon Nov 30)
- The Dec 2 balance-patch batch is ready and tested on ONE branch (patch-dec2-map.md + patch-dec2-psychup.md: move accuracies,
  Flash Fire / Stalwart texts, the Psych Up list...), with experiments/rules-fixes-test.mjs passing against a faked clock after 01:59Z.
  Reason: from 01:59Z that test fails CI until the patch data is in, and a red main blocks Promote (live data would freeze).
- The "Reg M-D has started; numbers switch at the next update" line (design6) merged, pictures shown to Sim.
- Re-read HOME news 878 and the in-game notice for any change to the times (a day early, so there's time to adjust).

## On the night (HH, Sim watching if he likes; times ET)
1. 8:45 PM: check main's last test run is green and no data job is running (gh api).
2. 8:59 PM (01:59Z): M-C ends. The site still shows M-C data, the countdown hides, the "M-D has started" line shows from 9:00 PM.
3. 9:00 PM: confirm M-D is live from an official source (in-game news / official account). If not: wait, recheck every 15 min, change nothing.
4. 9:05 PM: land the patch batch on main through a test branch (GitHub green first; the merge gate for any merge).
5. 9:05 PM, or right after step 4 is green: run "Update Limitless data" by hand (workflow_dispatch) so the switch to M-D doesn't wait
   for the 09:07 UTC daily run (a 3-7 hour hole otherwise). Watch: data job -> tests -> Promote -> live.
6. After Promote: check live's out/reg.json says M-D, since 2026-12-02; the site shows "Day 1 of Reg M-D", "Not rated yet", the
   Strength Score labelled "Graded against Reg M-C's teams until Reg M-D results arrive".
7. Tell NP - Marketing M-D is live (their post still waits for nastyplot.org to be live, Dec 2-3).
8. Log everything with its time; morning summary to Sim.

## If something goes wrong
- Tests red after the patch: fix forward on a branch; live keeps yesterday's data (it never moves while main is red).
- Data job fails: its last good data stays live (each step keeps the old file); rerun by hand after a fix.
- Official times change: update regulation.json + tests first, then everything else.

## Changes from Scout's review (Nov 25, 5:53 PM; docs/rebuild/test-runs/scout-reports-nov25.txt), adopted
- HIGH 1: the night's test branch is made from origin/main with ONLY the 4 patch commits cherry-picked (8b1a2dd8, a381ae97,
  33daaf70, 47607635 or their rebased copies), never the whole alt/patch-dec2 branch (it carries 44 design6 commits).
- HIGH 2: the 24 new M-D Pokémon + 4 Megas are not in our data yet. Add a roster step AFTER the patch: confirm the roster
  generator, run it, then re-run experiments/apply-dec2-patch.mjs (a regenerated LEARN would drop the patch's moves) and
  require patch-dec2-test 103/103. Sim OKs the change list.
- MEDIUM 3: get that main+patch test branch GREEN on Tue Dec 1 afternoon (it passes before 01:59Z too). At 9 PM ET only: confirm
  M-D live AND the balance patch in the game (MEDIUM 5: news 877 gives no hour), merge gate, push main, start the data job.
- MEDIUM 4: FAST_DATA stays off until the patch is on main (step 1 checks it).

===== FILE: docs/rebuild/test-runs/patch-dec2-map.md (claude/design6)
# Dec 2 balance patch: everything in our code and data it touches (report only, Nov 24)

Source of the patch list: CLAUDE.md "Balance patch on Wed Dec 2, 2026" (official notes: news.pokemon-home.com/en/page/877.html,
not re-read here). Searched at claude/design6 33ec968b: app/src, app/test, experiments, out/. Every line below is **checked**
(grep plus reading the line) unless marked otherwise. Nothing changed.

## Generated files (change by re-running the generator, never by hand)
| File | What it holds today | Patch change | Generator |
|---|---|---|---|
| app/src/data/move-acc.js (MOVE_ACC) | `"Rock Slide":90`, `"Steel Wing":90`, `"Frost Breath":90`, `"Screech":85`, `"Metal Sound":85` | Rock Slide 95; Steel Wing, Frost Breath, Screech, Metal Sound reach 100, so they **drop out** of the list (it only lists moves under 100) | experiments/gen-move-meta.mjs |
| app/src/data/move-meta.js (MOVE_META) | `"Rock Slide":{"t":"allAdjacentFoes","a":90,"s":[[30,0,0,1]],"sf":1}`; the other four carry `"a"` too | Rock Slide `a:95`, flinch `s:[[20,0,0,1]]`; the others lose `a` | same script |
| app/src/data/info-text.js (INFO_TEXT) | `"Rock Slide":["30% chance to make the foe(s) flinch.",12]`; Flash Fire "…1.5× damage if hit by one Fire move…"; Stalwart "…cannot be redirected…" | Rock Slide 20%; Stalwart adds "ignores Substitute"; Flash Fire: "keeps the boost after Mega Evolution", only if Showdown's text gets it | experiments/gen-info-text.mjs (our own wording goes in its OVR table) |

Both generators read Pokémon Showdown's champions mod **pinned at commit 1242c1e** (gen-move-meta.mjs lines 2-4). On Dec 2 they
need a Showdown commit that has the patch, and the pin in both file headers moves to that commit. The default source path in
gen-move-meta.mjs is `C:/Users/<you>/code/pl-mech/sim`, so the scripts run on Sim's PC or with the path passed as an argument.

## Hand-written (edit by hand)
| File:line | Today | Change |
|---|---|---|
| app/src/data/data.js LEARN (learnsets, as indexes into MOVES) | none of the new pairings: Emolga Tailwind, Hydreigon Roost, Beedrill Roost, Emboar Slack Off, Umbreon Wonder Room, Play Rough on Vaporeon, Jolteon, Flareon, Espeon, Umbreon, Leafeon, Glaceon (checked: all false). Sylveon already has Play Rough. | Add each pairing. Psych Up goes to 22 Pokémon: **the list is not in the repo** (CLAUDE.md says only "x22"), so it must come from the official notes. Not checked. |
| app/src/data/data.js MOVES | all the moves already exist as rows (Psych Up, Slack Off, Wonder Room, Roost, Tailwind, Play Rough) | no change (rows hold type, category, power; no accuracy) |
| app/src/data/data.js ABIL | Flash Fire "This Pokemon's Fire attacks do 1.5× damage if hit by one Fire move; Fire immunity."; Stalwart "This Pokemon's moves cannot be redirected to a different target by any effect." | Update Flash Fire (Fire Boost status persists after Mega Evolution) and Stalwart (ignores targets' substitutes) to match official Dec 2 wording |
| app/src/v2/pages/infosheet.js:15 | Rock Slide: `'Hits both foes. Each one hit has a 30% chance to flinch.'` | 20% |
| app/src/v2/pages/infosheet.js:13 | Rock Slide notes (spread 0.75×, Wide Guard…); no number | no change |

Per docs/rebuild/research/regmd-readiness-nov17.md line 68 and docs/reg-md-plan.md line 19, data.js (ROSTER, ABIL, MOVES, LEARN)
is "hand-built in October, no generator". Checked in those docs, not by tracing code.

## Rules the code does not model (no code change; say so on the site if it matters)
- **Flash Fire through Mega Evolution:** the calc treats Flash Fire only as a Fire immunity (39-damage-calc-speed.js:141,
  engine/types.js:12, 46-battle-data.js:23, 55-threats.js:310). Its 1.5× boost isn't modelled at all, so nothing changes.
- **Stalwart ignores Substitute:** nothing models Substitute (the only mention is infosheet.js:26, sound moves). The Stalwart
  code (55-threats.js:226, 40-turn-one-data.js:205) is about redirection, which is unchanged.
- **Accuracy in the calc** comes only from MOVE_ACC (39-damage-calc-speed.js:540-541), so regenerating covers it.
- **Rock Slide's flinch** isn't shown as a number anywhere in the calc; King's Rock only checks whether a move has its own flinch
  (39-damage-calc-speed.js:430), which stays true at 20%.

## Tests that must change
- **app/test/run.mjs:144/147:** `hustle: T.moveAcc(M('Rock Slide'), 'Hustle', …)` expects **72** (90 × 0.8). After the patch it
  is 76 (95 × 0.8). The expected number must change with the data, or the check should read Rock Slide's accuracy from MOVE_ACC.
- Should keep passing, checked by reading only: calc-check.mjs:102 (Frost Breath in the always-crit list, unchanged),
  stress-checks.mjs (Rock Slide only as a set member), v2-t1rules.mjs:132 (Stalwart redirection), sheet-tabs.mjs:79 (Flash Fire
  as an immunity note), behaviour A-team/B-build and v2-saved (Rock Slide only in sets).
- **experiments/legality-check.mjs** reads LEARN, so it will see the new pairings once they're added. Its expectations weren't
  read here (not checked).
- Not found: no threat-check, calc-check or golden expectation that depends on these accuracies or the flinch chance
  (searched by move name). Golden files that store whole calc outputs could still shift if a regenerated MOVE_META changes another
  field. Not checked.

## Also on Dec 2 (from CLAUDE.md, not code)
Run legality-check and calc-check, and add a site note that ratings and sets come from games played before the patch.

| 21 | Drampa | Drampa; also Drampa-Mega | no; no |
| 22 | Annihilape | Annihilape | no |
All 22 exist in our data; none learns Psych Up today. The Psych Up row already exists in MOVES.

## The patch's other new moves (official list)
| Move | Official Pokémon | Ours today |
|---|---|---|
| Slack Off | Emboar | Emboar no; **Emboar-Mega no** |
| Roost | Beedrill, Hydreigon | Beedrill no; **Beedrill-Mega no**; Hydreigon no |
| Tailwind | Emolga | no |
| Wonder Room | Umbreon | no |
| Play Rough | Vaporeon, Jolteon, Flareon, Espeon, Umbreon, Leafeon, Glaceon | all no (Sylveon isn't on the list; it learns it already) |

## Against docs/rebuild/test-runs/patch-dec2-map.md
- **Same:** the other new moves and their Pokémon; Screech 85→100, Rock Slide 90→95 and flinch 30→20, Steel Wing 90→100, Metal
  Sound 85→100, Frost Breath 90→100 (all confirmed word for word on the official page).
- **Filled in:** the map said the Psych Up list wasn't in the repo; it's above now.
- **Missed by the map:** Mega forms have their own LEARN entries, and none has these moves either: Alakazam-Mega, Starmie-Mega,
  Mawile-Mega, Altaria-Mega, Absol-Mega, Absol-Mega-Z, Audino-Mega, Drampa-Mega (Psych Up), Emboar-Mega (Slack Off) and
  Beedrill-Mega (Roost). The update must add them to the Mega entries too (my reading: a Mega keeps its base form's moves; true
  in the games, not checked against Champions' own data).
- **Official wording of the two Abilities:**
  - Flash Fire: "the Pokémon now gains the Fire Boost status. The Fire Boost status will persist even if the Pokémon Mega Evolves."
  - Stalwart adds: "When using its moves, the Pokémon ignores the effects of targets' substitutes."
  Both match the map, which noted that our calc models neither Flash Fire's boost nor Substitute.
- "All regulations": the page says the changes apply to battles in every regulation.

===== FILE: experiments/apply-dec2-patch.mjs (alt/patch-dec2)
// Applies the Dec 2, 2026 balance patch to our data, word for word from the official page:
// Pokémon HOME news 877, "Wednesday, December 2, 2026 Battle Balance Adjustments" (copy: docs/rebuild/test-runs/patch-dec2-official-877.html).
// Why a script and not the generators: on Nov 25 Showdown's champions mod didn't have the patch yet (checked: master moves.ts has
// no Rock Slide entry), and the generators would put the old numbers back. Once Showdown has it, re-run the generators and
// experiments/patch-dec2-test.mjs must still pass.
// Safe to run twice: each edit checks the old text first, skips it if the new text is already there, and stops on anything else.
// Run: node experiments/apply-dec2-patch.mjs   then   node experiments/patch-dec2-test.mjs
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const P = f => path.join(root, f);
const NOTE = '// Dec 2, 2026 balance patch (official news 877) applied on top by experiments/apply-dec2-patch.mjs.';

function swap(file, pairs) {
  let s = fs.readFileSync(P(file), 'utf8');
  for (const [from, to] of pairs) {
    if (s.includes(to) && !s.includes(from)) { console.log(`  ${file}: already done: ${to.slice(0, 60)}`); continue; }
    const k = s.split(from).length - 1;
    if (k !== 1) throw new Error(`${file}: expected the old text once, found it ${k} times: ${from}`);
    s = s.replace(from, () => to);
    console.log(`  ${file}: ${from.slice(0, 50)}  ->  ${to.slice(0, 50)}`);
  }
  if (!s.includes(NOTE) && /^\/\/ (Generated|GENERATED)/.test(s)) s = s.replace(/\n/, '\n' + NOTE + '\n');
  fs.writeFileSync(P(file), s);
}

// 1. Accuracy (MOVE_ACC lists only moves under 100, so the four that reach 100 leave it)
swap('app/src/data/move-acc.js', [
  ['"Rock Slide":90', '"Rock Slide":95'],
  ['"Steel Wing":90,', ''], ['"Screech":85,', ''], ['"Metal Sound":85,', ''], ['"Frost Breath":90,', ''],
]);
swap('app/src/data/move-meta.js', [
  ['"Rock Slide":{"t":"allAdjacentFoes","a":90,"s":[[30,0,0,1]],"sf":1}', '"Rock Slide":{"t":"allAdjacentFoes","a":95,"s":[[20,0,0,1]],"sf":1}'],
  ['"Steel Wing":{"t":"normal","f":"c","a":90,', '"Steel Wing":{"t":"normal","f":"c",'],
  ['"Screech":{"t":"normal","f":"s","a":85,', '"Screech":{"t":"normal","f":"s",'],
  ['"Metal Sound":{"t":"normal","f":"s","a":85,', '"Metal Sound":{"t":"normal","f":"s",'],
  ['"Frost Breath":{"t":"normal","a":90,', '"Frost Breath":{"t":"normal",'],
]);

// 2. Words: Rock Slide's flinch, Flash Fire, Stalwart
const FF = s => `${s}'s Fire immunity: when hit by a Fire move it gains Fire Boost (its Fire attacks do 1.5× damage), and Fire Boost stays if it Mega Evolves.`;
const ST = s => `${s}'s moves cannot be redirected to a different target by any effect, and they ignore targets' substitutes.`;
swap('app/src/data/info-text.js', [
  ['"Rock Slide":["30% chance to make the foe(s) flinch.",', '"Rock Slide":["20% chance to make the foe(s) flinch.",'],
  ['"Flash Fire":"This Pokémon\'s Fire attacks do 1.5× damage if hit by one Fire move; Fire immunity."', `"Flash Fire":"${FF('This Pokémon')}"`],
  ['"Stalwart":"This Pokémon\'s moves cannot be redirected to a different target by any effect."', `"Stalwart":"${ST('This Pokémon')}"`],
]);
swap('app/src/data/data.js', [
  ['"Flash Fire":"This Pokemon\'s Fire attacks do 1.5× damage if hit by one Fire move; Fire immunity."', `"Flash Fire":"${FF('This Pokemon')}"`],
  ['"Stalwart":"This Pokemon\'s moves cannot be redirected to a different target by any effect."', `"Stalwart":"${ST('This Pokemon')}"`],
]);
swap('app/src/v2/pages/infosheet.js', [
  ["'Rock Slide': 'Hits both foes. Each one hit has a 30% chance to flinch.'", "'Rock Slide': 'Hits both foes. Each one hit has a 20% chance to flinch.'"],
]);

// 3. New moves in LEARN (lists of MOVES indexes, kept sorted). A Mega form gets the move too: in our data every Mega's
// list equals its base form's (checked Nov 25, all 80), so it stays that way.
const NEW = {
  'Psych Up': ['Alakazam', 'Starmie', 'Mr. Mime', 'Mawile', 'Altaria', 'Castform', 'Absol', 'Roserade', 'Weavile', 'Rotom', 'Rotom-Heat', 'Rotom-Wash', 'Rotom-Frost', 'Rotom-Fan', 'Rotom-Mow', 'Liepard', 'Musharna', 'Audino', 'Cofagrigus', 'Slurpuff', 'Drampa', 'Annihilape'],
  'Slack Off': ['Emboar'], 'Roost': ['Beedrill', 'Hydreigon'], 'Tailwind': ['Emolga'], 'Wonder Room': ['Umbreon'],
  'Play Rough': ['Vaporeon', 'Jolteon', 'Flareon', 'Espeon', 'Umbreon', 'Leafeon', 'Glaceon'],
};
{
  const f = 'app/src/data/data.js', lines = fs.readFileSync(P(f), 'utf8').split('\n');
  const at = name => { const i = lines.findIndex(l => l.startsWith(`const ${name}=`)); if (i < 0) throw new Error(name + ' not found'); return i; };
  const parse = name => JSON.parse(lines[at(name)].slice(name.length + 7).replace(/;\s*$/, ''));
  const MOVES = parse('MOVES'), LEARN = parse('LEARN'), names = parse('ROSTER').map(r => r.n);
  let added = 0;
  for (const [mv, mons] of Object.entries(NEW)) {
    const i = MOVES.findIndex(r => r[0] === mv);
    if (i < 0) throw new Error('move not in MOVES: ' + mv);
    for (const m of mons) {
      const forms = names.filter(x => x === m || x.startsWith(m + '-Mega'));
      if (!forms.length) throw new Error('Pokémon not in ROSTER: ' + m);
      for (const fm of forms) {
        if (!LEARN[fm]) throw new Error('no LEARN entry: ' + fm);
        if (LEARN[fm].includes(i)) continue;
        LEARN[fm] = [...LEARN[fm], i].sort((a, b) => a - b); added++;
        console.log(`  LEARN: ${fm} + ${mv}`);
      }
    }
  }
  lines[at('LEARN')] = 'const LEARN=' + JSON.stringify(LEARN) + ';';
  fs.writeFileSync(P(f), lines.join('\n'));
  console.log(`  LEARN: ${added} added`);
}
console.log('done; now run: node experiments/patch-dec2-test.mjs');

===== FILE: experiments/patch-dec2-test.mjs (alt/patch-dec2)
// Dec 2, 2026 balance patch: checks that every change on the official page is in our data.
// Source: Pokémon HOME news 877, "Wednesday, December 2, 2026 Battle Balance Adjustments" (copy: docs/rebuild/test-runs/patch-dec2-official-877.html).
// Applied by experiments/apply-dec2-patch.mjs. If a generator is re-run from an older Showdown build, this test fails and says what went back.
// Run: node experiments/patch-dec2-test.mjs   (exit 0 = all in, 1 = something missing)
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
const root = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
const read = f => fs.readFileSync(path.join(root, f), 'utf8');
const grab = (src, name) => { const l = src.split('\n').find(x => x.startsWith(`const ${name}=`)); if (!l) throw new Error(`${name} not found`); return JSON.parse(l.slice(name.length + 7).replace(/;\s*$/, '')); };

const data = read('app/src/data/data.js');
const ROSTER = grab(data, 'ROSTER'), ABIL = grab(data, 'ABIL'), MOVES = grab(data, 'MOVES'), LEARN = grab(data, 'LEARN');
const MOVE_ACC = grab(read('app/src/data/move-acc.js'), 'MOVE_ACC');
const MOVE_META = grab(read('app/src/data/move-meta.js'), 'MOVE_META');
const INFO = grab(read('app/src/data/info-text.js'), 'INFO_TEXT');
const sheet = read('app/src/v2/pages/infosheet.js');

let bad = 0, n = 0;
const ok = (c, msg) => { n++; if (!c) { bad++; console.log('FAIL', msg); } };

// 1. New moves, with each Mega form too (our LEARN gives every Mega its base form's list; checked Nov 25: all 80 equal)
const NEW = {
  'Psych Up': ['Alakazam', 'Starmie', 'Mr. Mime', 'Mawile', 'Altaria', 'Castform', 'Absol', 'Roserade', 'Weavile', 'Rotom', 'Rotom-Heat', 'Rotom-Wash', 'Rotom-Frost', 'Rotom-Fan', 'Rotom-Mow', 'Liepard', 'Musharna', 'Audino', 'Cofagrigus', 'Slurpuff', 'Drampa', 'Annihilape'],
  'Slack Off': ['Emboar'], 'Roost': ['Beedrill', 'Hydreigon'], 'Tailwind': ['Emolga'], 'Wonder Room': ['Umbreon'],
  'Play Rough': ['Vaporeon', 'Jolteon', 'Flareon', 'Espeon', 'Umbreon', 'Leafeon', 'Glaceon'],
};
const names = ROSTER.map(r => r.n);
for (const [mv, mons] of Object.entries(NEW)) {
  const i = MOVES.findIndex(r => r[0] === mv);
  ok(i >= 0, `${mv} is a row in MOVES`);
  for (const m of mons) {
    const forms = names.filter(x => x === m || x.startsWith(m + '-Mega'));
    ok(forms.length > 0, `${m} is in ROSTER`);
    for (const f of forms) ok((LEARN[f] || []).includes(i), `${f} learns ${mv}`);
  }
}
ok(Object.values(LEARN).every(a => a.every((x, k) => k === 0 || a[k - 1] < x)), 'every LEARN list stays sorted with no repeats');

// 2. Accuracy and flinch
ok(MOVE_ACC['Rock Slide'] === 95, `Rock Slide accuracy 95 (MOVE_ACC has ${MOVE_ACC['Rock Slide']})`);
for (const mv of ['Screech', 'Steel Wing', 'Metal Sound', 'Frost Breath']) {
  ok(!(mv in MOVE_ACC), `${mv} is 100% so it leaves MOVE_ACC (has ${MOVE_ACC[mv]})`);
  ok(MOVE_META[mv] && !('a' in MOVE_META[mv]), `${mv} has no accuracy in MOVE_META`);
}
ok(MOVE_META['Rock Slide'] && MOVE_META['Rock Slide'].a === 95, 'Rock Slide a:95 in MOVE_META');
ok(JSON.stringify(MOVE_META['Rock Slide'] && MOVE_META['Rock Slide'].s) === '[[20,0,0,1]]', 'Rock Slide flinch 20% in MOVE_META');
ok(/^20% chance/.test(INFO.mv['Rock Slide'][0]), 'Rock Slide info text says 20%: ' + INFO.mv['Rock Slide'][0]);
ok(/'Rock Slide': 'Hits both foes\. Each one hit has a 20% chance to flinch\.'/.test(sheet), 'info sheet Rock Slide line says 20%');
ok(!/Rock Slide[^\n]{0,80}30% chance/.test(sheet + INFO.mv['Rock Slide'][0]), 'no Rock Slide 30% left');

// 3. Abilities (the words rules-fixes-test.mjs check 5 needs from Dec 2 01:59 UTC: Fire Boost, Mega Evolves, substitutes)
for (const [where, t] of [['data.js ABIL', ABIL], ['info-text ab', INFO.ab]]) {
  ok(/Fire Boost/.test(t['Flash Fire']) && /Mega Evolves/.test(t['Flash Fire']) && /Fire immunity/.test(t['Flash Fire']), `${where} Flash Fire: ${t['Flash Fire']}`);
  ok(/substitutes/i.test(t['Stalwart']) && /redirected/.test(t['Stalwart']), `${where} Stalwart: ${t['Stalwart']}`);
}

console.log(`${n - bad}/${n} checks passed`);
process.exit(bad ? 1 : 0);
